Absolute Regression
===================

Chapter 601: The Best Person Is the One Who Buys Meat
-----------------------------------------------------
After meeting the Divine Maiden, Geom Mugeuk returned to the Murim Alliance camp.

All the way back, he thought over what she had said.

What did she mean by saying he had already repaid everything, so there was no need to be grateful?

It sounded as though his connection with her extended all the way to his original world.

Then, suddenly, someone came to mind. A person who, like her, had given off a truly mysterious feeling.

But he could not be certain it was that person on that basis alone.

Fine. I’ll confirm it later.

What he had to focus on now was the prophecy that had been given to him.

Darkness will lead you to Heaven’s Will.

What exactly did darkness mean? And what was the Heaven’s Will spoken of in the prophecy?

Everything was uncertain, but he had one definite premonition.

He had to discover the meaning of the prophecy before the duel between the Palace Lord of Heaven’s Will and the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword took place.

Because he did not think the mysterious chest would leave him here even after that fight ended. Just as it had sucked him in without warning, there was no telling when it might suddenly fling him back out.

‘I don’t have much time.’

By the time he returned to the Murim Alliance tents, dawn was just breaking.

Though it was early, squad leader Jeong Dae was awake.

“Where have you been?”

He asked with a worried face. Of course, it would be strange to him that Geom Mugeuk vanished like a ghost and then reappeared like this, but in any case, Geom Mugeuk was his lifesaver.

“I went out for some air.”

“Jo Ung is watching you. At times like this, you keep your head down.”

“Yes, I’ll be careful.”

“Have you eaten?”

“I’m fine.”

“Here, eat this. It’s gone a little cold, but it should still be decent.”

He handed over some meat he had saved. As the war dragged on, meat had become truly hard to taste. Yet he had specially set some aside for him.

When Geom Mugeuk accepted the meat and smiled brightly, Jeong Dae said in disbelief. It was the first time he had seen him smile so brightly.

“You like it that much?”

“Isn’t the person who buys you meat the best person in the world?”

He had not smiled so brightly because of the meat. He felt good because of Jeong Dae’s kindness.

The thought that even three hundred years ago, good people like this had been living in the murim. If it had all been nothing but the swordplay of the law of the jungle, then perhaps the murim would never have lasted until his own era.

“Squad Leader, what will you do once this war is over?”

“This time, I’m definitely getting married. It’s my old mother’s wish.”

“Do you have a future sister-in-law?”

Jeong Dae nodded.

“Hard to believe with this face, but I do.”

He sighed and added,

“She’s too kind for her own good. She waited for me until she’s reached an age where she can’t wait any longer.”

Yes, this war needed to end even a day sooner for reasons like this. Not because of conspiracies being plotted behind the scenes.

“If I hold the wedding, I’ll invite you too.”

Geom Mugeuk smiled without a word. If he truly could go, he wanted to go no matter what.

‘The Young Cult Master of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult has arrived!’ At that shout, the groom, the bride, and all the guests would faint from shock.

“You’re not coming, are you?”

“I’ll congratulate you even from afar.”

It truly would be from far away, though.

Jeong Dae stared at Geom Mugeuk as he happily tore into the meat, then shook his head.

“You’re definitely a strange bastard.”

* * *

Geom Mugeuk went out on patrol with the squad members.

When he thought of the prophecy, his heart grew urgent.

Is this really the time to be leisurely going on patrol?

The thought came to him unbidden, but Geom Mugeuk silently did the work given to him.

He had experienced it many times in his life before the regression. Spending all day agonizing over something in order to obtain an answer did not mean that answer would appear.

Rather, the thought that he had spent so much time and still failed to find an answer only made him more anxious, and the results became even worse.

At times like this, living one’s daily life more diligently was actually helpful. Sometimes, an answer was not obtained by digging deeply in one place, but was hanging there like a shoe tossed onto a branch glimpsed by chance.

Of course, he did not find that shoe today.

During the patrol, he learned nothing about the prophecy, but there was not no gain at all.

Because by deliberately asking Jeong Dae about this and that, he confirmed where Baegyang Peak was.

After finishing the patrol and returning to eat, cheers rose from the other side.

When he looked over, the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword was inspecting the warriors. He still enjoyed absolute support from the warriors of the Murim Alliance.

When the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword had first arrived here, he had asked the names of each person before him and greeted them. Judging by what he had shown, he was an alliance leader who respected and cherished his subordinates.

Before long, the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword passed by Geom Mugeuk and the squad members lined up before their meal tables.

“You were eating? Go on and eat comfortably.”

He greeted them like that and was passing by when a vigorous voice rang out from behind him.

“If you haven’t eaten, why not join us?”

Of course, the owner of the voice was Geom Mugeuk.

The squad members’ eyes went wide as they stared at Geom Mugeuk.

Their gazes were truly exactly like this. Even if he was their lifesaver, they said this.

Is this bastard insane?

The Martial Sovereign Divine Sword turned his head and looked at Geom Mugeuk. He recognized him.

“You’re that young man from then, aren’t you?”

The young man who had asked him an insolent question on the first day. For some reason, he had been memorable, so he recognized him.

“You remember me. I will consider it an honor to my family.”

Among the several warriors standing behind the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword, Jo Ung openly frowned. He had gone to him personally and even warned him, yet Geom Mugeuk had stepped forward again.

Geom Mugeuk pretended not to see him. If he wanted to inform the alliance leader that Jo Ung had colluded with the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, he could have done so somehow, but he did not.

It was not because the place he had colluded with was the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

It was to avoid interfering with the major flow of events. Of course, there were things that had changed because he had come here, and even this moment would have some effect, but it would not influence the general trend.

“If I join you, you will be uncomfortable, will you not?”

“We will be fine.”

Of course, the squad members were screaming inwardly.

We are not fine!

There was a reason Geom Mugeuk had asked him to eat together. The alliance leader was passing right before his eyes; he could not simply let him pass. It was a reason separate from his effort to find clues to the prophecy.

“Very well.”

Whatever he was thinking, the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword sat down with them.

Before those following him could tell him not to, the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword sent them away to a distance. It was because he thought that otherwise, the warriors would not be able to eat comfortably.

From such behavior, Geom Mugeuk could feel that the alliance leader was a good person.

One could sit down as a performance, to show, “I am this easygoing.” But it was not easy to take care of such small details, like sending one’s subordinates away. Unless it was ingrained in one’s body, it was an easy thing to miss.

It was the same principle as the fact that it was easy for people to fake the big things, but rather difficult to fake the small ones.

“I will eat what you are eating.”

Jeong Dae hurriedly went and brought a meal for the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword. What on earth was this commotion?

“I don’t know if such humble side dishes will suit your noble palate.”

“It is fine.”

The Martial Sovereign Divine Sword had sat down purely because of Geom Mugeuk.

He was a strangely compelling young man. Those clear, deep eyes that did not suit his age were one thing. Asking the alliance leader to eat with him? That unexpectedness was another.

He wondered if perhaps he was a master of martial arts hiding his skill, but the internal energy he sensed amounted to only a few years.

If he were a master capable of deceiving his eyes, then he would have to be stronger than himself, and that could not be.

It was a moment when the fact that Geom Mugeuk had started with only a few years of internal energy proved useful.

And so the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword ate with White Tiger Seventeenth Squad.

As he watched him silently move his chopsticks, a thought suddenly came to Geom Mugeuk.

Perhaps he was feeling tense ahead of his decisive battle with the Palace Lord of Heaven’s Will. Tense enough to feel like eating a meal together with his subordinates like this.

“When I return to my hometown, I’ll boast to my friends that I ate with the Alliance Leader.”

Jinha, I may not have crossed blades with the most famous alliance leader in your Murim Alliance from a full three hundred years ago, but I did cross chopsticks with him.

Of course, it was a boast he could only make in his heart.

Like that, the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword finished his meal. He emptied his bowl cleanly.

After finishing his meal, before he stood up, the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword spoke to Geom Mugeuk seated beside him. He spoke in a low voice so the others could not hear well.

“You asked me before, did you not? Whether there was a way to defeat them.”

“Yes. At the time, Alliance Leader, you said this. That you did have a way, but could not speak of it where there were many people.”

Then the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword brought up something unexpected.

“There is no special method. We have no choice but to fight and win.”

Geom Mugeuk silently nodded.

“Why are you not surprised?”

It was surprising enough that the leader of the Murim Alliance would speak honestly to an ordinary warrior, but what he was saying was that he had lied. He had clearly expected a reaction more surprised than this.

“I thought that would be the case.”

“You thought that would be the case?”

“If there had been a way to win, Alliance Leader, you would have won already. I believe that, for whatever reason, you are not someone who would simply stand by and watch warriors die.”

“!”

“So please, win for our sake as well.”

A strange light passed through the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword’s eyes as he looked at Geom Mugeuk. Where could there be a warrior, and one this young, who could say such things to the leader of the Murim Alliance? And those words had certainly given him strength.

“I have one request.”

“What is it?”

The request was truly something he had never expected.

“When this war ends, our squad leader here is going back to his hometown to hold his wedding.”

What? Why are you bringing that up? When he was mentioned, Jeong Dae was greatly shocked inside. Why else would he have brought it up?

“Please deliver the congratulatory address at that wedding, Alliance Leader.”

A strangled cry burst from the utterly shocked Jeong Dae’s mouth.

“No, Alliance Leader. No!”

He even waved his hands in refusal, but Geom Mugeuk pretended not to notice and added,

“There were thirteen squad members, and now there are five. He has endured all that grief, steadfastly taking care of his squad members while holding the battlefield. I believe he is more than qualified to receive your congratulations, Alliance Leader.”

Jeong Dae belatedly rushed over and covered Geom Mugeuk’s mouth.

“This fellow must have gone mad in the middle of the war. I apologize, Alliance Leader.”

At that moment, the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword silently nodded and granted permission.

“Very well. I will deliver the congratulatory address.”

At his agreement, everyone was shocked. Jeong Dae in particular was on the verge of fainting.

Geom Mugeuk could tell from his eyes that he had spoken sincerely.

“Then finish your meal.”

The Martial Sovereign Divine Sword left the place.

Jeong Dae stood for a moment with a dazed face, recalling that instant. The leader of the Murim Alliance would deliver the congratulatory address at his wedding? Just imagining it made him happy. His old mother would boast about it to everyone she met.

“I did well, didn’t I?”

When Geom Mugeuk asked casually, Jeong Dae shouted, though he was pleased for no reason.

“You really are a mad bastard!”

Three hundred years in the past, he was being called a mad bastard by Jeong Dae. That also meant he was doing well in a way true to himself.

Geom Mugeuk smiled and said to him,

“I should repay someone who saved meat for me at least this much.”

* * *

Geom Mugeuk had taken care of Jeong Dae’s wedding, but he still had not found a clue to the prophecy.

That night.

Geom Mugeuk climbed Baegyang Peak.

Since it was the site of the duel, someone might be guarding it. For that reason, Geom Mugeuk was careful, and then careful again, as he climbed.

Separate from the fact that he could not use the Nine Hells Demonic Art because he lacked internal energy, Geom Mugeuk’s senses remained as they were.

Using those senses, he moved cautiously. Fortunately, he met no one along the way.

He encountered no one all the way to the summit of Baegyang Peak. Well, they could hardly send people carelessly to a place designated as the site of the duel. If the other side found out, they would be mired in rumors of having used underhanded methods before even fighting.

Once he reached the top, he understood why Baegyang Peak had been chosen.

The summit had enough open space for two absolute masters to display a splendid battle. There were trees and rocks, and it had elegance as well.

Geom Mugeuk looked around under the bright moonlight.

Could this place be connected to the prophecy?

But he could find nothing strange. The moon was especially brilliant today, making it even farther removed from darkness.

‘Mysterious chest, where am I supposed to find the clue to the prophecy?’

Just then, he sensed a presence far behind him.

In that instant, Geom Mugeuk vanished. He disappeared into the Spacetime Transposition Art in the blink of an eye.

Because he had reacted so quickly, the other party did not realize that Geom Mugeuk had been standing there.

From within the darkness of the Spacetime Transposition Art, where only a single person could stand, Geom Mugeuk watched the person walking toward that place.

A moment later, a man arrived.

He was a young man wearing a black long robe embroidered with ornate patterns, someone Geom Mugeuk was seeing for the first time since arriving here.

Despite his youth, the aura he revealed was anything but ordinary. It was not the aura of the orthodox faction, but it was also different from Wi Mucheon’s, and different from the Palace Lord of Guardians’ as well.

‘Who is he?’

The man’s eyes, filled with moonlight, shone intensely. They were eyes in which a burning desire could be felt.

How long had he stood there alone like that?

Another person appeared at that place.

Amazingly, the second person to arrive was someone Geom Mugeuk had seen before. It was none other than the masked man of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult who had secretly met with Jo Ung, now revealing himself here.

The man’s identity was revealed from the masked man’s mouth.

“Lord of the Dark Palace.”

The moment he heard those words, Geom Mugeuk knew who the man was. He was the palace lord of the Dark Palace, the last of the Three Divine Palaces of Heaven’s Will Palace.

He did not know why this man was meeting a demonic practitioner of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult here, but he knew one thing.

The prophecy the Divine Maiden had told him rose in Geom Mugeuk’s mind.

Darkness will lead you to Heaven’s Will.

He knew instinctively. That darkness referred precisely to the last palace lord of Heaven’s Will Palace, the Lord of the Dark Palace.

‘This man is the one who will guide me to Heaven’s Will.’

Chapter 602: Make It Wrong This Time
------------------------------------
As if it were the cry of fate, a low rumble of thunder came from far away.

Rrrrrumble.

Dark clouds were gathering in the distance. The air riding in on the wind already felt damp.

Like the weather changing in an instant, Geom Mugeuk could feel that this encounter would change his fate.

Within the Time-Space Displacement Art, Geom Mugeuk stared at the Dark Palace Lord.

He was meeting someone he should not have met, in a place where that person should not have been.

‘Just why are you in contact with the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult here?’

As it happened, he was standing in a position where he could see the other man’s face in detail.

From the Dark Palace Lord’s intense gaze, he felt two contradictory sensations at once: coldness and heat.

The first impression was heat. In his eyes burned the fierce flame of a man who would surely achieve whatever he had set out to do.

And at the center of that flame, Geom Mugeuk could see it.

A cold-blooded ruthlessness that would sacrifice anything for the sake of its goal.

Because Geom Mugeuk had seen countless villains until now, he knew. This was a man who created answers from death. A man who solved problems by killing.

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned this time toward the masked man. Last time, he had seen him only from a distance, but today he could see him up close.

‘His skills are no ordinary thing.’

By feel, he was not a Demon Venerable, but he was at least a demonic practitioner of division-leader level or higher. He was probably someone adept at handling matters like this.

The Heavenly Demon Divine Cult had met with Jo Ung of the Murim Alliance, and they had also met with the Dark Palace Lord of Heavenly Will Palace. In other words, they had made connections with both sides.

“It looks as though rain will fall soon.”

After looking around at the surroundings darkening beneath the clouds, the masked man added,

“Isn’t darkness on your side? It seems the heavens are helping you.”

The Dark Palace Lord turned toward the masked man standing behind him.

At that moment, Geom Mugeuk saw it. His gaze changed in an instant into a lukewarm look that was neither cold nor hot.

‘He is skilled at hiding himself.’

With an indifferent expression, the Dark Palace Lord gave an unexpected reply.

“That notion that darkness is evil, that darkness is bad, is a prejudice. Surprisingly, most bad things happen in broad daylight. Darkness merely gives humans rest and repose.”

At those half-joking words, the masked man’s eyes smiled.

“I know very well what sort of darkness yours is. Hearing someone like you speak of repose is amusing.”

The masked man spoke as though he knew exactly what kind of person the Dark Palace Lord was. He did not read the other’s mood, but neither was he rude.

Their gazes tangled in midair.

Between them, Geom Mugeuk was watching the two.

‘They don’t trust each other.’

In other words, they had joined hands for the sake of something they needed.

Just what sort of deal was the Dark Palace Lord trying to make with this dangerous opponent?

The masked man asked the Dark Palace Lord,

“Who has it now?”

To the masked man’s question, the Dark Palace Lord answered,

“The Palace Lord has it.”

Geom Mugeuk could tell. The thing the masked man was asking about was none other than the secret chest.

‘So the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult also knows that the secret chest has awakened.’

If so, the reason they were intervening this time would also be because of the secret chest.

“Has he obtained the power?”

“Not yet.”

“Why are you so certain? He may have already obtained it.”

“No.”

The Dark Palace Lord was certain that was not the case.

“If he had, the Martial Emperor Divine Sword would already be a dead man.”

He knew the personality of the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will better than anyone. If he had obtained power, he was not the sort of man who would leave the Martial Emperor Divine Sword be.

“Here, take it.”

The masked man handed him the leather pack he had been carrying on his back.

The Dark Palace Lord checked the contents of the leather pack.

“By tomorrow, everything will be over. Since we will have no reason to meet again, allow me to congratulate you in advance on ascending to the seat of the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will.”

After saying that, the masked man was about to turn away when the Dark Palace Lord spoke to him in warning.

“You had best keep your promise. If you do not want darkness to become a nightmare for the rest of your life.”

In the masked man’s gaze flashed a sneer that seemed to say, ‘You dare threaten us?’ but the words that came from his mouth were gentle.

“Even now, I do not sleep well at night. So do not worry. Our cult will certainly keep its promise.”

Geom Mugeuk confirmed one fact in this place.

‘The Dark Palace Lord has betrayed the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will.’

At first, he had thought that perhaps he was carrying out this matter under the orders of the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will. After all, they might have been trying to draw in the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult and kill the Alliance Leader.

But that was not it. This was the Dark Palace Lord’s rebellion.

Just what was inside that leather pack?

Rrrrrumble.

As the thunder sounded even closer, the Dark Palace Lord slung the leather pack over his shoulder and slowly began descending the mountain.

Once he disappeared, Geom Mugeuk emerged from the Time-Space Displacement Art.

The Dark Palace Lord was undoubtedly going to meet the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will. To learn what he was scheming, Geom Mugeuk had to reach the camp faster than him.

‘I have to hear what they talk about.’

Of course, if the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will was in the tent, he would not be able to enter, but that was a problem to worry about when the time came.

Geom Mugeuk looked down below the cliff. With his current internal energy, jumping down felt precarious, but—

“Wind God, I’m counting on you!”

Geom Mugeuk hurled himself off the cliff.

* * *

Swaaaaaaaaa!

Amid the pouring rain, the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will sat cross-legged on a rock.

The falling rain did not strike his body, but bounced away. It felt as though an invisible membrane surrounded him.

He was in a secluded place a little distance from the camp, settling his mind ahead of tomorrow’s decisive battle.

The power of the secret chest had still not been transmitted to him.

If he fought like this? Would he truly be able to defeat the Martial Emperor Divine Sword?

Not even vaguely could he say, “Of course.” His opponent was a man whose martial skill bore the character for emperor, and whose sword bore the character for god.

‘Heavens, are you toying with me?’

He even felt resentment.

Just then, the voice of the Guardian Palace Lord was heard.

“The Dark Palace Lord has arrived.”

At that, the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will’s eyes snapped open. The emotion that flashed through them was unmistakably anticipation—‘At last!’

When the two returned to the tent, the Dark Palace Lord was waiting while looking at the map of the Central Plains hanging on the wall.

As the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will entered, the Dark Palace Lord greeted him with the utmost courtesy.

“I pay my respects to the venerable Palace Lord.”

“Welcome, Palace Lord Hua.”

The Dark Palace Lord, Hua Shaoqiang.

He also exchanged greetings with the Guardian Palace Lord. Outwardly, they greeted each other with proper courtesy, but the gazes they exchanged were far from pleasant.

In particular, the Guardian Palace Lord always suspected and guarded against the Dark Palace Lord. He believed that the Dark Palace Lord harbored ambitions to seize Heavenly Will Palace.

Of course, he had never told the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will of such suspicions.

There was no proof, and above all, the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will trusted the Dark Palace Lord. He knew that the Palace Lord liked him more than he liked himself.

Then again, unlike himself, who always spoke against his will, the Dark Palace Lord usually said what the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will wanted to hear.

His first words today were no different.

“Tomorrow, at last, the master of the orthodox murim will change.”

At those words, as if guaranteeing the victory of the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will, a thought suddenly occurred to the Guardian Palace Lord.

‘So the Palace Lord must have wanted to hear those words.’

He had been by the Palace Lord’s side the entire time, yet he had not said such a thing even once.

Perhaps it was because although he now believed that Heaven’s Will had descended upon the Palace Lord, he still had lingering discomfort about this war itself.

The Palace Lord of Heavenly Will responded humbly.

“Tomorrow’s battle cannot be guaranteed. The Martial Emperor Divine Sword is not an opponent to be taken lightly.”

“Heaven’s Will has reached you, Palace Lord. It is not something a mere human can stop.”

At that, the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will mentioned the Divine Maiden. His intention was to hear what Hua Shaoqiang thought of the prophecy.

“The Divine Maiden came.”

The Dark Palace Lord asked carefully,

“Was a prophecy given?”

When the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will slowly nodded, the Dark Palace Lord respectfully cupped his fist and said,

“I dare ask about the sacred prophecy.”

After a brief pause, the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will answered.

“It was a prophecy that I would lose this war. She said our Heaven’s Will would only be fulfilled three hundred years from now.”

Even at those astonishing words, the Dark Palace Lord’s expression did not change at all.

“You are not surprised.”

The Dark Palace Lord said calmly,

“Three hundred years from now… It is too distant a matter to feel real.”

He had not directly commented on the prophecy, but his words already contained more than enough of his feelings.

“It is not as though you can wait three hundred years, is it?”

At the Dark Palace Lord’s words, a faint smile formed on the lips of the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will.

The Guardian Palace Lord could tell. That, too, was what the Palace Lord had been hoping to hear. He had been wishing for someone to support him like that. Because the Dark Palace Lord was someone who said such things, the Palace Lord had been so pleased earlier when he heard that the Dark Palace Lord had arrived.

“A prophecy is, in the end, no more than a prophecy.”

“It has never once been wrong.”

“Then make it wrong this time.”

The Palace Lord of Heavenly Will laughed loudly.

What the Dark Palace Lord had prepared was not only words pleasing to the ear.

“To that end, this should be of help.”

The Dark Palace Lord took something out of the leather pack he had brought.

“Please wear this, Palace Lord.”

What he presented was a suit of protective armor.

“It is the Ice Dragon Divine Armor.”

Both the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will and the Guardian Palace Lord were surprised that it was the Ice Dragon Divine Armor. This Ice Dragon Divine Armor was truly difficult to obtain.

“How did you acquire this?”

“I made inquiries in every direction and barely managed to obtain it.”

Moreover, there was a reason why it had to be the Ice Dragon Divine Armor.

“The Martial Emperor Divine Sword’s martial arts are based on extreme yang internal energy. This Ice Dragon Divine Armor contains extreme yin energy, making it the most suitable protective armor for facing the Martial Emperor Divine Sword.”

The Palace Lord of Heavenly Will was greatly delighted.

“Thank you.”

The Dark Palace Lord had prepared more.

He took a small box from the leather pack.

“Please take this as well.”

When the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will opened it, there was a single pill inside.

Seeing it, the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will and the Guardian Palace Lord were startled. It was a spiritual medicine they both knew.

“It is the Heavenly Yin Pill.”

The Heavenly Yin Pill was a spiritual medicine with effects superior to Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng, and it was not easy to obtain.

“This, too, is a spiritual medicine based on extreme yin, so it will be of help in facing the Martial Emperor Divine Sword.”

“Thank you. Truly, thank you.”

“It will take time for the extreme yin energy to spread throughout your body. Therefore, please take it now. We will stand guard for you.”

Once the energy of a spiritual medicine was dissolved and refined, it became internal energy, but it took time for its unique properties to take effect within the body. Especially in the case of this spiritual medicine, the extreme yin property was more important than the internal energy itself.

“Thank you. I will take it now.”

At that moment, the Guardian Palace Lord sent a sound transmission to the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will.

—It would be best not to take the pill.

He knew very well how this would sound to the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will. The Dark Palace Lord had even obtained protective armor and a spiritual medicine for tomorrow’s battle, while he was suspecting the other man’s goodwill.

But ahead of this important duel, he thought the spiritual medicine given by the Dark Palace Lord must not be taken.

—Do you think the Dark Palace Lord would betray me?

Did he truly not know that there was such a possibility? Or was he himself blinded by jealousy?

—The Palace Lord Hua I know is a man of great ambition. I worry he may be plotting something on this occasion.

The Dark Palace Lord realized that the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will and the Guardian Palace Lord were exchanging sound transmissions. But he did not step forward and simply waited, pretending not to know. The situation was on his side.

An astonishing statement flowed from the mouth of the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will.

—That is right. More than anyone, Palace Lord Hua is a man of great ambition.

He knew as well. In fact, he knew it even better than the Guardian Palace Lord.

—His lifelong dream is to sit in my seat.

—!

The Guardian Palace Lord asked in shock,

—Then why do you intend to take the pill?

There was another reason for that.

—Precisely because of that, he would not do something like poison the pill.

—What do you mean?

—The moment he poisons me to death, the seat of Palace Lord will vanish for him.

It meant that the members of the palace would never accept someone who had poisoned the previous Palace Lord.

The Guardian Palace Lord looked at the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will in astonishment. He had thought the Palace Lord was simply melting beneath a treacherous subject’s honeyed tongue, but that was not so. He saw through everything.

“The two of you stand guard.”

The Palace Lord of Heavenly Will took the Heavenly Yin Pill on the spot.

Then he sat down, circulated his energy, and began dissolving and absorbing the pill’s medicinal power.

He circulated his true qi once, then again.

While the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will dissolved the medicine’s effects, the Guardian Palace Lord waited inwardly tense.

By contrast, the Dark Palace Lord acted as though it were nothing at all, arms folded, merely staring at the map on the wall.

The Guardian Palace Lord found it strange. If the Palace Lord’s words were correct—

‘Just what are you scheming?’

He must be hoping that the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will would die in tomorrow’s duel. Then why had he obtained and given him protective armor and a spiritual medicine?

A short while later, after dissolving all the energy of the spiritual medicine, the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will opened his eyes. His gaze had deepened even further. Just as he had said, there had been no poison in the Heavenly Yin Pill.

“It has greatly helped increase my internal energy. Moreover, by tomorrow, the extreme yin energy should protect me from the Martial Emperor Divine Sword’s extreme yang true qi. Thank you.”

“For the sake of your victory, Palace Lord, I would gladly offer even this life.”

Since there was nothing wrong with the spiritual medicine, the Guardian Palace Lord had nothing to say. Rather, he felt awkward, but the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will did not rebuke him.

“Please put on the Ice Dragon Divine Armor as well.”

The Palace Lord of Heavenly Will also put on the Ice Dragon Divine Armor in front of them. This, too, was so precious that it was only proper to wear it before the person who had gifted it.

After putting on even the Ice Dragon Divine Armor, the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will said to the Guardian Palace Lord,

“Leave us for a moment.”

Supporting the Palace Lord’s will, bringing protective armor, and even a spiritual medicine—if he was told to leave ten times over, he was in no position to refuse.

The Guardian Palace Lord bowed respectfully and left.

Swaaaaaaaaa.

He slowly stepped into the rain.

Since the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will had said he knew of the man’s ambition as well, he was not worried, but for some reason he felt troubled. Everyone was living while using one another, and he alone felt left behind. He wondered if he was merely being stubbornly loyal.

The reason the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will sent the Guardian Palace Lord out was not because he favored the Dark Palace Lord. It was because there was an order he had secretly given to the Dark Palace Lord.

“Did you bring the secret chest?”

“Yes, I brought it.”

He had brought the large secret chest from Heavenly Will Palace’s treasury to this place.

The reason the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will had started this war, and the reason he intended to continue fighting while ignoring the prophecy, was all because of the secret chest. Because he believed the secret chest had chosen him and would grant him power.

But as time passed and he still failed to obtain power from the small secret chest, he grew more anxious by the day.

In the end, the conclusion he reached was this.

Perhaps the large secret chest was needed in order to obtain the power of the small secret chest.

He had secretly ordered it brought here in order to make even that final effort.

Because now, the only thing he could believe in was the secret chest.

“Where is it now?”

Chapter 603: Now That I've Reached You
--------------------------------------
Geom Mugeuk was there.

When he leapt down from the cliff and arrived at the encampment first, fortunately, the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace was not in his tent.

Thanks to that, he succeeded in slipping in unnoticed, and was able to watch and hear the entire conversation among the three of them.

The matters concerning the prophecy were things Geom Mugeuk already knew, so there was nothing remarkable there.

What startled him was the gift the Dark Palace Lord had brought.

When he took the Ice Dragon Divine Armor and the Heavenly Yin Pill from his leather pouch, Geom Mugeuk was truly shocked.

‘Those two treasures were received from the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, weren’t they?’

There was no way the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, which had joined hands with the Dark Palace Lord, would have offered protective armor and an elixir for the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace.

‘Did they mix poison into the elixir?’

At first, that was what he thought. That they must have tampered with the Heavenly Yin Pill somehow.

But after taking it, the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace confirmed that there was nothing wrong.

‘The Heavenly Demon Divine Cult wants the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace to win?’

No matter how he thought about it, that did not seem likely.

After that, the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace put on the Ice Dragon Divine Armor. Watching him, Geom Mugeuk grew suspicious once more. From the second point onward, these were suspicions the Guardian Palace Lord had been unable to harbor.

‘Did they do something to the Ice Dragon Divine Armor?’

What if the Heavenly Yin Pill was bait?

What if they had aimed for the moment he let his guard down after confirming there was nothing wrong with the elixir?

Indeed, the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace put on the Ice Dragon Divine Armor without the slightest suspicion.

What if something had been applied inside that divine armor, something even the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace could not detect?

But the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace did not seem to feel anything amiss.

Soon, Geom Mugeuk shook his head.

‘No. That could be discovered.’

The Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace might examine the inside of the protective armor, or he might say he would wear it later and not put it on at all. At the very least, it was not a perfect plan, and the Dark Palace Lord, filled with that ice-cold ambition, did not seem the type to handle matters in such a way.

‘Then what on earth is his intention?’

As he continued thinking on the premise that there had to be a trap there.

‘No way?’

Extreme yin and extreme yin. With that Heavenly Yin Pill and the Ice Dragon Divine Armor combined, the body of the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace had entered an indescribably extreme yin state.

At a time when he was only preparing against the extreme yang attack the Martial Emperor Divine Sword would unleash.

What if, in that state, he were struck by a surprise attack carrying extreme yin internal energy?

At that moment, the Heavenly Yin Pill and the Ice Dragon Divine Armor would act as a fatal poison.

In any case, the conclusion was set.

In tomorrow’s duel, the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace would die.

Because the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult had interfered in this way, tomorrow’s battle had not been recorded in detail in the history of the Martial Alliance. Only the record that the Martial Emperor Divine Sword killed the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace remained.

And after sending the Guardian Palace Lord away, the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace and the Dark Palace Lord exchanged a startling conversation.

“Did you bring the secret coffer?”

“Yes, I brought it.”

He brought the great secret coffer here? The moment Geom Mugeuk heard those words, the prophecy came to mind.

Darkness will lead you to the Heavenly Will.

Geom Mugeuk sensed instinctively that the prophecy was related to the great secret coffer. The Dark Palace Lord was leading him to the place where the great secret coffer was.

“Where is it now?”

To the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace’s question, the Dark Palace Lord gave an inscrutable answer.

“The secret coffer is beneath the lamp.”

* * *

Shwaaaaaaaa.

The fierce rain made it hard to see ahead. Rainwater pouring down like blades shattered against the ground into countless fragments.

Flash! Rumble!

Whenever lightning struck, the surroundings brightened, and the figures of the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace and the Dark Palace Lord, wearing bamboo hats and straw raincoats as they climbed the mountain, appeared for an instant before vanishing.

Each time lightning flashed, they were in a different place.

After walking through the rain like that, the place the two arrived at was, surprisingly, the mountainside where Baegyang Peak—the site of tomorrow’s duel—stood.

As they forced their way along a path where no people passed, the Dark Palace Lord said,

“From here begins this palace’s maze formation. From now on, please step exactly where I step.”

The Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace walked, stepping precisely where the Dark Palace Lord stepped.

After passing through the formation and walking deeper through the undergrowth, they came upon a somewhat spacious clearing where a temporary wooden building had been erected.

“We had to move it in haste, so we could not build it properly.”

For that, it was a fairly well-made structure.

Before entering, the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace asked,

“Why here, of all places?”

It was a question asking whether it could not have been stored somewhere in the rear.

There was a reason the Dark Palace Lord had done this.

“It was for your sake, Palace Lord.”

“For my sake?”

“I thought that if the secret coffer was kept not far from the site of the duel, it might exert some sacred power.”

Even at this moment, the Dark Palace Lord was saying words pleasant to hear.

The Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace knew the ambition of this Dark Palace Lord well, but he had to acknowledge at least one thing.

The only person capable of conceiving the idea of hiding the secret coffer in such an unexpected place, avoiding the eyes of their enemies, and then putting that idea into action was the Dark Palace Lord.

“Please go in. I will wait here.”

The Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace nodded once, then entered the building.

Inside was the secret coffer.

The very secret coffer that had been the beginning of all this.

The Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace stood before the secret coffer.

How urgent must his heart have been for him to have had the sacred treasure of the Heavenly Will Palace brought all the way here?

When the great secret coffer had first produced the small secret coffer, he had never imagined a moment like this would come. He had rejoiced, believing that Heaven’s will had reached him.

As he gazed at the secret coffer, remorse for days past flickered through his eyes. His Heavenly Will had begun when he was very young.

“Do you know this? Since I was a child, I grew up looking at you.”

He spoke as if the secret coffer were a person.

“Since I was young, I was told that protecting you was the mission of this palace. Because you were a sacred treasure containing Heaven’s will, I did not dare even look at you directly. I have lived with the mission of protecting you.”

At this moment, he did not honestly confess one fact. That the reason he had believed in and protected the secret coffer so deeply was neither to uphold the will of his family, nor because of the secret coffer’s sanctity.

Unification of all under heaven.

It was to fulfill that true dream. He had believed that if only the secret coffer awakened, he would be able to achieve that dream.

“Even as I grew older, I strove not to let that feeling change. You are not a dust-covered statue of Buddha. I believed you were not merely an ancient symbol handed down in this palace.”

The voice of the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace, who had been staring at the secret coffer, suddenly grew louder.

“And then you awakened. You, whom I had watched over my entire life, whom I had believed in my entire life.”

The Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace took the small secret coffer from his bosom and held it high.

“So grant me strength! Convey Heaven’s will to me!”

But neither the great secret coffer nor the small secret coffer showed any change.

The expression of the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace hardened.

He had held a thread of faith that if he conveyed his sincerity, the secret coffer would convey its sincerity in return.

This time, he brought the small secret coffer to the great secret coffer. He grew tense in case it might absorb the small secret coffer again, but once more, nothing happened.

He did not give up.

He circled around the secret coffer, and even tried injecting internal energy into it.

In the past, touching this sacred secret coffer would have been unthinkable.

“If you are not going to grant me power, then why did you awaken at all!”

The Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace’s cry rang out thunderously.

But the thing he had hoped for never occurred. Suddenly, the Divine Maiden’s prophecy came to mind.

—Heaven’s will does not lie with you, Palace Lord.

The Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace did not abandon hope until the very end.

“I believe in you.”

When the fierce battle erupted, when life and death hung in the balance, power would be transmitted from the secret coffer.

After all, the birth of a great hero who would unify all under heaven could not come so easily.

After leaving those final words, the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace went outside.

Outside, the Dark Palace Lord was waiting for him in the rain. Because he had heard the words from within, he knew that the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace had failed to obtain power.

“Let us go.”

The Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace walked out into the rain.

The Dark Palace Lord followed behind him. A look of regret appeared on his face, but his true thoughts were different.

‘The prophecy has abandoned you, so how could the secret coffer respond?’

It was clear that the Dark Palace Lord believed in the Divine Maiden’s prophecy.

He interpreted the awakening of this secret coffer in this way.

‘The secret coffer did not awaken as Heaven’s will for you. In the end, it was to seat me as the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace.’

Seeing that the secret coffer did not grant him power was enough for him to believe his thoughts were correct.

Thus, the two men, sharing the same bed but dreaming different dreams, disappeared into the rain together.

After they left, before long, one person revealed himself.

Of course, it was Geom Mugeuk. Using the Divine Eye Art, he had tailed the two of them from afar. Thanks to the pouring rain, he had been able to succeed in following them.

Truly, this rain seemed like a gift Heaven had sent for him.

Geom Mugeuk had followed them from a distance, using the Divine Eye Art to confirm where the two stepped as they went in.

And so, after the two left, he entered this place. Geom Mugeuk stepped into the building where the secret coffer was.

The secret coffer that would change everyone’s fate was there.

The three people who had come here today were each sensing their own Heaven’s will.

Geom Mugeuk was no different.

“You, three hundred years from now, brought me here.”

Geom Mugeuk slowly approached the secret coffer and spoke to it in a friendly tone. Since he had spent so much time treating the secret coffer in his bosom like a friend, this great secret coffer was also easy for him to face.

“There must be a reason your future self brought me to your present self, right?”

Geom Mugeuk placed his hand on the secret coffer. It looked exactly the same as the secret coffer beneath the Yellow Dragon Martial Hall that he had seen earlier with the Sword King, but the aura he sensed from it was somehow different. This one was more vigorous, more brimming with vitality.

“You’ve changed a lot in the meantime. Well, if you suffered for three hundred years, I suppose it’s only natural that you’d change.”

Geom Mugeuk decided that when he returned, he would comfort the secret coffer of the future and tell it it had suffered much. Did that feeling reach it? The secret coffer trembled faintly.

‘The secret coffer is sensing me.’

If so, what he had to say to the secret coffer at this moment was surely this.

Because he believed that the reason it had called him to this world was not only to show him the past, but to convey these very words.

With his hand resting on the secret coffer, Geom Mugeuk spoke calmly.

“Darkness will lead you to the Heavenly Will.”

Now that I have reached you, show me my Heaven’s will.

The moment Geom Mugeuk uttered the prophecy, the small secret coffer in his bosom trembled.

The next instant!

Papapapak.

A strange, thunderous noise came from the great secret coffer. It was the very response the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace had longed for so desperately.

Startled, Geom Mugeuk sprang backward.

The next moment, an astonishing sight unfolded.

Chiiiiing.

With a sound he had also never heard in his life, something began to be engraved on the wall of the secret coffer.

They were circles. A black circle, a white circle, a blue circle, a red circle, a yellow circle, and finally, a purple circle.

Upon seeing the circles engraved on the secret coffer, the energies he had already absorbed all awakened at once and began to writhe.

The next moment!

Light seemed to burst forth from the six circles all at once, and that light formed words in the air.

—This mind art is completed only when the six energies are moved as a single energy. In moving true qi, be as free as the wind, yet as heavy as the earth. You must be able to surge hotly like fire, yet stop like ice. You must be as deep as darkness and as powerful as lightning. If you can unite these six energies into one, you will be able to possess the greatest power of all ages, past and present.

The moment he read those words, Geom Mugeuk’s heart surged.

This incantation was undoubtedly the mind art formula that would allow him to use the energies he had obtained through the secret coffer.

At that very moment!

Riiiiip.

The empty air directly before Geom Mugeuk’s eyes tore open. The space where there had truly been nothing split apart as if pulled from both sides, and darkness unfolded within the gap.

From within that darkness, white smoke rose and began forming words.

‘It’s a mind art formula!’

But he could not understand what it meant. There were unfamiliar words mixed in, seemingly terms used in the martial world of ancient times.

That was the beginning.

Rumble!

The ground beneath his feet overturned, and a crust of earth surged up from below. The formula was written in soil upon that earthen crust. Some of these words, too, he could recognize, while others he could not.

At the same time, something pressed down from the ceiling.

Kwa-kwa-kwa-kwang.

What descended from the air were enormous icicles. As though carved with an awl, formulas glowed white upon those icicles as well.

Fwoooooosh.

Flames soared up from all directions.

Within the flames that spewed scorching heat, formulas wavered.

Truly, the spectacle that continued to unfold was enough to inspire awe merely by witnessing it.

Whoooooosh.

Wind whirled above his head. Astonishingly, letters were drifting within the wind as well.

Craaaack!

Lightning struck down. Even in the scorched place where the lightning had fallen, traces of the formula remained.

In a form he had never seen in his life, the formulas revealed themselves. Geom Mugeuk could feel just how extraordinary this mind art was.

But no matter how intelligent Geom Mugeuk was, he could not memorize all of it when it was filled with words he did not even know.

A brief silence flowed.

The small secret coffer at Geom Mugeuk’s chest trembled.

Wooooong.

The next moment, the characters of the formulas all around began shining in their respective colors.

Shwaaaaaaaaaang!

Six streams of light, each containing its own formula, began pouring into Geom Mugeuk’s body.

Chapter 604: If It Doesn’t Come Out Today Either, With It Still Inside
----------------------------------------------------------------------
Six colors of light poured down upon Geom Mugeuk.

Saaaaaa.

The light seeped into Geom Mugeuk’s body like rain soaking the earth.

Once inside him, the light flowed through his body like a river, as though it were internal force. Each time his heart beat, the lights spread swiftly throughout his entire body.

The light was hot, and it was cold; it was electrifying. It was refreshing, cozy, and it also brought fear.

Geom Mugeuk did not resist this light.

It was because he knew instinctively. He knew that this light was for him. No matter how hot it was, no matter how cold it was, no matter how deep a terror it stirred within him.

After circulating through every corner of his body, the six lights gathered in Geom Mugeuk’s head.

The lights gathered in his brain all burst forth with radiance at once.

Swaaaaaak!

The next moment!

The Black Essence, the Azure Essence, and the White Essence that had been sleeping within Geom Mugeuk’s body began moving along his meridians on their own.

Astonishingly, Geom Mugeuk knew the incantation formula for the internal cultivation method.

The clusters of light that had entered him moments ago had directly taught Geom Mugeuk the formula. No, rather than teaching it to him, they had made him fully comprehend it.

He had heard of transferring internal energy to another person, but this was the first time he had ever heard of imparting knowledge in an instant like this.

The internal cultivation method taught by the secret coffer was completely different from existing methods of circulating qi.

The three energies moved along his meridians in an order he never would have imagined. The landscape of the meridians through which they raced was different from what he knew. It was an exceedingly precarious and dangerous landscape.

True qi even flowed in reverse, and at times it forced its way along paths that seemed likely to cause internal injuries.

This internal cultivation method shattered, in a single stroke, all common sense of martial learning he had known until now.

But Geom Mugeuk trusted this formula. He did not know what the energy within his body was, nor what this internal cultivation method was, but he decided to simply trust anything related to the secret coffer.

Just like the inexplicable sense of relief he had felt when he first absorbed energy from the secret coffer, he trusted this instinct that told him it was an energy favorable to him.

When he had thus circulated the true qi through one full cycle, at the final moment, the three energies merged into one.

The very instant he felt an enormous power.

The three energies scattered weakly once more and quietly fell asleep throughout his meridians.

Geom Mugeuk could tell. As the words left behind by the secret coffer had said, this internal cultivation method would only be completed when all six energies were gathered.

Everything else remained unknown.

What would happen when all six energies had gathered and he operated this internal cultivation method? Would the gathered energies be operated separately, or would they merge with his existing internal energy?

Was there a martial art suited to this internal cultivation method, or would the method itself display some kind of power?

How, in the end, would it grant him the greatest power of all time?

It was at that moment that his surroundings began to change.

Sarururureung.

As though bidding farewell, the secret coffer made a cheerful sound he had never heard before.

Chiiiiii.

The six circles engraved on the secret coffer disappeared. They had been patterns carved deep as though etched by a blade, but they began to fade slowly until they vanished completely.

Starting with that, everything returned to normal.

Jjiiiiik.

The space torn open before him began to close. Within that space was a deep darkness.

Geom Mugeuk did not dislike that darkness. Because he knew better than anyone that a night spent awake alone was not lonelier than daytime.

Rumble!

The overturned surface of the earth began to descend back down. As it slowly sank, it seemed to be greeting him, saying, Rest assured, I am beneath the ground you stand upon.

Kwakakakaka.

The icicles that had been shining white began disappearing back into the ceiling. Perhaps reluctant to part, the ice sparkled with pure white light at the final moment.

The flames that had been surging fiercely began to gradually die down. As the flames swayed left and right, they looked as though they were waving goodbye.

Whooooosh.

A gust of wind blew in and brushed past the lightning marks carved into the floor, and astonishingly, the traces on the ground disappeared cleanly. They vanished like a drawing in sand washed away by waves.

The wind coolly fluttered Geom Mugeuk’s clothes and hair one last time before disappearing completely.

Thus, everything around him returned to the way it had been.

Now this place looked exactly as it had when Geom Mugeuk first entered.

It felt as though he had dreamed.

But Geom Mugeuk sensed the change.

The three energies were still asleep within his body, but they were different from before. If the previous energies had felt as though they were merely being stored inside him, now he felt, Those energies are mine.

It was as if they had once been guests in a guest room, but had now become the owners.

And from those energies, he could sense unprecedented potential. If three energies were like this, he could not know what would happen when all six were gathered.

Geom Mugeuk took the secret coffer from his robes.

The secret coffer was smiling brightly, just like his own heart.

Geom Mugeuk was happy. Truly happy. What made him happier than anything was that this power had not fallen into the hands of the enemy, but had come to him.

Since his regression, he had steadily changed his life from before, but now he felt as though he had passed through a major turning point differently.

‘Don’t let your guard down until the very end. Keep striving.’

Now, Geom Mugeuk put the small secret coffer back into his robes and looked at the large secret coffer. His gaze deepened as he stared at it.

Geom Mugeuk performed a deep bow to the secret coffer. With his entire body, he could feel that this ancient power was not something that could be obtained with a mere spiritual medicine.

“If this is the will Heaven has bestowed upon me, I will gladly accept that destiny.”

He meant it sincerely. After all, the very fact that he had been able to regress in the first place had only been possible because Heaven had valued his efforts.

After formally paying his respects, Geom Mugeuk approached the secret coffer and placed his hand upon it.

“Thank you, secret coffer.”

Heaven’s will was Heaven’s will, but he still considered these secret coffers his friends.

At that very moment!

Swoooop.

Geom Mugeuk’s hand entered the secret coffer. Before he could even resist, his body was sucked into it. The secret coffer had begun absorbing Geom Mugeuk.

“Huh? No! I still haven’t seen the final battle!”

But as if to say that he was not permitted that much, the secret coffer began sucking Geom Mugeuk in at once.

Geom Mugeuk did not resist. Just as he had not when he first entered.

Fine, if this is the destiny you have chosen.

‘I’ll see you in three hundred years!’

* * *

The Sword King was examining the walls of the space where the secret coffer stood.

Several days had passed since Geom Mugeuk had been sucked into the secret coffer. But he had not yet returned.

During that time, the Sword King had explored various methods to enter the secret coffer.

Of course, he had first examined the secret coffer thoroughly. He had knocked on it, tried moving it, and infused internal energy into it. But the secret coffer showed no response at all.

He had returned through the gates and inspected the previous gates as well, and had reread the secret manuals stored there. But nowhere was there any clue about how to enter the secret coffer.

After examining the surrounding walls that way, the Sword King came back to the secret coffer and sat leaning against it. The place where he sat now was the very spot from which Geom Mugeuk had been sucked in.

Just in case he might also be able to enter, he made sure to sit there and lean against it once a day, but today as well, the secret coffer was only cold and hard.

“Hooo.”

For the first time, a sigh escaped the Sword King’s lips. He could not simply keep waiting like this forever.

In truth, his mission was to take that secret coffer.

Whether Geom Mugeuk was inside it or not, once he delivered it, his job would be done.

And yet… he had been waiting here for days.

He was worried about Geom Mugeuk, yet on the other hand, he was not worried. Looking at it coldly, it was closer to not being worried.

The sight of him being sucked in had looked exactly like someone sinking into a swamp, but he did not feel that it had swallowed him in order to harm him.

Perhaps it was because of the special quality Geom Mugeuk had shown.

‘You’re not someone who would die so meaninglessly, are you?’

When he thought of Geom Mugeuk, foolish words came out on their own.

“If you don’t come out today either, I’ll take you away with you still inside.”

Even at the Sword King’s threat, Geom Mugeuk did not come out.

This time, he threatened the secret coffer.

“If you don’t hand over my officer, I’ll cut you clean in half.”

He spoke as though joking, but there truly was a part of him that wanted to cut the secret coffer open with his sword and look inside.

But Geom Mugeuk could not possibly be inside that hard coffer, so he could not do that either.

‘If this really is something that can change the fate of the murim, why did it swallow the Young Cult Leader?’

The Sword King, fiddling with the shoe hanging at his waist, spoke as if to himself.

“Don’t tell me the person who will change the fate of the murim… is you?”

It was right then.

“Uwaaaaak!”

With a scream, something shot out from behind the Sword King’s back. The person who emerged over the head of the Sword King, who had been leaning against the secret coffer, and rolled across the floor was none other than Geom Mugeuk.

The Sword King stared at Geom Mugeuk with a startled expression. He had been so shocked that he had nearly cut Geom Mugeuk down reflexively.

After startling someone this badly.

“Instructor, you’ve gotten haggard in just a few days.”

At those first words, the Sword King spoke with an incredulous expression.

“You look like you went off and had a good time.”

Geom Mugeuk’s eyes were even clearer, and the vitality felt from his body seemed even more vigorous. It was a change that could be sensed because the Sword King was an expert of his caliber.

“Ah, don’t even ask. I suffered quite a lot without any internal energy.”

Geom Mugeuk first examined his own body. The majestic internal energy he could feel in his dantian.

Fortunately, he had returned to the body he had before going in. His sword had also returned to the Black Demon Sword. The secret coffer was still safely in his robes as well.

Geom Mugeuk rose to his feet and took a deep breath.

The scent of three hundred years later. I came back alive!

“You were really worried about me, weren’t you? You must have even stopped eating and drinking.”

“Not at all. I ate meat every day.”

But Geom Mugeuk felt it. The relief the Sword King had felt when he saw him.

He had been waiting for him here. Worried, he had waited for him to come out.

“What happened in there?”

This was truly the first time in his life that the Sword King had ever been this curious about something.

“Before I tell you, please wait a moment.”

Geom Mugeuk walked toward the secret coffer.

The feeling he had when he saw the secret coffer upon first entering this place and the feeling he had now, after meeting the secret coffer three hundred years ago and returning, were completely different.

That secret coffer had passed through three hundred years and was now before his eyes again.

Geom Mugeuk placed his hand on the secret coffer.

‘You must have been lonely for three hundred years. You endured much all this time. And thank you. Thank you for sending me back here.’

It was regrettable that he had not seen the final fight, but it was a fight whose outcome had already been decided.

After greeting the secret coffer that way, Geom Mugeuk turned back to the Sword King.

How much should he tell him? Geom Mugeuk decided to leave out only two things.

The prophecy concerning himself, and the fact that he had learned the secret coffer’s formula.

“Have you heard of the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword?”

Just how much did the Sword King know about this history?

“I’ve heard of him. He was the Murim Alliance Leader three hundred years ago.”

He was, after all, the most famous Murim Alliance Leader.

“Why are you suddenly asking about him?”

Then astonishing words flowed from Geom Mugeuk’s mouth.

“I met the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword and came back.”

“What?”

At first, the Sword King’s eyes seemed to say, This is a joke, right? But gradually, surprise bloomed across his face. He realized that Geom Mugeuk was not joking.

“It’s true!”

“I even ate with the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword.”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned toward the secret coffer.

“This box took me three hundred years into the past.”

The Sword King also stared at the secret coffer in astonishment.

Geom Mugeuk continued speaking.

“This box was once a divine relic of a mysterious force called Heavenly Will Palace.”

Geom Mugeuk watched how the Sword King reacted to Heavenly Will Palace.

It was not a special reaction, but an ordinary one.

“I know of it. The Martial Sovereign Divine Sword destroyed Heavenly Will Palace.”

The history known by most people who knew of the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword went only this far. From this reaction, Geom Mugeuk could guess. The current Sword King was not connected to the descendants of Heavenly Will Palace. To him, Heavenly Will Palace was merely a part of history.

“Don’t tell me you met the palace lord of Heavenly Will Palace too?”

Geom Mugeuk nodded. As if that was all he had met. He had even met the Divine Maiden.

“I spent several days at the place where the final war between the two sides was taking place.”

“Why did it take you there?”

Since he intended not to tell him about the prophecy concerning himself, Geom Mugeuk had no way to explain.

“How would I know? Perhaps because I’m better-looking than you, Instructor? If one is going on a long journey, then of course it’s better to take someone young and handsome…”

“Enough nonsense!”

The playfulness vanished from Geom Mugeuk’s face. Now it was finally time to ask this question.

“Why did you bring me here? Please tell me the real reason.”

There had definitely been some intention behind it. It had not been to save those underground, nor because he would be useful in dismantling the mechanisms. There had clearly been another reason of his own.

At last, the Sword King spoke his true feelings.

“Because I wanted to see whether you were the one who would change the fate of the murim.”

Their gazes tangled in midair.

“Why did you think it might be me?”

After a brief pause, the Sword King answered.

“Because you changed our fate.”

They had believed that they would change the fate of this murim, but Geom Mugeuk had changed their fate instead.

“And now you are trying to change even my fate.”

Geom Mugeuk could sense it. This was the moment when the fate between the Sword King and himself would be decided. It would be decided by the answer to this question.

“What if I am the person who will change the fate of the murim?”

Looking straight into the Sword King’s eyes, Geom Mugeuk added another question.

“Can our relationship change?”

Chapter 605: What Awaits Us
---------------------------
“What if you are the one who will change the fate of Murim?”

It would have been nice if he had said their relationship might change, but the Sword King was cold and resolute.

“Even so, our relationship will not change.”

Geom Mugeuk filled his face with desperation, but the answer did not change.

In truth, Geom Mugeuk had expected him to answer this way. They had formed a special bond thus far, but not to the extent that they would join hands.

His opponent was none other than the Sword King, who had become the First King among the Twelve Earthly Branch Kings. How could the heart of such a man be turned so easily?

No, turning his heart aside, this was still a relationship in which there remained the possibility that he might kill him. After all, they had agreed to cross blades once on the dueling platform.

Geom Mugeuk walked over to the secret coffer and embraced it.

“I’ve been abandoned. Send me back three hundred years again.”

Watching him make such a fuss, the Sword King let out a small laugh. It seemed he had indeed wanted to see that sight again.

As he stroked the secret coffer like that, Geom Mugeuk suddenly asked,

“Is it because of the debt?”

They had spoken about it before. When asked why he had joined hands with them, the Sword King had answered that he owed them a debt.

Turning back to the Sword King, Geom Mugeuk spoke earnestly.

“If it’s because of the debt, then take this to them and pay it all off at once. This divine relic—I’ll give it to you!”

At that, the Sword King thrust his face close to Geom Mugeuk’s and asked,

“Why are you trying to be generous with what’s mine?”

Geom Mugeuk refused to back down and asked in return,

“Why is this yours, Instructor? It’s mine.”

“Yours?”

“You said you’d give me what was in this room!”

—Please give me everything in this room.

Those were the words Geom Mugeuk had spoken just before entering this room, the final trial.

“I never answered that I would.”

Of course, it was Geom Mugeuk being unreasonable. The Sword King had gone through the trouble of dismantling the mechanisms beneath Hwangryong Martial Hall to obtain this secret coffer. There was no way he would hand it over so readily.

“Didn’t you see earlier? I came out of this divine relic. I’m no ordinary person, you know.”

The Sword King could not refute that much.

What if this truly was an object that could change the fate of Murim? And what if the person this divine relic had chosen was him? Would that not make the Young Cult Leader the one who would change Murim’s fate, and at the same time the master of this divine relic?

Seeing him unable to say anything, Geom Mugeuk instead grew embarrassed.

“If you react like that, I can’t keep insisting until the end, can I?”

The Sword King approached the secret coffer behind Geom Mugeuk. He reached out and touched it. As it had for the past few days, the only thing the secret coffer allowed him was this coldness.

Removing his hand from the secret coffer, the Sword King said,

“If you kill me, you may be able to have it.”

Geom Mugeuk said nothing.

“Why are you silent?”

“I’m thinking. Whether I want it badly enough to take it even if I have to kill you, Instructor.”

He looked as though he were truly agonizing over it.

“I don’t think I want it that much.”

The Sword King stared at Geom Mugeuk. The problem was that those words sounded sincere.

“You might need this if you’re going to change the fate of Murim, you know?”

“It doesn’t matter. To me, you are more important than this secret coffer, Instructor.”

The Sword King fixed Geom Mugeuk with a suspicious gaze.

“Don’t tell me?”

The deeper look in his eyes when Geom Mugeuk returned, and that more vibrant vitality as well.

“You gained something inside, didn’t you? So now you don’t need it anymore, do you? This thing is empty. That’s why you’re trying to give it to me, isn’t it?”

Geom Mugeuk did not deceive him.

“You really are quick on the uptake.”

In truth, that was only half true. Looking at the secret coffer, Geom Mugeuk said,

“It’s true that I gained something, but it’s not as though only the shell remains.”

It was clearly connected to the small secret coffer as well. He did not know when or in what form it would show him another surprise.

“Then why are you trying to give it up?”

Because if such a thing as fate truly existed, he believed that no matter where this was, it would become connected to him again.

That was why he could focus on his relationship with the Sword King.

“I told you, didn’t I? You are more important, Instructor.”

“What do you even know about me!”

“Isn’t it better when people don’t know each other too well?”

The Sword King flinched for an instant, and Geom Mugeuk’s words continued.

“If we come to know each other more, we may be disappointed by sides we didn’t know about, or we may grow weary of each other. But not now. What lies within this person? What does he have that draws people in like this? What am I trusting in, to be trying so hard to persuade a man who may kill me?”

“Enough!”

At the Sword King’s shout, a shoe came flying.

Geom Mugeuk caught the flying shoe in his hand.

“If you had launched a surprise attack while throwing this shoe, I would have died. You once threw one as a joke before, so I let my guard down and caught it just now. But why didn’t you ambush me? Why don’t you kill me, go back, and say, ‘I obtained the divine relic and even avenged my dead comrades!’ Why don’t you claim that achievement?”

The Sword King looked down at the shoe that remained at his waist.

“Because I still have one left. So stop talking nonsense. You may really end up dead.”

The reason the Sword King was angry was that he recognized the feeling growing within him: he did not want to kill the Young Cult Leader.

“If our relationship changes? Do you intend to use me as your guide and uncover all the secrets of our organization? Put me in front and kill them all?”

“No.”

“Then what? Are you telling me to become your subordinate?”

“That isn’t it either.”

“Then!”

Geom Mugeuk offered words he did not expect.

“I know you are not someone who will remain by my side, Instructor. I also know that one day, you will suddenly put on those shoes and leave for somewhere far away.”

“Then why? Why do you keep trying to shake me?”

To the Sword King, who shouted the question in a roar, Geom Mugeuk spoke even louder.

“If you didn’t like that, then you shouldn’t have appeared before me barefoot, wearing shabby martial robes, your hair tied tightly, with an old iron sword at your waist that could be bought anywhere in the marketplace. You shouldn’t have appeared before me like that while I was wearing the Black Demon Sword, layering protective garments two and three deep, wrapping myself in top-grade heavenly silkworm silk, doing anything and everything just to survive!”

“!”

“I cannot kill someone like that. No, I will not kill him. For my own sake, I will not do something I would regret for the rest of my life.”

The Sword King was left speechless, for he had never imagined he would hear such words.

The emotion he felt in his heart at that moment was joy. He felt good. He did not know why he felt good, but he truly did. To the point that he wondered if he had ever felt this good before.

Breaking the awkward silence, the Sword King spoke curtly.

“Just because of my atmosphere?”

Geom Mugeuk answered curtly as well.

“What do you mean, just? That is the image I long for, and the freedom I long for.”

“You’re a fool who thinks he’s clever, if I looked free to you.”

“That is why you should come to me. Pay off your debt, cast off everything that binds you, Instructor, and come to me.”

“And when I do?”

At that, the most astonishing words of their entire conversation flowed from Geom Mugeuk’s mouth. Words that shocked the Sword King more than the claim that he had gone to a world three hundred years in the past.

“Please become my friend.”

The Sword King made the most dumbfounded expression he had shown since they met. Instead of saying, You madman, enough already, the Sword King asked,

“Are you serious?”

“I am. Aren’t there friendships in this world that transcend age?”

“Of all the many relationships there are, why friends?”

“My master will be waiting above. So I do not need a master. As it happens, the person who came with me is the Sword Venerable. Since there is already a sword-wielding Demon Venerable, I cannot serve you as a Demon Venerable either. I like wandering around alone, so I do not need a personal bodyguard. As the Young Cult Leader, I have more subordinates than I could ever need. So please become my friend. I am often told I am an old man in a young body. So I think we could become friends who suit each other well.”

The Sword King could feel it. Geom Mugeuk was speaking sincerely.

“A friend I can see when I want to see him, and help when he must be helped. A friend with whom I can sometimes throw shoes at each other.”

As he said that, he held the shoe out again.

The Sword King stared at the shoe for a moment, then reached out to take it. Just then, as something occurred to him, his expression hardened sharply.

“You said the Demon Venerable would be waiting above?”

“Yes. He is surely waiting for me.”

The Sword King sighed. It was the moment he suddenly awoke from a pleasant dream he had been having until now.

“For that reason alone, we cannot become friends.”

“What do you mean?”

“Because there are people waiting for me up there as well.”

In other words, he meant that the Demon Venerables would have died.

“When you go up there, you will be sunk in deep sorrow. Grieving the loss of your master and the Sword Venerable, you will draw your sword and point it even at me. Even so, I will understand.”

He knew very well what the Fist King’s skills were like. Moreover, there had been other masters who came with him.

“Master and the Sword Venerable would not have lost to anyone.”

“We have grasped the Demon Venerables’ abilities. They are not people those two can handle.”

The Sword King could not hide the sorrow in his eyes.

“What awaits us will be nothing but an empty dueling arena where you and I must fight.”

By contrast, Geom Mugeuk’s eyes were full of faith.

“What if the two of them are standing safely on that dueling platform?”

* * *

“Father, I’ll keep watch, so please go in and rest for a while.”

At Ian’s words, the Fist Demon shook his head.

“I’m fine.”

Several days had passed, and Geom Mugeuk had still not come up.

During that time, the Fist Demon had not left the dueling platform. He ate his meals there, and replaced sleep with circulating his qi and regulating his breath.

Ian came to realize it anew. He had vaguely thought of Geom Mugeuk and his father as no more than master and disciple, but his father cared for Geom Mugeuk far more than he had imagined.

“Please don’t worry too much. The Young Cult Leader will come up while making some ridiculous joke.”

The Fist Demon looked at Ian and silently nodded.

The reason he did not leave this place and kept watch was not merely because he wanted to be the one waiting for Geom Mugeuk when he emerged here.

It was also because, should any urgent situation arise concerning Geom Mugeuk, he wanted to respond even a little faster. He did not want the brief moment he stepped away to become a regret for a thousand autumns.

Yeon Baegin was also in a corner of the dueling platform.

Having cleaned away the corpses and broken debris, he had still not left the place.

It was his own decision. The Fist Demon and Ilhwa Sword Venerable did not care whether he left or not.

Yeon Baegin had not yet abandoned hope. He waited only for the Sword King, who would come up from underground. Where would he go after abandoning this Hwangryong Martial Hall? Mistaking resignation for resolve, he was holding out until the end.

Unlike the Fist Demon, Ilhwa Sword Venerable came and went freely. She trained in martial arts and slept comfortably.

Regarding Geom Mugeuk, she had said only one thing to Ian.

“Who is worrying about whom?”

Though she said that, when several days passed without Geom Mugeuk coming up, she too was inwardly worried.

‘Young Cult Leader, I know you like pranks, but do not play a prank like this.’

If Geom Mugeuk did not come up even after today passed, she had decided to discuss going down together with the Fist Demon.

It was right then.

Srrrk.

The chair fitted with the mechanism descended.

Everyone there was startled. Ilhwa Sword Venerable lightly flew through the air and landed behind the Fist Demon. Ian, too, stood behind his father and stared only at the place where the chair had been.

Srrrk.

A moment later, the chair rose again.

The expressions of the three who were watching hardened at the same time.

The person sitting in the chair was not Geom Mugeuk, but the Sword King.

The Fist Demon observed his opponent with a cold gaze.

A barefoot man with his hair tied tightly and an iron sword at his waist.

He revealed no aura, but the Fist Demon could tell at once that he was no ordinary master. If his presence was already this great when he was not properly revealing his aura?

The Fist Demon clenched his fists more powerfully than ever.
