Looking at Azure Mountain Sword, who had become one with his blade, the Demon Buddha rose from the rock.

“Do you know? People outside may not realize it, but our cult has changed greatly of late. Especially among the Demon Venerables these days, growing stronger is all the fashion. Everyone is mad for martial arts.”

The Demon Buddha hopped down from the rock.

“So as not to fall behind that trend, I, too, have made efforts without others knowing. After all, I am the sort of person who absolutely detests losing.”

A new golden radiance flowed from the Demon Buddha’s body. The golden light he ordinarily emitted and the golden light he gave off when fighting differed in the depth of their color.

Ziiing.

Along Azure Mountain Sword’s blade, which vibrated in the air, blue aura flowed like waves.

The very instant Azure Mountain Sword was about to rush at the Demon Buddha!

Shuuuk!

Slice!

What shot forth powerfully was the Demon Buddha’s prayer bead. The bead split in half and fell.

Shuk-shuk-shuk-shuk-shuk-shuk-shuk!

Prayer beads flew in one after another. It was an attack with enough power to pierce straight through the body if struck.

Azure Mountain Sword’s blade embroidered the air. Dozens of lines of sword light appeared in an instant. The prayer beads split in half and rained down to the ground.

When he cut apart the final bead, Azure Mountain Sword saw it.

The Demon Buddha’s two hands, now empty, had already formed a hand seal.

Golden Great Lama Art!

Murderous Dharma Seal!

From the Demon Buddha’s two hands, golden aura surged and flew forth.

Shwaaaaaaak!

The aura spreading like waves could not simply be blocked.

Azure Mountain Sword Art (碧山劍法)!

Azure Mountain Wind Forest (碧山風林)!

Blue aura poured from his sword as well and flew forth.

Thus the golden aura and the blue aura collided in midair.

Kwaaaaaang!

With a thunderous roar, a shockwave powerful enough to shake the earth spread in all directions. Trees snapped, rocks rolled, and clouds of dust rose.

Shwaaaaaak.

Cutting through that dust, Azure Mountain Sword charged in. The surest way to deal with the Demon Buddha was to close the distance and prevent him from forming those hand seals.

At that moment, Azure Mountain Sword saw it.

Another hand seal had already been formed in the Demon Buddha’s hands.

Sky Vow Seal (天空誓印)!

A single streak of aura struck down from the air toward the crown of his head like lightning.

As he was stabbing toward the Demon Buddha, he twisted his body.

Kwaaaang!

With lightning speed, Azure Mountain Sword swung his aura-filled blade and blocked it. A shock rushed in as if his wrist would be severed. The Demon Buddha was far stronger than he had thought.

‘I have to cut off those hands somehow!’

Azure Mountain Sword charged again and flew in. In a flash, he had rushed right before the Demon Buddha, but another flash was waiting for him.

Dharma Radiance Seal!

In that instant, light more powerful than the sun burst from the Demon Buddha’s body. Because it was night, that light was all the more intense.

Azure Mountain Sword squeezed his eyes shut and swung his sword. He cut at the Demon Buddha not with his eyes, but with his senses.

Shiiik!

‘It missed!’

Drawing up his protective aura, he unleashed another ultimate technique. For attack was defense.

Azure Mountain Revolving Wheel (碧山回輪)!

Whiriririririk!

Azure Mountain Sword spun in place like a whirlwind, sending his sword in every direction.

Papapapapapak!

Dozens of strands of sword qi flew out in all directions and lodged in trees and rocks.

Papapapapapapak!

Sword qi flew toward every place he might be, but there was no sound of the Demon Buddha’s flesh being torn.

‘Where is he?’

For a single instant, he had lost sight of the Demon Buddha.

When he found the shining golden radiance beyond a severed tree behind him,

the Demon Buddha had already completed a hand seal. It was the most splendid hand seal, with the fingers of both hands intricately interlaced.

Mara Palm Annihilation Seal!

Whoooooooo.

A massive energy pressed down, and a shadow formed around Azure Mountain Sword’s body.

He raised his head and looked up at the sky. The palm of an enormous golden Buddha statue was striking down at him. Golden radiance poured from that hand.

It was already too late to evade. Azure Mountain Sword raised his protective aura to the utmost limit.

Kwaaaaaang!

With an incredible roar, the skin of the earth overturned and dust billowed up.

When the dust settled, Azure Mountain Sword stood there. He was inside an enormous, palm-shaped pit sunken into the ground. Its depth was several times the height of a man.

As Azure Mountain Sword tried to leap upward, he suddenly vomited blood. His internal injuries were so severe that he could no longer fight. The fact that he had not died was a miracle.

Staggering, he sank down where he stood.

At that moment, he saw it.

From above the pit, the Demon Buddha was now looking down at him.

Seen from below, the Demon Buddha looked like an enormous man.

‘To think I was defeated so emptily?’

It was not because of bad luck. He felt a clear difference in skill. From the moment those tiny prayer beads were launched until this enormous palm struck down, the battle had flowed according to his opponent’s design from beginning to end. He had thought it would at least be close, but the Demon Buddha was a level above him.

From up there, he could not clearly see what expression the Demon Buddha wore. Then the Demon Buddha’s voice reached him.

“Fortunately, it seems I have not fallen behind the trend.”

Seeing the Demon Buddha form another hand seal, Azure Mountain Sword closed his eyes.

‘A perfect defeat.’

It was a fight in which he would have nothing to say even if he died.

He thought the aura that would end his life would fly in.

Whiiiiiik.

His body rose out of the pit on a golden wave.

And landed gently before the Demon Buddha.

Azure Mountain Sword asked with a startled face,

“Why are you not killing me?”

“If I intended to kill you, you would already have died down there.”

It meant that, at least with that final move, he had not used his full strength.

Azure Mountain Sword looked at the Demon Buddha with eyes full of disbelief.

“……Were you always this strong?”

The Demon Buddha’s eyes were gentle, and his face was calm. The momentum with which he had fought as if to kill had already vanished entirely. The radiance that had shone brightly throughout the battle had already subsided.

After the succession struggle, there had been a change in the Demon Buddha’s state of mind. He had also been greatly influenced by his meeting with Geom Mugeuk. And while carving stone statues, he had calmed his heart.

He did things he had never done in his life and felt emotions he had never felt before. As the Demon Buddha’s life changed in that way, his martial arts changed as well.

Because the Demon Buddha had let everything go, he had become stronger.

“Even if you spare me, I will not tell you who summoned me here.”

“I will not ask.”

Azure Mountain Sword looked at him in surprise. He had naturally thought the Demon Buddha had spared him in order to ask that.

“Finding out who stands behind you is the First Young Master’s task. I only came so that the First Young Master could do his own work.”

The Demon Buddha gave Azure Mountain Sword two choices.

“Now, choose. Will you return to your hometown like this? Or will you die by my hand now?”

Azure Mountain Sword had tried to kill the First Young Master, and he had tried to kill the Demon Buddha as well.

“You are going to spare me? Why?”

The Demon Buddha brought his two hands together in prayer.

“Our Buddha bestows compassion upon pitiful sentient beings.”

“Enough nonsense!”

The Demon Buddha answered honestly.

“As you said, this is Wuhan.”

The moment word spread that Azure Mountain Sword had been killed by a Demon Venerable in Wuhan, the orthodox murim would rise like fire. Perhaps that was what those bastards wanted in the first place.

“Still, if you wish it, I will kill you. A great problem will arise, but somehow it will be handled. In the end, only the dead man will feel wronged. You know that too, do you not?”

The Demon Buddha calmly persuaded him.

“Why do you think the First Young Master came all the way to Wuhan and caused this uproar? To topple the Martial Alliance? Do you truly think so? Do you think the best plan we could come up with was merely to send the First Young Master to Wuhan and bring down the orthodox murim?”

After a brief pause, Azure Mountain Sword asked. It was a question without confidence.

“……If not?”

“What did the person who summoned you say it was for?”

Azure Mountain Sword could sense that there was another meaning within the Demon Buddha’s words. Did you truly come because you trusted the person who summoned you?

One thing was certain. If these people were truly plotting a tremendous conspiracy, they would not have spared him.

After remaining silent for a long while, Azure Mountain Sword chose one of the two options.

“I will return. I will go back to my hometown and not come out for three years.”

The Demon Buddha knew. If only because of his pride, he would not say that he had returned because he had lost to him. He would return with some other excuse, so at least the fact that the Demon Buddha had come to Wuhan would not become known.

“This is the fourth time, so when we meet for the fifth, let us share a drink.”

Azure Mountain Sword gazed at the Demon Buddha, then turned and walked away without a word.

As he staggered away, the Demon Buddha called to him.

“Hey, friend.”

When Azure Mountain Sword looked back, the Demon Buddha said calmly,

“Not the First Young Master next time either.”

From the Demon Buddha’s deepened gaze, Azure Mountain Sword could tell just how much he cared for the First Young Master.

“If something like this happens again……”

And the Demon Buddha did not hide such feelings.

“Whether it is not in Wuhan, but even before the Alliance Leader’s Hall of the Martial Alliance, you will die by my hand.”

After finishing his words, the Demon Buddha respectfully brought his hands together, then turned and walked away.

Watching the faint golden radiance disappear into the darkness, Azure Mountain Sword once again moved his steps in the opposite direction.

And so the two banners grew distant from each other once more.

Chapter 626: When Will the Person You Called Arrive?
----------------------------------------------------
“I can’t believe it.”

Gwak Yeong was deeply shocked when she learned that the White Dragon Corps Leader was involved in this affair.

She could understand, to a point, that the merchant who had supplied materials to the iron workshop had been involved. But the White Dragon Corps Leader had been colluding with the people behind it?

‘Could this man be deceiving me?’

How unbelievable did it have to be for such a thought to cross her mind?

But Gwak Yeong trusted this man. That was why even this thought occurred to her.

‘If he intended to deceive me, I’d be deceived helplessly.’

How many times had they even met? And yet now, she trusted this man more than the White Dragon Corps Leader.

“If it hadn’t been you who told me, I wouldn’t have believed it.”

Geom Muyang, who normally would not have said anything, for some reason offered a single remark.

“If you trust just anyone like that, it will be hard to survive in this murim.”

After a brief pause, Gwak Yeong said,

“You’re not just anyone. You’re the benefactor who will save my younger brother, aren’t you? I have to trust you. Even if I can’t trust anyone else, I have to trust you. Even if everyone in the world curses you, I alone won’t.”

When Geom Muyang stared at her, Gwak Yeong said to him,

“Yes, I know I’m putting pressure on you. It’s just that this is all I can do right now.”

Geom Muyang, who had been looking at her, turned his head toward the window.

Just then, Homyeong entered the room.

“These are the materials sent by Tongcheon Pavilion regarding the White Dragon Corps Leader.”

Geom Muyang took the few sheets of paper Homyeong handed him and read them. They contained personal information on the White Dragon Corps Leader—what sort of temperament he had, and through what path he had risen to the position of corps leader.

“His wife died early, and he has one daughter.”

At that, Gwak Yeong cried out,

“His daughter must have been kidnapped.”

She had naturally assumed everyone would think so.

“Why do you think that?”

“What?”

“It could be something he chose to do himself, couldn’t it?”

At Geom Muyang’s question, Gwak Yeong was left speechless.

She had always thought the White Dragon Corps Leader was a good person. Whenever he saw her, he treated her warmly, with a smile.

But thinking about it, she knew almost nothing about the White Dragon Corps Leader.

If she judged it coldly, the man who had just read those documents likely knew more about him than she did, despite having known him for over ten years.

Could it truly have been his choice, not because of threats?

“What will you do now?”

“I’ll meet the White Dragon Corps Leader.”

“Will he agree to meet you?”

There was no way the White Dragon Corps Leader would meet him. And even if he did, he would surely have countless experts lying in ambush.

But Geom Muyang said something she had not expected.

“The one meeting the White Dragon Corps Leader won’t be me. It will be you.”

Gwak Yeong was startled. She could tell he meant it. Since meeting him, this man had never once made anything like a joke.

“You told me to use you to catch them, didn’t you?”

“That’s true, but…”

She knew it too. This was not a problem that would be solved by hiding as though they were being chased.

To find out where her younger brother was, they had to follow this clue. Right now, everything they knew about the enemy began and ended with the White Dragon Corps Leader.

But that was not the important part.

“You said my brother would die if I went outside, didn’t you?”

Geom Muyang nodded.

“And you’re still telling me to go?”

Once again, he nodded. Rather than resentment, bewilderment came first. She knew well that he was not the sort of person to break a promise so easily.

“You promised you would save my brother.”

“This is to keep that promise.”

Gwak Yeong stared at Geom Muyang. His eyes seemed to say this. No, she hoped they were saying it.

If you’ve decided to trust me once, trust me to the end.

Last time, she had begged and shouted for him not to let her out even if she died. She had said that even if he told her to lie buried in the dirt breathing through a reed, she would do it.

But this time, those words did not come out. Because despite everything she had said, he was telling her to do this. That meant it was something that had to be done.

“Fine. In return, make me one more promise.”

She spoke honestly.

“If you’re going to save him anyway, make sure you save me too.”

* * *

“Bring me some porridge.”

At the customer’s request, the restaurant owner was about to say that they did not sell porridge, but then he flinched.

The customer was a martial artist with white hair and a square face, and his face was filled with anger.

From long experience, the owner knew very well that he must not offend the temper of such a guest.

“Yes, I’ll prepare it at once and bring it out.”

The martial artist was none other than Han Wolgaek.

After going in and out of the privy all day, he had only just managed to settle down. This was truly the first time he had ever suffered such a severe stomach upset.

‘To think I couldn’t regulate it with my internal energy. Have I grown old too?’

It never occurred to him that someone might have deliberately caused him to suffer an upset stomach.

Since it had happened after he drank wine and ate side dishes at Honghwaru, he ought to have turned the place upside down. There was only one reason he held himself back.

They say Han Wolgaek had a stomachache. They say he spent the whole day running to the privy.

He absolutely could not tolerate such rumors spreading through the murim.

A short while later, the owner brought the porridge.

After eating even a bowl of porridge like that, he finally felt somewhat alive again. To think skipping meals for just one day could leave him this hungry.

Just then, a little child entered the restaurant and handed Han Wolgaek a note.

“A martial artist told me to give this to you.”

Then the child scampered away.

After checking the contents of the note, Han Wolgaek burned it with his Fiery Yang true qi.

Fwoosh.

Those Demonic Cult bastards he had failed to kill yesterday—he could simply kill them all today. Those miserable lives had merely been granted one more day by his mercy.

Han Wolgaek rose from his seat and left the restaurant.

As he walked toward the entrance, he saw a familiar young man eating.

‘Where have I seen him before?’

Then his eyes fell on the collecting basket and herb pouch set beside the young man.

‘Ah! That fellow from then!’

The one who had been crouching by the roadside staring at grass, to whom he had advised that he should swing a sword instead if he had time for that. Of course, he was the Poison King.

Han Wolgaek was about to simply pass by, but then turned back and walked over to the Poison King.

He had met this man right before his stomach trouble began. With a faint suspicion, he looked down at him. If the man had done something to him, surely he would not show his face again like this.

As if he had no idea someone was standing before him glaring, the Poison King simply kept his head lowered and continued eating.

“Hey.”

Only when Han Wolgaek called out did the Poison King raise his head.

“Why are you loitering around me?”

The Poison King wore an expression that said he had no idea what Han Wolgaek was talking about. He even seemed not to remember him.

I remember him, but he doesn’t remember me? It was a subtly irritating blow to his pride.

How dare he not remember me?

He was displeased, but he could not get angry over something like that.

“Fine. If your head is dull, then I suppose you’ll have to dig up herbs diligently to make a living.”

Han Wolgaek turned away. For one reason or another, he was in a foul mood.

Was failing to remember his face really something to snap at the man over? How petty. Truly petty.

That thought crossed his mind, but he did not go back and apologize.

How long had he walked after leaving the restaurant?

Han Wolgaek began scratching his arm. It itched as though he had been bitten by a mosquito, but scratching did not make it feel better.

At first his arm prickled, and then his back began to itch.

‘Damn it, why am I suddenly so itchy?’

When he rolled up his sleeve, his arm was covered in millet-sized hives. The sight startled him.

‘No way. Did that porridge upset me too?’

It was not only his arm. His back, stomach, legs, and even his buttocks—his whole body itched so much he could not bear it.

Forget dignity or anything else; he stood in the street and scratched himself furiously. Passersby went far around him to avoid him. It itched too much for him even to get angry.

He tried to draw up his internal energy and do something about it, but no method he knew could stop the itching.

At that moment, an old man who had been telling fortunes by the roadside spoke to him. His words were enough to make Han Wolgaek forget even the itching.

“Sir, you have come to a place you should not have come.”

Han Wolgaek looked at the fortune-telling old man while scratching himself. The old man’s gaze was rather deep, and he seemed to possess some spiritual insight.

“What are you talking about?”

The fortune-telling old man said to Han Wolgaek,

“You came to drive out devils, only to become possessed by one yourself.”

Han Wolgaek stood blankly for a moment and stared at the fortune-telling old man. The reason he did not roar at him for spouting nonsense was that the old man had guessed he had come to drive out devils.

“Leave the land of Wuhan at once, before a greater disaster befalls you.”

In the end, he could not hold back and shouted,

“Enough nonsense!”

He was going mad from the itching, and now what was this old man saying? What was wrong with everyone today?

He should have spoken with the old man a little longer, but Han Wolgaek could no longer endure it.

He began running with lightness skill toward a physician.

Once Han Wolgaek left, the Poison King came over and sat side by side with the old man.

The fortune-telling old man was none other than Sangseon, who served the Poison King.

“He won’t be able to move today either.”

If a mere physician could solve it, how would he be called the Poison King? Perhaps if the Martial Alliance’s Divine Physician came, it would be another matter. As for today, he would get nothing done but scratch himself.

“I had originally intended to use something less painful.”

It was the result of Han Wolgaek letting his mouth run carelessly.

Sangseon looked at the Poison King with a smile. Though he so hated coming out into the world, once he did, the Poison King handled anything this well.

“Still, this much won’t make him leave.”

At Sangseon’s words, the Poison King spoke with meaning.

“If he sees even a ghost, he’ll believe your fortune.”

* * *

“Artisan Gwak has returned.”

At his subordinate’s report, the White Dragon Corps Leader was startled.

Indeed, Gwak Yeong was entering after following his subordinate.

“What on earth happened?”

“That’s what I want to ask. I went out briefly for outside business and came back, only to hear that I had been kidnapped.”

At those words, not only the White Dragon Corps Leader, but all the martial artists with him were shocked.

“Then you weren’t kidnapped?”

“As you can see.”

The White Dragon Corps Leader looked her over. Since she did not have a single injury, her words seemed true.

“Who on earth said that? That I had been kidnapped?”

“There was an anonymous report from someone who claimed to have witnessed you being kidnapped. After receiving the report, we checked your residence and found you gone.”

“It was a false report.”

The White Dragon Corps Leader looked at her for a moment, then smiled broadly.

“It is fortunate that you have returned like this.”

The White Dragon Corps Leader said to his subordinates,

“Stop the search and return to the Alliance. Prepare a carriage to escort Artisan Gwak.”

The subordinates who received the order moved busily. They also sent people to relay the order to those searching other areas.

The White Dragon Corps Leader said to Gwak Yeong,

“Come, let us return together.”

“I’ve made you go through trouble for nothing because of me.”

“It is enough that you are safe.”

The two boarded the carriage, and the subordinates escorted it as they went.

No matter how Gwak Yeong looked at him, she could find nothing suspicious about the White Dragon Corps Leader. He asked her several times whether she was hurt anywhere.

‘This man is a villain?’

This time, it seemed that man had made a mistake. Seeing him again, she could tell. The White Dragon Corps Leader was a good person.

As they were returning, the White Dragon Corps Leader had the carriage stop.

He said to his subordinates,

“You go back first.”

And so the White Dragon Corps Leader sent the carriage and his subordinates back ahead of them.

“You and I should have a brief talk.”

The place where the carriage had stopped was the very estate where Geom Muyang had first hidden.

The two entered the estate. Traces of construction remained here and there.

“What is this place?”

At that, the White Dragon Corps Leader said coldly,

“You know, don’t you?”

“What do you mean?”

“The place where they first brought you.”

He seemed certain that she had been kidnapped.

“Why are you lying?”

“I am not lying.”

The White Dragon Corps Leader’s expression changed. It was an expression that made her think, So this man can wear such a cold face too.

“Where are they now?”

At the White Dragon Corps Leader’s interrogation, a question arose in her mind.

“Why didn’t you ask this when everyone was present, instead of here, when it’s just the two of us?”

“For your sake.”

Gwak Yeong’s expression also settled calmly.

“You think I am in league with them.”

“Are you not?”

At that, Gwak Yeong said with a meaningful expression,

“I am in league with you, Corps Leader.”

For an instant, the White Dragon Corps Leader’s expression flinched.

“I know as well. That you are on my side.”

The White Dragon Corps Leader silently stared at Gwak Yeong. From that gaze, Gwak Yeong could tell. This man had truly joined hands with them.

“If you suspected me, isn’t it dangerous for you to be here alone like this?”

Just then, a voice came from behind.

“He must have something he’s relying on.”

Geom Muyang emerged from behind the building. Behind him, Homyeong and the martial artists of the Ji Corps followed.

But even at their appearance, the White Dragon Corps Leader showed no surprise or fear whatsoever. Rather, a trace of gladness crossed his face.

“You must have intended to use this woman to lure me out, but I had already expected as much.”

At those words, Gwak Yeong looked at Geom Muyang in shock.

They had set a trap, only to be caught in one themselves.

Geom Muyang’s face remained impassive, revealing no emotion.

The White Dragon Corps Leader shouted loudly.

“Please come out now and wipe out all these devil bastards!”

The person he was relying on was Han Wolgaek. If it was him, not a single demonic practitioner here would be able to return alive. The Han Wolgaek he knew was an absolute master who represented the orthodox sects.

“Please come out!”

But Han Wolgaek did not reveal himself.

The White Dragon Corps Leader’s expression hardened. He had proceeded with this matter believing that he would definitely come. He had said he would definitely come.

“Whoever it was, it seems he isn’t coming.”

At that very moment!

The manor gates slowly began to open.

‘He’s here!’

The White Dragon Corps Leader, who had been rejoicing, was startled out of his wits. The person who entered was not Han Wolgaek. Someone truly unexpected had appeared.

‘Why is this person here?’

He was so shocked that he was at a loss for words for a moment.

But soon, one thought occurred to him. There was no way this man could be on the same side as those demonic practitioners.

‘He sent this person instead of Han Wolgaek!’

Inwardly, the White Dragon Corps Leader was truly astonished. To think his influence was enough to move even this person.

Amazingly, the person who had appeared there was Jin Hagun, the leader of the Demon Extermination Unit.

Martial artists climbed up and surrounded the manor atop its walls. They were the warriors of the Demon Extermination Unit.

Homyeong and the martial artists of the Ji Corps all drew their swords at once and formed a ring around Geom Muyang.

A smile appeared on the White Dragon Corps Leader’s lips.

“Leader Jin! Quickly eliminate these wicked enemies of the Demonic Cult!”

Jin Hagun’s and Geom Muyang’s gazes met in midair.

Among their subordinate warriors, a taut tension flowed, as if it might burst at any moment.

Geom Muyang’s gaze shifted from Jin Hagun to the White Dragon Corps Leader.

“When is the person you called coming?”

At the unexpected question, the White Dragon Corps Leader turned to Jin Hagun with an incredulous expression and shouted.

“What madness are you spouting? Is he not right here?”

Then astonishing words flowed from Geom Muyang’s mouth.

“That man is someone I called.”

Chapter 627: I Am Accustomed to Killing People
----------------------------------------------
Who called whom?

The leader of the White Dragon Corps hoped he had heard wrong.

“What nonsense is that!”

After barking the words, the White Dragon Corps leader looked at Jin Hagun, who had walked up beside him.

“Is what he said true?”

He asked with a sinking sense of disbelief, but Jin Hagun’s answer struck him like a bolt from the blue.

“It is.”

The White Dragon Corps leader’s heart dropped. The Demon-Extermination Unit should have come in Han Wolgaek’s place.

‘Han Wolgaek has to come, even if he’s late.’

What if he arrived and attacked them before any words could be exchanged? Everyone knew how much Han Wolgaek hated the Demonic Cult.

If a fight broke out like that, the Demon-Extermination Unit would end up helping Han Wolgaek. For no reason whatsoever would they side with the Demonic Cult.

‘Please, come!’

It was a plea he would never have made had he known what state Han Wolgaek was in right now.

The White Dragon Corps leader was not the only one shocked. Gwak Yeong and the warriors of the Earth Corps were just as stunned.

The Eldest Young Master had called the leader of the Demon-Extermination Unit?

Only Homyeong, who had been ordered by Geom Muyang to send word to Jin Hagun through Tongcheon Pavilion, had known this in advance.

Gwak Yeong’s gaze turned toward the leader of the Demon-Extermination Unit. As someone belonging to the Martial Alliance, she knew better than anyone what kind of man that leader was.

The grandson of the Martial Alliance Leader, and the most likely successor to the position.

The Demon-Extermination Unit he commanded was the finest elite force in the Martial Alliance. That was why the best-quality weapons from the Iron Workshop were always issued to the Demon-Extermination Unit first.

‘You called someone like that? He’s not the kind of person who comes just because he’s told to. Just who on earth are you?’

Gwak Yeong stared at Geom Muyang in astonishment. The one who answered her question was Jin Hagun. He asked the White Dragon Corps leader,

“Leader Do, do you know who that man is?”

“I do not.”

Jin Hagun then said calmly,

“He is the Eldest Young Master of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.”

The White Dragon Corps leader was startled. No, he pretended to be startled.

In truth, he had known the other party was Geom Muyang. If the other party had been an ordinary demonic practitioner, he would have dealt with him personally instead of waiting for Han Wolgaek.

The one most shocked by those words was Gwak Yeong.

‘That man is the Eldest Young Master of the Demonic Cult?’

That meant he was the elder brother of the next Heavenly Demon. The fact that the Demonic Cult’s succession struggle had ended peacefully had once been the talk of the martial world.

She truly had not imagined it. That the man she had been staying with all this time was the Eldest Young Master of the Demonic Cult. She had shouted at such a person and even pestered him to save her.

She had done that to someone who had nearly become the Heavenly Demon.

‘No, seriously! If you had such an incredible status, you could have told me beforehand. Didn’t you want to brag, “I’m someone like this”?’

If it had really been just the two of them, she would have said exactly that.

Geom Muyang was the first to greet Jin Hagun.

“It has been a long time.”

“Have you been well?”

Jin Hagun showed Geom Muyang proper courtesy. This greeting was not to the Eldest Young Master of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. It was a greeting to Geom Mugeuk’s elder brother.

“Thank you for coming.”

“Of course I had to come.”

Jin Hagun suddenly recalled what Geom Mugeuk had said to him during the three-party meeting.

—My brother is naturally taciturn. He resembles you quite a lot.

Do I really resemble that man so much? He could not tell yet, since he had never had a private conversation with him.

When he received Geom Muyang’s letter, he had found it unexpected. Truly, Geom Muyang had seemed like someone who would never contact him, no matter what happened.

The letter had contained the following.

There was an organization forging and selling hidden weapons of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, and while pursuing that organization, they had come all the way here.

But no matter the reason, it was unacceptable for them to have entered Wuhan.

Even if the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult was in the middle of an operation, Wuhan alone was a forbidden operational zone.

If Geom Mugeuk’s existence had not served as a buffer, the atmosphere right now would have been far more vicious.

The White Dragon Corps leader did not miss this moment.

“Leader Jin, what is your relationship with the Eldest Young Master of the Demonic Cult? You seem acquainted.”

There was clear intent behind this question.

‘He called the leader of the Demon-Extermination Unit? I’ll make sure that becomes a bad move instead.’

The White Dragon Corps leader brought out Jin Hagun’s greatest weakness.

“If it becomes known that Leader Jin is acquainted with the Eldest Young Master of the Demonic Cult, the orthodox martial artists will be greatly shaken. Does the Alliance Leader know of this?”

Since Jin Hagun had not yet formally become the successor, if he fell out of the Alliance Leader’s favor or caused a scandal, he could lose that chance.

He was aiming for that point, preventing the Demon-Extermination Unit leader from showing any friendliness toward Geom Muyang.

As if that intent had worked, Jin Hagun asked Geom Muyang in a tone that sounded like a rebuttal.

“What does that matter have to do with the White Dragon Corps leader here?”

“I believe the White Dragon Corps leader has been recruited by that organization.”

At Geom Muyang’s answer, the gazes of the Demon-Extermination Unit warriors all turned toward the White Dragon Corps leader at once.

The White Dragon Corps leader raised his voice boldly.

“No matter how much you may be the Eldest Young Master of the Demonic Cult, watch your tongue. To frame me with such accusations is to disregard the orthodox martial world and look down on this Alliance!”

His voice rang out thunderously.

The White Dragon Corps leader turned to Jin Hagun, urging him to agree. He had deliberately mentioned the Martial Alliance. If this became a matter tied to the Alliance’s honor?

‘As long as the position of successor to the Martial Alliance is at stake, the leader of the Demon-Extermination Unit has no choice but to take my side.’

That was what he believed.

But instead of agreeing with him, Jin Hagun asked a question.

“Leader Do, do you know what kind of organization that is?”

“No, I do not.”

Jin Hagun stared intently at him and asked,

“Then why did you not ask what kind of organization it was? That should have been the first thing you were curious about.”

For an instant, the White Dragon Corps leader flinched. In his agitation, he had missed that point.

Soon, he spoke as if it were nothing.

“It’s obvious they must be an organization created by those Demonic Cult bastards.”

Jin Hagun nodded once, then asked again,

“Who were you waiting for?”

“What do you mean?”

“I heard you when I arrived earlier. You called for someone to come out, did you not?”

For a moment, the White Dragon Corps leader was at a loss for words. Earlier, thinking Han Wolgaek was there, he had shouted for him to come out. It seemed Jin Hagun had heard it. Who should he say it was?

“I was pretending someone was there so these people would not act recklessly.”

Though he answered on the fly, when Jin Hagun stared at him without a word, he instead raised his voice.

“Are you suspecting me as well, Leader Jin? Me, who has devoted my entire life to the Alliance!”

Did he not know that the louder he shouted, the weaker and more suspicious he appeared?

Jin Hagun’s gaze turned back to Geom Muyang.

In truth, he did not know Geom Muyang well. And yet the reason he felt an inexplicable trust in him was because of Geom Mugeuk.

Because he was the elder brother whom Geom Mugeuk had wanted so desperately to save.

“Do you have proof?”

“The place where the forged hidden weapons were made was rented by that White Dragon Corps leader.”

At Geom Muyang’s answer, the White Dragon Corps leader once again appealed his innocence.

“What an absurd false charge! To put it bluntly, if I were part of that organization, would I have rented it myself?”

Just then, someone spoke coldly.

“Hypocrite.”

Everyone’s gaze turned to Gwak Yeong, who had said it. Earlier, when she had been alone with him, she had been able to tell that he was on the same side as those bastards.

“And I actually believed in you.”

Jin Hagun naturally knew Gwak Yeong. She was a rare woman in the Iron Workshop, and its youngest master craftsman, so there was no way he would not know her. He also knew that the White Dragon Corps had searched Wuhan this time because of her.

“Master Craftsman Gwak, how did you become involved?”

At Jin Hagun’s question, she could not bring herself to speak. Whatever the reason, she had damaged the honor of the Alliance.

After taking a deep breath, she spoke honestly.

“I made the forged weapons.”

Jin Hagun found it unexpected. He had known her to be someone who would never do such a thing. As expected, there had been a reason.

“My younger brother was kidnapped by them.”

Jin Hagun nodded. He had a younger brother himself, so how could he not understand her? The only thing he could not understand was this.

“Why did you not inform us?”

The one who answered in her stead was Geom Muyang.

“I believe the head of those bastards is among the highest ranks of the Martial Alliance.”

Jin Hagun’s expression hardened. It was already difficult to believe that the White Dragon Corps leader was involved, but someone even higher up was connected?

At that moment, Gwak Yeong hesitated. Should she cling to Jin Hagun’s trouser leg and beg? Please save my younger brother. For her brother, that would be the best thing.

But the promise she had made with Geom Muyang held her back. It was a promise made by the Eldest Young Master of the Demonic Cult, so she could not ask someone else to save him too.

Just then, Geom Muyang abruptly said to Gwak Yeong,

“I will keep only the second promise.”

The second promise?

Gwak Yeong understood that it referred to her request that, since he was saving her brother anyway, he must save her as well.

Then what about the first promise? That’s the more important one.

That promise passed to someone else.

“Leader Jin. Please save this woman’s younger brother.”

“!”

Jin Hagun was surprised, and Gwak Yeong was even more surprised.

“I am someone more accustomed to killing people than saving them. Rescuing people is something you are far better at, is it not, Leader Jin?”

At that moment, Jin Hagun wondered whether the reason Geom Muyang had called him might not have been to handle the White Dragon Corps leader or deal with the leader supposedly among the higher-ups.

Because if it were him, he would likely have tried to resolve it somehow on his own.

And yet he had still called Jin Hagun?

Perhaps it was because he wanted to keep his promise to that woman.

For people like them, whose activities were restricted, saving someone was far less likely than having him and the Demon-Extermination Unit do it.

‘Were you this kind of person?’

Their gazes intertwined in the air. Even he would have made the same choice. A life that had to be saved was more important than pride. Perhaps that was why Geom Mugeuk had said they were alike.

At last, Jin Hagun said calmly,

“Understood. We will make certain to save your younger brother.”

Since this was a matter concerning Gwak Yeong, who belonged to the Martial Alliance’s Iron Workshop, they had justification to step forward.

Gwak Yeong was overjoyed.

“Thank you so much, Leader.”

It might have been rude to Geom Muyang for her to be this happy, but she could not hide her joy.

The finest martial artists she knew were the warriors of the Demon-Extermination Unit.

She also knew better than anyone how righteous a martial artist Jin Hagun was. There could be nothing better for her younger brother.

After expressing her gratitude to Jin Hagun, Gwak Yeong turned back to Geom Muyang.

He was still looking at her with an impassive face.

She could feel it.

This taciturn man had chosen the surest method to save her younger brother.

Even if it meant setting aside his pride. This man, who seemed likely to possess stronger pride than anyone else.

‘If my brother returns safely, I will make weapons for you for the rest of my life.’

That was the resolve she made at that moment.

And for Geom Muyang’s sake, she gave one more statement.

“The White Dragon Corps leader was recruited by them.”

“How do you know that?”

At Jin Hagun’s question, she looked coldly at the White Dragon Corps leader and said,

“Because today, here, I saw that man’s other face.”

Since the words could not serve as evidence, the White Dragon Corps leader snorted.

“Leader Jin, that woman has already gone over to the demonic practitioners. She may have been subjected to mind-control arts, or she may have been threatened. Surely you do not mean to say you distrust my words and believe the words of those demonic practitioners?”

Jin Hagun had yet to make any decision.

“We will know once we investigate.”

“Let us go, then. I will tell you anything.”

The White Dragon Corps leader was inwardly relieved. If only he could leave this place and return to the Alliance, that man would save him.

Jin Hagun was about to tell Geom Muyang to leave it to them and return as he was. He intended to smooth over Geom Muyang’s unauthorized entry into Wuhan at his own level somehow.

At that moment, Geom Muyang said something he had not expected.

“Leave that investigation to us.”

A silence fell in an instant. Everyone understood what those words meant. It meant they would interrogate the White Dragon Corps leader themselves.

“Impossible.”

Jin Hagun refused firmly. He could concede other matters, but that was impossible.

“Once they learn that he has been captured, they will cut off every tail.”

As he said that, he looked at Gwak Yeong.

Jin Hagun understood what he meant. He meant that woman’s younger brother would be killed first.

“We must follow the tails before that happens.”

It meant this fight was a battle against time.

They could not torture the White Dragon Corps leader when his guilt had yet to be proven.

And if, as Geom Muyang said, the head of these people was truly among the leadership, then once he cut off the tail, that bastard would never be found.

“Give us exactly one hour.”

Jin Hagun looked at the White Dragon Corps leader standing beside him.

“You’re not really going to hand me over, are you?”

When Jin Hagun gave no answer, the White Dragon Corps leader struck at his weakness again.

“The moment you hand me over to them, Leader Jin, your position as successor will be finished. The leader of the Demon-Extermination Unit handed over the White Dragon Corps leader to demonic practitioners—do you think you can bear the consequences? Absolutely not! I swear on my life! I am innocent!”

Swish.

Tap.

Jin Hagun sealed his acupoints.

Then he said firmly to Geom Muyang,

“I will give you exactly fifteen minutes.”

Geom Muyang and the warriors of the Earth Corps took him and went inside the building.

There were countless reasons it should not be allowed, but only one reason it should.

Jin Hagun followed his instinct. The instinct that told him this choice was right.

The warriors of the Demon-Extermination Unit knew full well how dangerous this choice was.

What if the White Dragon Corps leader had committed no crime?

The aftermath would be enormous.

But the warriors of the Demon-Extermination Unit, whose bond with Jin Hagun had deepened during their escape from the formation last time, did not doubt this decision.

And fifteen minutes later.

Geom Muyang walked out of the building, shook the blood from his hands, and said,

“He confessed.”

Chapter 628: I Intend to Settle Things with These Bastards
----------------------------------------------------------
It had been exactly a quarter of an hour.

Everyone stared at Geom Muyang in astonishment. Judging from the blood on his hands, it was clear he had conducted the torture himself.

Geom Muyang slowly walked over to Jin Hagun.

Jin Hagun was inwardly relieved to hear that the White Dragon Unit Leader had confessed.

He had suspected that the man was lying, but if that suspicion proved wrong, the consequences would be irreversible.

First, he asked about the White Dragon Unit Leader’s life.

“Is he alive?”

Geom Muyang answered as if it were only natural.

“His life is in no danger.”

If the man had died, it would have been a grave discourtesy to Jin Hagun, who had trusted him and left the matter in his hands.

That made it all the more astonishing. He had extracted a confession from someone who did not seem likely to open his mouth easily, and had done so while leaving him intact.

“What did he confess?”

At Jin Hagun’s question, Geom Muyang looked around. It was a silent question asking whether he could speak in front of the men of the Demon Extermination Squad.

Jin Hagun nodded once, as if to say it was fine.

“The person behind it was not one of your higher-ups. It was someone with no position whatsoever in the Murim Alliance.”

Jin Hagun felt relieved inwardly, yet puzzled at the same time. If it was not the Alliance’s leadership, why had the White Dragon Unit Leader been won over by them?

Then, from Geom Muyang’s mouth came a name he had never imagined.

“Cheonae Geosa (天涯居士), Hwa Yulcheong (華律淸).”

“!”

Shocked, Jin Hagun’s eyes flew wide open.

Impossible. He had never expected that name to appear. It was a name that should not have appeared.

Is that true? Truly? Jin Hagun glared at Geom Muyang.

“That cannot be.”

Instead, Jin Hagun grew calm. Because this was simply absurd.

Hwa Yulcheong was an absolute master of the orthodox faction. Not only was his martial arts superb, his character was so admirable that countless martial artists respected him. He did not even covet wealth or power. When he obtained money, he helped the poor, and he never forgave those who committed evil.

And most decisively of all.

“He is my grandfather’s old friend.”

He was the closest friend of the Murim Alliance Leader, Jin Paecheon. The two had spent long years together. Perhaps his grandfather trusted him even more than he trusted his own grandson.

How could Jin Hagun possibly believe it when such a man had been named as the one behind all this?

Not only the warriors of the Demon Extermination Squad, but Gwak Yeong as well could not believe it. What orthodox martial artist did not know Cheonae Geosa?

Jin Hagun spoke firmly.

“That man must have lied.”

Jin Hagun even found Geom Muyang suspicious. Was he deceiving him for some unjust purpose? No, this was a name he could not be deceived by even if he wanted to be.

“I must hear it from the White Dragon Unit Leader himself.”

“Do so.”

Jin Hagun strode into the building, and Geom Muyang followed after him. The warriors of the Demon Extermination Squad and the Earth Unit followed as well.

In the middle of a spacious room, the White Dragon Unit Leader was sitting in a chair.

Geom Muyang had said he was fine, but Jin Hagun had still expected him to be half dead. Surprisingly, however, he looked unharmed.

There was not a single wound on his face, and his eyes still had life in them. Only his clothes were soaked through with blood.

The White Dragon Unit Leader had looked glad to see Jin Hagun, but when he saw Geom Muyang enter behind him, he was horrified and averted his gaze. He was utterly terrified. It was a stark contrast to the White Dragon Unit Leader who had been shouting so boldly earlier.

Jin Hagun walked up to him.

“Unit Leader Do, are you all right?”

The White Dragon Unit Leader was overcome with emotion, but he could not show it. His fear of Geom Muyang standing behind Jin Hagun was tremendous.

“I am here, so you are safe. Now say it again. Who is behind this?”

At last, the White Dragon Unit Leader opened his mouth, his eyes filled with fear and resentment.

“The pain was too much, so I blurted out any name I could think of! I have no face to show you.”

After speaking, he broke down sobbing. To anyone watching, he looked full of grievance.

Jin Hagun looked back at Geom Muyang. Even after the White Dragon Unit Leader changed his statement, Geom Muyang did not press him. He looked like someone who had expected the man to alter his testimony.

Sure enough, Geom Muyang said indifferently.

“This is why I do not trust people.”

He said nothing more. It felt as though he were saying, It is your choice whose words you believe.

Until the moment he had entrusted the White Dragon Unit Leader to him, Jin Hagun had believed Geom Muyang. He had handed the man over because he trusted him.

But now, he was troubled. Should he truly believe Geom Muyang’s words?

Because of that heavy name, Cheonae Geosa.

The White Dragon Unit Leader said tearfully.

“Please believe my words. Please believe my loyalty.”

After being lost in thought for a moment, Jin Hagun said to the White Dragon Unit Leader.

“It seems I misunderstood you.”

Jin Hagun’s attitude toward Geom Muyang changed abruptly.

“To think Cheonae Geosa is behind this? I nearly let myself be deceived by demonic men.”

He glared at Geom Muyang with cold eyes and said,

“Did you think such a trick would work?”

At that, the warriors of the Demon Extermination Squad drew their swords in unison and pointed them at the warriors of the Earth Unit. The warriors of the Earth Unit drew their swords as well.

A taut tension spread between them.

But in both number and skill, the warriors of the Earth Unit could not be opponents for the Demon Extermination Squad. If the order to attack was given, annihilation would only be a matter of time.

Geom Muyang merely gazed at Jin Hagun with deep eyes. Their gazes tangled in midair.

Gwak Yeong was flustered by the sudden situation. She wanted to step forward and shout.

‘I believe the Young Master!’

She had personally confirmed that the White Dragon Unit Leader was not the man she had known. She wanted to say she would stake even her life on it.

But she could not step forward. This was not her place to intervene. Moreover, the one who had promised to save her younger sibling was that leader of the Demon Extermination Squad.

In a gentle tone, Jin Hagun apologized to the White Dragon Unit Leader.

“Unit Leader Do. I am sorry.”

“Not at all. It is enough that you recognize my loyalty even now.”

He truly looked like a man who had returned from the brink of death. With his personality, one would have expected him to say something to Geom Muyang in a situation like this, but the fear had been carved so deeply into him that he would not even meet his eyes.

“When you return, go to a physician first and receive treatment. I will report this matter to the Alliance Leader.”

In that instant, the White Dragon Unit Leader flinched. If this matter reached the Alliance Leader, the fact that he had confessed would also reach Cheonae Geosa. The fact that he had given up his name so readily under torture.

“Please do not tell him that I named such an esteemed person.”

“I cannot do that. I must tell the Alliance Leader the full account of what happened. But do not worry too much. Once he hears the circumstances, he will understand that you had no choice but to give that name because you were tortured.”

The White Dragon Unit Leader pleaded again.

“Is there any need to trouble such an esteemed person with this matter?”

Jin Hagun stared at him. The eyes that had been gentle until now turned cold.

“Are you perhaps afraid you will be killed to silence you?”

The moment he said those words, Jin Hagun did not miss the White Dragon Unit Leader’s expression. The unmistakable bewilderment of a man struck in a vital spot.

Jin Hagun interrogated him.

“You could not endure the pain, so you gave any name, and of all the countless people in the world, it just happened to be Cheonae Geosa? You call that an excuse? Did I look foolish enough to believe it?”

Cheonae Geosa was the person furthest removed from conspiracy. His was not a name that would come up when someone was naming any random person.

Moreover, Geom Muyang had said earlier that their leader was among the highest ranks of the Murim Alliance. If the man had been going to give a false name, he would have chosen one of them.

Not only that, but from the way he was desperately trying to keep the fact of his confession from reaching Cheonae Geosa’s ears, Jin Hagun could tell that he had truly named Cheonae Geosa as the one behind it.

That was why a chill ran down Jin Hagun’s spine, and he began to think that Cheonae Geosa might really be behind it. It was simply too unexpected a name.

The White Dragon Unit Leader begged and made excuses to the very end.

“No. Please! It is a misunderstanding! I merely…”

As if he did not want to hear any more excuses, Jin Hagun pressed his acupoint.

When the White Dragon Unit Leader collapsed to the floor, Jin Hagun ordered the warriors of the Demon Extermination Squad.

“All of you, lower your swords.”

The men who had been surrounding the warriors of the Earth Unit lowered their swords and stepped back.

The warriors of the Earth Unit also sheathed their swords in inward relief. In that brief moment, they had crossed the boundary between life and death.

Jin Hagun apologized courteously to Geom Muyang.

“I was only acting in order to confirm that man’s true intentions, so please understand.”

Astonishingly, Geom Muyang had understood his intent.

“I believed that was the case.”

“Did you not say you do not trust people?”

After a brief pause, Geom Muyang answered.

“Because Mugeuk is your friend.”

Just as Jin Hagun trusted Geom Mugeuk’s elder brother, Geom Muyang likewise trusted Geom Mugeuk’s friend.

“My younger brother would not have befriended just anyone.”

If Geom Mugeuk had heard it, it was something he would have gone around boasting about all day.

The misunderstanding had been cleared up, but Jin Hagun’s heart was still heavy.

‘Truly Cheonae Geosa?’

Even after confirming that the White Dragon Unit Leader had confessed, he still could not believe it.

“What will you do now?”

At Geom Muyang’s question, Jin Hagun shook his head with an expression that said he did not yet know what to do either.

Then he returned the question to Geom Muyang.

“What will you do?”

Ordinarily, he should have said, This is our affair, so all of you should withdraw from Wuhan.

But he did not. If not for him, they would never have uncovered this great secret.

Geom Muyang’s eyes took in Gwak Yeong, who stood far away.

Even if he left like this, Jin Hagun would protect her and save her younger sibling.

There was no need for him to help Jin Hagun either. This was an internal matter of the Murim Alliance, and it was something he ought to handle.

But the reason he had personally left the cult this time still remained.

Because of that forged weapon, he had nearly killed his own younger brother with his own hands. That rage would not subside unless he eliminated them all.

That alone was reason enough for this decision.

“I intend to see this through to the end.”

* * *

The Demon Buddha sat alone, lost in thought.

The place where he was staying was an empty hut in the mountains near Wuhan. After sending the Blue Mountain Sword back to his hometown, he had remained here for a while.

He could control the golden radiance emitted from his body, but he could not hide his short stature.

Since Wuhan was a place where countless masters came and went, he stayed inside whenever anyone passed by, lest someone recognize him.

Then he sensed a presence outside.

“Are you there?”

“Come in.”

The person who entered the hut was the Poison King. It was the first time the two had met since coming to Wuhan.

When they were in the main cult, it was usually the Demon Buddha who went to find the Poison King, but today it was the opposite.

“The atmosphere here is quite nice.”

He said it out of awkwardness, but what atmosphere could there be in this chilly hut abandoned by a hunter?

An awkward silence flowed for a moment. There had been a time when they had gone around Thousand Poison Forest digging up poisonous herbs together, but these days they did not see each other often, and the two had grown somewhat awkward. They had returned to their original relationship.

Just then, the Demon Buddha brought something from a netted sack in the corner and quietly held it out to the Poison King.

“I happened to notice it earlier while taking a walk.”

It was three or four roots of poisonous herb.

“It is Masaengcho!”

It was a poisonous herb on the list the Poison King had made of things he absolutely intended to dig up here in Wuhan.

No matter how hard he had tried to find it, it had not appeared, yet it had so easily caught the eye of the Demon Buddha out on a walk.

“Thank you truly.”

The moment the poisonous herb appeared, the awkward atmosphere vanished at once.

The Poison King said with sparkling eyes,

“Shall we go out for a walk?”

Seeing the Poison King look as though he wanted to go out immediately, the Demon Buddha smiled faintly. When it came to poisonous herbs, his eyes shone. The thought naturally came to him that one had to love poison that much to bear the name Poison King.

“Have you eaten?”

“Not yet.”

“I am a bit hungry. Let us have a simple meal and then take a walk. Wait a moment. It is nothing much, but it will fill our stomachs.”

The Demon Buddha rolled up his sleeves and began cooking.

Looking closer, there were several kinds of mushrooms in the sack. It seemed he had picked them while gathering poisonous herbs.

First, he lit the campfire in front of the hut, boiled water, and cooked the mushrooms.

“You gave me Masaengcho, so I should be the one treating you to a meal.”

“Can you cook?”

The Poison King shook his head. It was always Sangseon who made meals. By contrast, the Demon Buddha’s hands moved skillfully.

“You seem to have cooked quite a lot.”

“I wandered through the Central Plains and often had to eat alone.”

Because the Demon Buddha’s appearance stood out so much, he had usually filled his stomach while camping outdoors. Even when he came out this time, he had brought basic seasonings to use while camping.

How could the world know? That the short man cooking alone somewhere in the forest was the Demon Buddha.

The Poison King watched the Demon Buddha cook. Originally, he had no interest whatsoever in the other Demon Venerates, but the Demon Buddha was different.

Though it was a dish made in a flash, the Demon Buddha’s mushroom dish was excellent.

“It is delicious.”

“I am glad it suits your taste.”

The Poison King busily moved his chopsticks. All he wanted was to finish eating quickly and go out with the Demon Buddha to dig up poisonous herbs together.

Just then, Sangseon came and relayed the contents of an urgent message sent from Tongcheongak.

“According to the information sent by the Young Master, Cheonae Geosa is behind this affair.”

Unlike the calm Poison King, the Demon Buddha was startled.

That was because he knew very well what position Cheonae Geosa held among the orthodox faction, and how deep his friendship was with the Murim Alliance Leader.

“That is truly unexpected.”

At the Demon Buddha’s words, the Poison King mentioned two names.

“The Cold Moon Guest and the Blue Mountain Sword.”

The Demon Buddha nodded. Yes, it would have taken someone on the level of Cheonae Geosa to summon the Cold Moon Guest and the Blue Mountain Sword.

If he truly was involved?

This matter had become far more dangerous.

“Even so, the Young Master will try to see it through to the end.”

The Young Master he knew was not the sort of person to abandon something halfway through.

Without a word, the Poison King toyed with the Masaengcho before him. The look in his eyes now was different from the look he had worn when he suggested going for a walk. He knew as well. That he might have to step to the forefront.

The Demon Buddha recalled the most important reason he had needed to come.

To prevent the Poison King from stepping forward.

But if the matter grew this large? If misunderstandings piled up and things went wrong?

The golden radiance from the Demon Buddha’s body deepened.

“Our enemy will not be Cheonae Geosa, but the Murim Alliance Leader.”

Chapter 629: More Handsome and More Terrifying
----------------------------------------------
Geomugeuk was standing in a field, reading a book.

Having mastered the Divine Eye Art, his eyes did not tire no matter how many books he read, but for someone who had spent his life flying, leaping, and roaming the martial world, sitting with his backside glued down was agony.

So the idea he came up with was to read while standing like this. Even that soon grew tedious.

Geomugeuk moved his feet. He was not walking forward, but upward. Using Void-Stepping, he slowly walked through the air as he read, then this time flung himself down as though lying on a bed.

Geomugeuk rolled about in the air. He read lying on his back and read lying on his stomach. It was as though an invisible cloud were supporting him.

Within the Time-Space Transposition Art, he was employing the Sicheon Secret Art, displaying such divine might, and even reading books. Of course, since he was not training in martial arts, it was a kind of play made possible by his overflowing internal energy.

Geomugeuk had read more than several hundred books so far. One-third were books unrelated to martial arts, and the remaining two-thirds were martial arts manuals.

No matter how good something was, it only lasted a day or two; crises had come several times. Reading books was harder than martial training. It was boring, difficult, and stifling. And whenever that happened, a powerful temptation came to him.

—Will reading a few books really help? You! Do you have the luxury for this?

But Geomugeuk did not give up.

In his life before regression, he had walked that distant road alone. He was a man who did not know how to give up.

If he kept reading books like this, perhaps it would turn out this way.

If his father said, Where was that book again? he would answer, It is on the eighth bookshelf, fourth compartment.

Of course, realistically, reading every book here was impossible. He could not spend all his time here doing nothing but reading books. That should be yielded to someone else.

“Elder Doma, when I become the cult leader, I will grant you permission to enter the Heavenly Demon Library whenever you wish. Read to your heart’s content then. Read them all!”

If not for the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, he would not have been interested in reading at all, nor would he have even thought of entering this place. This training was thanks to the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

Moreover, all these books were already verified works of high quality.

Among the manuals Geomugeuk had read, several contained martial arts so supreme that if they were released into the current murim, a blood calamity could occur. Even the Severing Moon Sword Art (斷月劍法) he was reading right now was a sword art that had swept through murim three hundred years ago.

Geomugeuk, who had been reading the manual in midair, descended to the ground.

He seemed to stare quietly ahead, then struck out with the Black Demon Sword as fast as lightning.

In a flash, a streak of sword light split space vertically.

Swaaaaaaaaaa.

The grasses in the field all lay down in one direction at once.

Crraaaaaack.

At the same time, the ground split open in a straight line far into the distance.

After examining the cut surface, Geomugeuk shook his head as though dissatisfied, then thrust out his sword again.

Crraaaaaack!

This time, the ground split open anew beside the earlier fissure.

Comparing the second cut surface with the first, Geomugeuk sat between the two lines and pondered.

Through such a process, the third split was created.

After confirming the third cut surface, Geomugeuk finally wore a satisfied expression. It was the moment the Severing Moon Sword severed the moon.

Snap.

When he flicked his fingers, the split ground returned to its original state.

Geomugeuk placed the Severing Moon Sword Art on the desk and opened the next book.

This one was also a martial arts manual. Recently, he had become completely absorbed in the fun of reading martial arts manuals. If interest stirred while he read, he would actually try them out, as he had done a moment ago with the Severing Moon Sword Art.

One might think that, since it was a manual he was reading for the first time, he would only be able to perform the early forms. But that was not the case.

Rather, it was easier to perform profound and deep forms. Since Geomugeuk’s realm was so high, the more difficult they were, the easier they were for him to perform.

On the contrary, when he saw easy forms, many thoughts came to him. That, too, was because his realm was high. He did not merely perform them and be done with it; instead, thoughts followed one after another. If I changed it like this, it would be stronger. If I changed it like that, it would be faster.

This, too, was an experience he had for the first time. Most of what he had experienced after entering the library was new to him, and this learning had a great influence on Geomugeuk.

He compared them with one another, studied them, and discovered flaws as well.

As he continued reading martial arts manuals like that, Geomugeuk’s understanding of the origins of martial arts deepened.

How are martial arts created?

And beyond that.

How are martial arts inherited and developed?

Before long, he came to consider the differences between those martial arts and the Guhwa Demonic Art.

What makes the Guhwa Demonic Art special?

It was a question he had never once asked while cultivating the Guhwa Demonic Art until now.

If he had not read other martial arts manuals, he would only have been able to answer like this.

Because it is originally a powerful martial art.

But now he was experiencing the process of coming to know the reason in concrete detail.

Thinking and researching. Reading and reading again. Thinking again.

Then, at a certain moment, he was able to make one conjecture. Even that conjecture was possible only because he had read countless manuals.

Is this why the Guhwa Demonic Art is strong?

It was not a conclusion, only a conjecture, and yet at that moment, Geomugeuk felt that the time had come.

From deep within his heart, a passion rose up, telling him to draw his sword at once.

Geomugeuk immediately set down the book and unfolded the Guhwa Demonic Art.

First Form, Annihilation Form.

The four evil spirits appeared.

The frightening one, the handsome one, the mysterious one, and the clever-looking one. They had now become so familiar that they felt like family.

‘It’s been a while, East, West, South, North!’

Slice! Slice! Slice! Slice!

After slashing the targeted point from all four directions, they vanished.

Whenever his martial realm was about to rise, there was always a change in these fellows.

And today was no different.

Just before they disappeared, the look they gave Geomugeuk was different from before.

A gaze that wanted something.

That tickled Geomugeuk’s heart.

Geomugeuk knew well what this ticklish sensation was. A signal that his martial realm was ready to rise! At last, that moment had come.

‘I cannot miss this opportunity.’

When one’s martial realm rose, it did not rise gradually like climbing a slope; it was like stepping up onto a stair in a single moment.

Geomugeuk fell into a state of selflessness and trained in the Guhwa Demonic Art. He unfolded it again and again. He met the evil spirits dozens, hundreds, thousands of times.

“Ah, I’m exhausted!”

Geomugeuk plopped down where he stood.

“It must be easy for you, watching like that.”

The one Geomugeuk was speaking to was the secret coffer placed on the desk. When training wore him out, he would speak to the secret coffer like this.

“What? You think I can attain achievement in the Guhwa Demonic Art just because I read some books? You say I should meet the Demon Venerable instead during this time? How long has it been since you came here? The Drunken Demon must be feeling so neglected that he has fallen into a wine jar?”

If that much was him speaking in place of the devil’s whispers borrowed through the mouth of the secret coffer,

then the will within Geomugeuk whispered.

“Think of Father? Think how blood-soaked his training must be, when he has even entered closed-door cultivation. And yet you are idling? When Father comes out, are you going to say, Young as I am, I rested because I was tired?”

No, absolutely not.

Geomugeuk sprang to his feet. From the day he first regressed until now, the strongest driving force that raised him up had been his father. All the more so now, when his father was also in closed-door cultivation.

‘Father, I will absolutely accomplish it too.’

Geomugeuk swung his sword again.

The four evil spirits cut space with one will, and the terrifying evil spirits charged in a line, sweeping through the world.

When the strongest wall in the world was raised, a single streak of sword light split the thick darkness, and lightning of sword energy fell like rain.

Even when it felt as though his arms would fall off, he swung his sword. When his internal energy ran out, he regulated his qi and breathing, then repeated it again.

If he gave up now, he felt as though the four evil spirits, East, West, South, and North, would never again look at him with expressions that wanted something. He did not want to see their disappointed faces.

He did not know how many days and nights passed after that.

It was not hard. He did not think it was hard.

Effort does not belong in the category of hardship. What is truly hard is a situation in which one does not even have the chance to make an effort.

Geomugeuk poured out all at once the training he had neglected while reading books. As if gathering qi and releasing it in a single burst.

Only the secret coffer watched over that blood-soaked effort.

And then came the fated moment!

Geomugeuk’s eyes trembled as he stared blankly at one place.

He had performed the same form as always, yet the Annihilation Form had changed.

The four evil spirits, East, West, South, and North, had grown larger.

Even when he closed and opened his eyes, wondering if he had seen wrong, it was the same. Moreover, they had become more handsome, more frightening, more mysterious, and more intelligent-looking.

Swaek! Swaek! Swaek! Swaek!

The power of the swords they brought down had also become far stronger.

The moment he saw that, Geomugeuk let out an exclamation.

“…I’ve reached the ninth level.”

Sure enough, the gazes of the evil spirits that had wanted something from him had grown calm again.

And just before the four evil spirits disappeared, they smiled in satisfaction, as though telling him he had worked hard. Was he so exhausted that he was seeing things?

Thud.

With the tension released, Geomugeuk sank down where he stood. A severe fatigue rushed over him.

But soon, Geomugeuk sprang back up. He could not spend this precious moment asleep. He was too happy to sleep anyway.

Hoping it had not been a dream, he unfolded the Guhwa Demonic Art again.

Second Form, Great Annihilation Form.

The evil spirits that spread out before his eyes had likewise grown larger. And their number was a staggering twenty-six.

Kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa!

With tremendous force, they completely swept through the front. In the place they passed, the ground was gouged out, and not a single blade of grass remained. Its power could not be compared to before.

“I really have reached the ninth level!”

Geomugeuk cheered. He was truly happy. He had shown what kind of result came when a Heavenly Martial Body bestowed by the heavens made blood-soaked effort.

The one who had watched that effort with him was the secret coffer, and the one smiling at him now was also the secret coffer.

Of course, this achievement had not been attained simply by reading books. Nor was it only because of his blood-soaked effort.

A lifetime of experience and learning gained in his life before regression.

Added to that were the human relationships he had shared in this life with his father and the many people around him, and the real combat experiences in which he had crossed life and death while dealing with the Twelve Zodiac Kings.

All of these combined to produce this astonishing achievement.

‘This is only the beginning.’

For attaining full mastery at the ninth level was far more difficult than what he had done so far.

Moreover, Geomugeuk’s goal was not full mastery at the tenth level. It was full mastery at the twelfth level. He could not know how arduous the road ahead would be.

At this point, there was one anticipation that made Geomugeuk’s heart tremble.

If he climbed just one more step now, he would meet the Heavenly Demon Soul.

What kind of Heavenly Demon Soul will mine be?

There were many people with whom he wanted to share this joy, but the first person who came to mind now was his father.

If his father asked how he had reached the ninth level?

It is thanks to reading diligently.

How would his father react?

And what wall was his father overcoming right now?

‘Father, I have crossed one great hurdle!’

Just then, from outside, the report of Jeokyeon, the head of the guards, was heard.

“They are urgently seeking you at Tongcheon Pavilion.”

* * *

When Geomugeuk arrived at Tongcheon Pavilion, a new flag had been planted on the strategic terrain map.

A new flag standing side by side beside his elder brother’s flag.

Annihilation (滅).

It was the flag of Jin Hagun, meaning the commander of the Demon-Extermination Unit.

“The two of them met!”

Geomugeuk had sincerely hoped his elder brother would meet Jin Hagun. In the land of Wuhan, where everyone was an enemy, Jin Hagun was at least someone who would show consideration for his brother.

“The two most boring, taciturn fellows in the world have met.”

At those words, Sama Myeong smiled. He, too, knew the personalities of the two men.

“How did they meet?”

Then an unexpected answer came.

“They say the Eldest Young Master contacted him first.”

“My brother did, first? That can’t be.”

Given his brother’s personality, there was no way he would have sought out Jin Hagun first. There must have been a reason he had to.

Just then, a new letter sent from Wuhan arrived.

After checking its contents, Sama Myeong’s expression stiffened. From that expression alone, it was clear something serious had happened.

“A new figure has appeared.”

A subordinate strategist walked toward the strategic terrain map of Wuhan.

The Murim Alliance headquarters.

There, more than ten flags were planted.

The experts of the Murim Alliance who were worthy of having their own flags.

At the central Alliance Lord Hall stood a splendid flag with a dragon drawn on it. At the center of the dragon was written the character Righteous (正).

It was the alliance lord’s flag of the Murim Alliance Lord, Jin Paecheon.

The subordinate strategist placed another flag beside the flag of the Murim Alliance Lord. It was a new flag that had not existed in the Murim Alliance until now.

And in the seat beside the alliance lord’s flag, at that.

Splendor (華).

Chapter 630: This Fight Is Not Ours
-----------------------------------
“Behind them was Cheonae Geosa, Hwa Yulcheong.”

At Sima Myeong’s words, Geom Mugeuk recalled the man he had forgotten.

The Murim Alliance Leader’s closest friend, Cheonae Geosa.

In his life before the regression, the man had never appeared on the front stage. Even in the era when the Twelve Branch Kings ruled the murim, he had never shown himself to the outside world.

After the Alliance Leader’s death, Geom Mugeuk had never heard news of Cheonae Geosa.

No rumors that he had stepped forward to avenge the Alliance Leader, no rumors that someone had seen him somewhere. Thinking about it now, the man had vanished from the murim.

Before the regression, in Geom Mugeuk’s life, the Murim Alliance Leader’s intimate friend had held no significance at all.

He had forgotten even the man’s very existence, and yet today he had heard that name.

And the special meaning that single-character flag held only for Geom Mugeuk was this.

‘Come to think of it, this man was a Hwa as well.’

Looking at the flag together with him, Sima Myeong said worriedly,

“There are more than a few orthodox masters who follow Cheonae Geosa. If he truly is the one behind this matter, this fight will not be easy.”

The worst-case scenario was this:

If they killed him without being able to reveal to the world that he was a villain.

Then the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult would have murdered the Murim Alliance Leader’s closest friend and a venerable old master respected by the orthodox faction.

If that happened, the orthodox martial artists would rise like wildfire, and only after countless people had burned to death would the flames be extinguished.

Naturally, as the Chief Strategist, Sima Myeong knew this Cheonae Geosa well.

“He is a man who formed a friendship with the Alliance Leader from their youth. I find it hard to believe that such a person was recruited by those bastards.”

Sima Myeong also kept in mind the possibility that this information might be wrong. That was how unbelievable it was.

Geom Mugeuk, on the other hand, was suspecting the man who had suddenly intruded into his life.

“Perhaps he wasn’t recruited.”

Sima Myeong understood what Geom Mugeuk meant. It meant he might not have been recruited, but could be the mastermind of the conspiracy.

“Are you saying he approached him intentionally from the very beginning?”

He could not help being shocked. Those words meant this conspiracy had been planned for decades.

“We’ve already confirmed that the bastards behind the scenes we’ve faced so far prepared for a very long time, haven’t we? Perhaps the starting point of that preparation was the seat beside the Alliance Leader.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, Sima Myeong’s expression turned grave. If that was truly the case? This fight was not merely going to be difficult.

‘Of all times, this had to happen when the Cult Leader is absent!’

For a moment, the two of them silently watched the busy operations command room.

At last, Sima Myeong broke the silence first.

“Do you wish to go to Wuhan?”

Geom Mugeuk had never thought the day would come when he would say such words, but.

“Yes. I want to go to my brother.”

He wanted to rush there at once.

“Will you send me?”

Though he knew well how Geom Mugeuk felt, Sima Myeong firmly refused.

“No. Instead, I will send more Demon Venerables.”

Because the situation was dangerous, Sima Myeong was all the more unable to send Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk understood him.

But separate from that understanding, there was also a signal of fate that had been given to him.

Because this news had arrived on the very day the Guhwa Demonic Art reached the ninth stage.

“There is a reason I must go.”

“What reason?”

“I believe that with just the Poison King and Demon Buddha there, they can protect my brother.”

“Then why do you say you must go?”

Just as the reason he could not be sent was clear, the reason he had to go was also clear.

“If Cheonae Geosa is behind this, he will surely try to move the Murim Alliance Leader. If the Alliance Leader moves, the Murim Alliance moves, and then the entire orthodox murim will move.”

It might be too great a leap, but.

“I even think they may have dragged us in with that in mind.”

After all, they had used a master craftsman from the Murim Alliance’s Iron Workshop to make the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult’s Golden Dragon hidden weapons.

“They will likely make matters complicated.”
