How could Sima Myeong not know? That the person who could respond most flexibly in a situation like this was Geom Mugeuk. Moreover, the Young Cult Leader had a deep friendship with Murim Alliance Leader Jin Paecheon.

Even so, Sima Myeong could not make a decision easily.

His gaze turned to the two flags on the strategic terrain map.

Orthodox (正) and Hwa (華).

Around them were placed the flags of a dozen or so orthodox masters.

For now, there were only a dozen or so, but the moment the Murim Alliance Leader raised his banner, dozens, hundreds of flags throughout the Central Plains would gather to defend the orthodox murim.

He already wanted to summon back the Great Young Master who had gone out, and now he was supposed to send out Geom Mugeuk as well?

Just as the two of them stood firm, neither yielding his will, a new message sent from Wuhan arrived.

“It is a message from the Great Young Master.”

The letter was delivered to Sima Myeong. After checking its contents, Sima Myeong said to Geom Mugeuk with a startled expression,

“The Great Young Master has requested support from Demon Buddha.”

Since he did not yet know that the Poison King and Demon Buddha were already in Wuhan, he had requested Demon Buddha’s help from headquarters.

“You know my brother’s personality, don’t you?”

A man who would break before he bent had asked for help, like bamboo swaying in the wind.

If it had been his brother in the past, he would rather have died than hoped for someone’s help.

But now his brother had changed. He had become someone who could govern his emotions for the sake of the greater cause. They said he had called Jin Hagun first; perhaps that, too, had come from this same frame of mind.

“Given my brother’s personality, the fact that he requested support means he is certain Cheonae Geosa is behind this. It also means the pressure he feels on site is that great.”

After pondering deeply, Sima Myeong finally made his decision.

“Very well. Go. However, there is a condition.”

“What is it?”

“Take two more Demon Venerables with you.”

It was a proposal imbued with Sima Myeong’s determination that, even if tensions with the orthodox faction rose to the extreme, he would absolutely not allow Geom Mugeuk to die.

“My brother and I, and four Demon Venerables besides. We could wage a Great Demon-Orthodox War.”

Sima Myeong took Geom Mugeuk’s joke seriously.

“Even if war breaks out, the Acting Cult Leader must return safely.”

A war could be dealt with, but Geom Mugeuk’s death could not.

However, Geom Mugeuk declined Sima Myeong’s proposal.

“I will go alone.”

“Acting Cult Leader!”

“The people already there are enough to provoke the Murim Alliance. If more Demon Venerables go on top of that, problems will arise that we need not have faced.”

Sima Myeong acknowledged that he had no choice but to trust Geom Mugeuk. As he said, the more Demon Venerables he sent, the greater the danger that would arise. This was something only Geom Mugeuk could resolve.

“Then please be sure to remember this.”

Sima Myeong walked toward the strategic terrain map.

“Acting Cult Leader, you are not going there to fight them. In my judgment, we must not allow this fight to become a contest of strength.”

Sima Myeong picked up Cheonae Geosa’s flag from among the two placed side by side and moved it outside the Murim Alliance.

“This is what you are going for.”

It meant he was to make the Murim Alliance Leader learn the truth.

“And it must be the Murim Alliance that eliminates them.”

He was saying the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult must not stand at the center of the fight.

“You must persuade the Murim Alliance Leader and the Great Young Master to make it so. This fight is the Murim Alliance’s fight, not ours. All the more so because the Alliance Leader and Hwa Yulcheong are old confidants.”

Geom Mugeuk could understand what Sima Myeong was worried about. He feared that if matters went wrong, the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult might be misunderstood as having killed the Murim Alliance Leader’s friend.

After all, long friendship sometimes blinds our eyes and makes us turn away from painful truths.

“Acting Cult Leader, though you will be there, at the same time you will be in our cult’s Heavenly Demon Library.”

It was Sima Myeong’s request that he remain that secretive and safe, then return.

After listening to everything, Geom Mugeuk answered politely.

“I will move according to the Chief Strategist’s plan.”

One might have expected his expression to relax a little, but Sima Myeong remained even more on edge.

“I will deploy the demon army as far forward as possible and notify the Demon Venerables in advance not to leave the cult. If you need help, send word immediately.”

Geom Mugeuk obediently nodded, but he thought there would be no such thing.

If a situation came in which he had to request help even after he had gone, then by that time, war would already have broken out.

“I will be back.”

“Please return safely.”

Even at the moment of departure, Geom Mugeuk did not forget to make one joke.

“I’m absolutely not leaving because there’s too much work as Acting Cult Leader!”

Once Geom Mugeuk left, Sima Myeong immediately spoke to the staff officer beside him.

“From this moment on, raise the Tongcheon Pavilion’s emergency level to the highest grade!”

* * *

When Han Wolgaek, who had been unable to sleep for the past two days because of the itching, woke up in the medical clinic, it was already past noon.

He had suffered so much that only now did he realize this place was a physician’s treatment room.

He had truly itched enough to die, but when he opened his eyes, it did not itch at all. The hives that had broken out all over his body had vanished as if they had been a lie.

‘What on earth was that?’

Then suddenly, the old fortune-teller’s words came to mind.

—You came to drive out devils, only to be possessed by one.

Crazy old bastard. He had simply eaten something bad and fallen ill.

He felt sorry toward the person who had called him to Wuhan. He had already broken his promise twice. He was not normally such an unreliable person.

But he could not say he had failed to go because of an upset stomach, and even less could he say it was because of itching.

‘I have no choice but to repay it by annihilating those Demonic Cult bastards.’

That was the only way to make up for this.

Han Wolgaek rose from his seat and opened the door to go outside.

Someone was standing in front of the door.

“Gah!”

After confirming who it was, Han Wolgaek was startled and stepped back.

Who would dare make him retreat like that?

Han Wolgaek stared at the other person with eyes full of fear.

He had no choice.

Because the man standing before him was someone who had died long ago.

“……You are?”

Han Wolgaek’s voice trembled.

The man merely looked at him with indifferent eyes.

“What bastard is playing tricks on me!”

Han Wolgaek roared and thrust out a palm strike.

Shwaaaaaak.

Kwaaaang!

The wall of the corridor behind was smashed apart, but the other party remained standing in front of him.

Han Wolgaek saw it. His palm force had passed right through him. He was an illusion.

‘A ghost?’

And in broad daylight? It was hard to believe, but it was true. And that was not all.

Ssssst.

Behind the man, another person appeared, this time a woman.

Han Wolgaek knew that woman as well. She was the wife of the man who had appeared before him. The woman’s gaze toward Han Wolgaek was full of resentment.

Han Wolgaek sighed and recalled the past.

After his family had been killed by demonic practitioners, he had wandered the jianghu like a madman, killing demonic practitioners.

At the time, the one called the Lustful Ghost, who had been committing evil deeds, was also a demonic practitioner. After months of pursuit, Han Wolgaek found him.

That day, too, the bastard was in the middle of violating a woman.

He thought he could easily kill a mere lecherous fiend, but the bastard was stronger than expected.

After a life-and-death struggle, at the very moment he finally killed him!

Someone rushed into the room from behind.

Han Wolgaek instinctively swung his sword and cut down the one charging at him from behind.

A line of blood was drawn across the man’s neck, and he collapsed, spraying blood.

The man was the woman’s husband.

Cutting someone down without confirming who they were—thinking about it now, it was an absurd mistake. But back then, that was how he had been.

Even as he exchanged dozens of moves in that cramped room, he had fought while devoting all his attention to making sure the woman did not die. He had that much skill, and yet why? Just why had he done it? Even now, he did not know.

The man’s face, and the sight of the woman screaming as she saw him, remained unforgettable even decades later.

At that moment, Han Wolgaek’s stomach churned and dizziness struck him.

Then, on the neck of the man standing before the door, a line of blood appeared, and blood flowed down.

Han Wolgaek frantically unleashed palm techniques.

Shwaak! Shwaaaaaak!

Bang! Kwaang!

The palm forces he released in succession brought the walls on all sides crashing down.

The fortune-teller’s words seemed to resound in his heart.

—Leave Wuhan at once before you suffer a greater calamity.

The walls on all sides shattered.

The ceiling as it collapsed also turned to powder and flew away.

The figures of the ghosts were hidden by the dust. When the dust settled again, Han Wolgaek stood alone in the middle of the ruined room.

Instead of ghosts, another sight appeared before his eyes.

The people working at the clinic were staring at him with startled faces. Among them was a young physician sitting on his backside beside a collapsed wall.

At that sight, Han Wolgaek’s heart sank. Because he had just poured out his palm force like a fit, he had almost killed him.

Why had he done that back then? When even now, he was still making such mistakes?

“Haa.”

After letting out a deep sigh, Han Wolgaek took out all the banknotes he had in his breast pocket and placed them on the floor.

“I am sorry for destroying your house.”

Then he staggered out of the clinic.

He left Wuhan on that road without looking back.

Far away, atop a giant tree, two people were watching him. They were the Poison King and Demon Buddha.

“What on earth did you do?”

“That man was poisoned with a toxin made from a mushroom called the Heart-Phantom Spirit Mushroom.”

“What kind of hallucination did he see?”

“I don’t know. What one sees differs from person to person.”

What Han Wolgaek had seen were not ghosts, but the guilt he had buried deep in his heart for his entire life.

Demon Buddha looked at the Poison King with newfound eyes. He himself had struggled in battle to send the Blue Mountain Sword back, but the Poison King had sent this man back without even fighting.

The only thing that could send Han Wolgaek back like this was the Poison King’s poison. Ah, there was one more. The Young Cult Leader’s mouth.

“Would you like to experience it once? To see what sort of hallucination appears?”

When the Poison King asked seriously, Demon Buddha shook his head.

“I decline. What could there be inside me that is so pleasant?”

At that moment, Sangseon flew over to the giant tree where they stood and landed.

“They say the Great Young Master contacted headquarters.”

It was surprising news to Demon Buddha more than anyone else.

“The Great Young Master requested help from Demon Buddha.”

The moment he heard those words, Demon Buddha was astonished. It was truly something he had never expected.

Demon Buddha’s normally calm eyes trembled.

The Great Young Master had chosen him. Among all those people, he had asked him for help.

One might say it was only natural, since he had once been the Demon Venerable closest to him, but when he thought of the disappointment he would have felt if he had chosen someone else, this joy was beyond words. Moreover, was he not already here in Wuhan for his sake?

Demon Buddha struggled to hold back the urge to rush to him at once, but he could not stop the faint radiance he had been suppressing from flowing out.

“Tell him I am coming.”

Chapter 631: This Is What Happens When I Let Up Even a Little
-------------------------------------------------------------
As the dark clouds gathered, the surroundings quickly darkened.

The flashing lightning seemed to draw ever nearer, and then—

*Boom! Crash!*

The heavens spat out the thunder they had held back in rapid succession and poured down rain.

*Sshhhhhh!*

Geom Muyang stood by the window, watching the falling rain.

After Jin Hagun departed with the Demon Extermination Squad, Geom Muyang and the warriors of the Ji residence remained at the manor.

He had taken Baekryong Danju with him. After all, Geom Muyang couldn't continue to keep him in his custody.

Jin Hagun wouldn't be able to detain him for long either. If it became known that Baekryong Danju, of all people, had disappeared, the Martial Arts Alliance would be thrown into chaos. How, indeed, would he handle it?

If it were him, Geom Muyang would have broken through head-on. He would have taken the man before his father and made him confess everything. How to settle that long friendship would be entirely his father's burden.

Of course, Geom Muyang had said no such thing to Jin Hagun. He hadn't asked how he would handle it, either. He had entrusted him with rescuing Gwak Yeong's younger sibling; they were not in a relationship to join hands on other matters.

Then, Gwak Yeong spoke from behind.

"The rain makes me wish for a drink."

In truth, the rain made her desperately miss her younger sibling. Where were they, and how were they faring? Were they eating properly? Were they being beaten? How terrified must they be right now? This matter would torment her sibling like a lifelong nightmare.

What would become of everything now?

Gwak Yeong was inwardly afraid. Before the name Cheonae Geosa had emerged, she had believed that this man, and the Lord of the Demon Extermination Squad, would be able to rescue her sibling.

But Cheonae Geosa?

That he, who had spent his life sacrificing for the orthodox martial arts, was behind this?

Even though she had watched from the side as Geom Muyang and Jin Hagun uncovered the truth, she could not believe it. Could something be wrong? Could it be someone's plot and false accusation?

Even seeing it with her own eyes, she could not believe it—who else would believe such words? Cheonae Geosa was such a figure to the orthodox martial artists.

To think he forged weapons to earn money, and even resorted to kidnapping and threats.

She had thought her opponent was a man-eating fish that swarmed about tearing off flesh. But behind it was a giant whale that seemed capable of swallowing even the sea.

"Why didn't you hand me over to the Demon Extermination Squad?"

He could have sent her to Jin Hagun.

A promise to save her sibling was a promise, but she had committed the crime of betraying the Iron Forge artisan's honor and crafting counterfeit hidden weapons. If he had handed her over, he could have washed his hands of her and been done with it.

Geom Muyang, who had been looking out the window, turned to her.

"Was it a lie?"

"Pardon?"

"Your promise to make weapons for me."

From the gaze looking at her, Gwak Yeong could tell. Ah! This person truly intends to take me and use me.

"I had no idea your Iron Forge was suffering so badly from a labor shortage."

She answered with an unnecessary jest. Fortunately, without saying much else, Geom Muyang turned his gaze back to the window.

The words about making weapons for him had been sincere. She had even resolved to live as a demonic person.

But this was what truly frightened her. What if she could never meet her sibling again?

*Sshhhhhh.*

Quietly watching the rain pour down beyond Geom Muyang's shoulder, she spoke.

"Behind it..."

*With Cheonae Geosa behind it, can the Demon Extermination Squad truly rescue my sibling?*

Those words did not leave her mouth. The name Cheonae Geosa would not stick to her tongue.

*What if the Demon Extermination Squad fails to investigate him due to internal pressure?*

She was afraid that if she needlessly spouted such presumptuous words, it might actually come to pass.

As if understanding her inner thoughts, Geom Muyang answered without turning back.

"Nothing has changed."

Had she not known that he was the Great Young Master of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, she would have thought it mere bluster.

But now she knew those words were spoken in earnest. That he intended to face the opponent regardless of who stood behind it.

And those words were a great comfort to her.

Then Homyeong, the lord of the Ji residence, entered and reported.

"We've received word from the Tongcheong Pavilion regarding Hwaryeolcheong's whereabouts."

Homyeong conveyed the information to Geom Muyang.

"Hwaryeolcheong resides at the Musojang in Muhan. It is known that he travels throughout the Central Plains and is away for most of the year, but we have learned that he recently returned to Muhan."

"He is in Muhan?"

"Yes, it is said he returned a few days ago."

He was someone who was away for most of the year, yet he had returned to Muhan a few days ago? Precisely when he himself was in Muhan? Could this truly be a coincidence?

Geom Muyang thought not. This fact alone would prove that Hwaryeolcheong was involved in this matter.

"Send me all information regarding Hwaryeolcheong without omission. His personality, family relations, martial arts, even those who follow him."

"Yes, understood."

As Homyeong went outside, Gwak Yeong cautiously asked.

"Will you truly face him?"

Geom Muyang silently nodded.

"You know that many experts follow him, don't you?"

*Flash!*

The strike of lightning illuminated Geom Muyang's face brightly. His eyes resembled the lightning itself. This was a man who seemed as though he would silently push forward no matter who blocked his path. As he had said: nothing has changed.

After a moment, Geom Muyang spoke in a low voice.

"Such a person..."

Someone who had overcome difficult times together, who had shown many unsightly moments and thus knew his own shortcomings better than anyone, and whom he trusted more than anyone.

"I have one too."

* * *

Jin Hagun entered the Alliance Leader's hall.

"Alliance Leader, have you been well?"

As always, Jin Hagun observed the propriety of treating his grandfather as the Alliance Leader.

"Welcome."

Jin Paecheon's gaze toward his grandson was gentle. Lately, Jin Hagun was fulfilling his role more excellently than any Demon Extermination Squad Leader in history. He seemed much more mature, and his reputation among his subordinates was good.

"I heard you changed the Demon Extermination Squad's original schedule and returned."

"Yes, there were circumstances."

"What is it?"

Unexpected news was delivered by Jin Hagun.

"I received word from the Great Young Master of the Divine Cult."

When the Great Young Master was mentioned, Jin Paecheon's expression stiffened slightly. Although he had lost the succession to Geom Mugik, he knew the man was highly ambitious.

"It seems there were people who forged the Divine Cult's hidden weapons and distributed them in the Central Plains. He is pursuing the mastermind and has requested our cooperation."

Jin Paecheon made a puzzled expression. The Heavenly Demon Divine Cult had never requested cooperation from them.

"For what matter?"

"Our Iron Forge artisan is involved."

Jin Hagun reported that Gwak Yeong was involved and that it was because her younger sibling had been taken hostage.

"Because of this matter, the Great Young Master has come to Muhan."

Jin Paecheon's expression completely stiffened. The Great Young Master of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult had entered Muhan without his permission? That was unacceptable to Jin Paecheon.

"Anyway, those people go too far the moment you loosen the reins."

Jin Paecheon revealed his displeasure toward the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

The rudeness of not informing him in advance was bad enough, but he was more furious that the Martial Arts Alliance's intelligence network had lost track of Geom Muyang's movements.

"You sent them back, I presume?"

"No. They are still in Muhan."

Jin Paecheon's rebuke flew toward his grandson.

"You should have sent them back!"

"My apologies."

Jin Hagun knew. It was not that he was displeased with him, but that he was treating him with strictness. Because the day to make him successor was drawing near. His grandfather wished for him not to have a single flaw as successor.

Jin Paecheon silently observed Jin Hagun for a moment, then rose from his seat of honor and came down.

Approaching his grandson, he called him gently.

"Hagun."

Jin Hagun could tell. In this moment, it was not the Alliance Leader looking at the Demon Extermination Squad Leader, but a grandfather addressing his grandson.

"Yes, Grandfather."

"I well understand your heart that trusts the young cult leader."

Even he in his old age had been so captivated by the man called Geom Mugik—how could young Jin Hagun remain calm?

That was why there were words he must impress upon him. Words he needed to repeat going forward.

Words that must be emphasized all the more because he knew Geom Mugik was a good person.

"No matter how close you become in life, no matter how deep your relationship grows, you must never forget for a single moment that he is a demonic person."

No matter how good a person he was, everyone around him was a demonic person.

Jin Hagun well understood the heart with which his grandfather spoke.

"Yes, I will keep it in mind."

He had thought that because the man was Geom Mugik's older brother, he could overlook it to that extent.

"There is one more thing to report."

In truth, this report was the core purpose of today's visit.

"I am investigating Baekryong Danju in connection with this matter."

"Baekryong Danju? Why?"

"He is one of the masterminds behind this incident."

Jin Paecheon thought it preposterous.

"Do you have evidence?"

"Yes. The one who rented the warehouse where the counterfeit hidden weapons were made is Baekryong Danju. Furthermore, we have uncovered circumstances indicating yet another person exists behind him."

He did not say directly who it was.

Because Jin Paecheon had to hear directly from Baekryong Danju's own mouth that Hwaryeolcheong was behind it.

"I must see him myself."

"I have him waiting outside."

"Bring him in."

Though you may be confused and find it hard to believe at first, Grandfather is the only one who can resolve this matter.

Because Grandfather was the only one capable of handling Cheonae Geosa.

Shortly after, the Demon Extermination Squad warriors brought Baekryong Danju in and then left.

Jin Hagun had already finished speaking with Baekryong Danju earlier. He had promised that if he told the Alliance Leader all the facts as they were, not only would he be spared execution, but his sentence would be reduced to the minimum as a reward for revealing the truth.

In truth, watching Baekryong Danju accept the promise, Jin Hagun had thought:

'So he truly is the mastermind.'

It seemed he had been doubting that fact in one corner of his heart. No, even in this moment, he couldn't fully shake off that doubt. Was he really behind it? He was someone Jin Hagun had respected so much since childhood.

Jin Paecheon looked down at him and spoke.

"Raise your head."

Baekryong Danju raised his head. He was trembling in fear.

"What is going on? Were you truly involved in this matter?"

Jin Paecheon asked calmly, but his gaze was as cold as frost.

Baekryong Danju opened his mouth with a trembling voice.

"This matter is..."

Unable to continue, he hung his head deeply.

"I am truly sorry, Alliance Leader."

Perhaps too ashamed, he couldn't confess and merely sighed.

Jin Hagun spoke to him.

"It is all over, so confess quickly."

Eventually, Baekryong Danju revealed the truth.

"This was ordered by Cheonae Geosa."

Though it was a shocking statement, Jin Paecheon was not surprised at all. Instead, he calmly asked again.

"Who ordered it?"

"Cheonae Geosa ordered it."

At that very moment.

*Flash!*

Jin Paecheon's figure had already twisted and grasped Baekryong Danju by the throat.

"How dare you play tricks!"

Jin Hagun naturally thought he was angry because Cheonae Geosa had been mentioned.

But that was not it.

Jin Paecheon let out a roar like a martial shout before him.

"Haaaaaaaap!"

A violent wind lashed at Baekryong Danju as if a typhoon were blowing.

It was the Lion's Roar imbued with the Alliance Leader's majestic inner power.

In that moment when a tremendous momentum that seemed ready to blow him away shook Baekryong Danju's face and body!

Jin Hagun saw.

Baekryong Danju's eyes rolled back before returning to normal. It was as if the black pupils had disappeared behind the eyeballs and then reappeared.

"Haaaaaaaap!"

Under the greater Lion's Roar, Baekryong Danju's pupils rolled back again. This time they did not return; only the whites of his eyes were visible.

"Uuuuuugh."

A scream flowed out, and simultaneously, black liquid trickled from his two eyes. It was as if the vanished black pupils had turned to liquid and flowed out. Baekryong Danju seemed unaware that such a thing was happening to him.

The next moment.

"...It is Cheonae Geosa's doing."

The slowly spoken words were not Baekryong Danju's voice. Having spoken in an eerie voice as if possessed by a monster,

Baekryong Danju's body trembled violently once, and then he collapsed with a thud.

Jin Paecheon looked down at him with complicated feelings.

"What has happened here?"

When Jin Hagun examined him, he was already dead. Infusing true energy to inspect his body's interior, he found the blood vessels severed strand by strand.

Since his inner power had been suppressed, it was not suicide.

'Had a sorcery been placed that severed blood vessels upon confession?'

But hadn't he already confessed under Geom Muyang's torture earlier? Why hadn't they severed then?

Jin Paecheon spoke in a low voice.

"It is the Mind Control Technique."

Jin Hagun was startled. The Mind Control Technique was a sorcery that controlled people's minds at will.

His grandfather had realized he was under the Mind Control Technique and destroyed it with the Lion's Roar.

"When the Mind Control Technique is forcibly released, all blood vessels are severed, causing death."

Why on earth?

If they had placed the Mind Control Technique, why make him confess about Cheonae Geosa? Rather, they should have hidden the fact that he was the mastermind...

In that moment, Jin Hagun flinched in shock, and a chill ran down his spine.

'No!'

Because Grandfather would think someone had placed the Mind Control Technique to frame Cheonae Geosa!

Because he had been revealed, he was no longer the culprit.

And naturally, Jin Paecheon's suspicion turned toward one person.

"Where is the Great Young Master right now?"

Chapter 632: Rather Than the Scheming Geom Woojin
-------------------------------------------------
“Do you suspect the Eldest Young Master?”

At Jin Hagun’s question, Jin Paecheon asked in return.

“Then are you suspecting Layman Hwa?”

A taut tension flowed between the two of them.

Even until he came here, Jin Hagun had harbored a faint, surely not, in one corner of his heart....

But the instant he heard those words from his grandfather, he knew for certain.

‘Cheonae Geosa planted it in him with Mind Control Art so he would say his own name.’

If one implanted Mind Control Art in a subordinate one commanded, would one not make him give someone else’s name even under torture?

Yet Cheonae Geosa had struck at the blind spot of that common sense; by driving suspicion upon himself as the mastermind, he had perfectly extricated himself from being the mastermind.

This choice was a method possible precisely because the target was his grandfather. His grandfather had known Cheonae Geosa since his youth. Since then, the friendship between the two had continued to this day.

It was probably something like this.

Suppose he continued building his friendship with Geom Mugeuk from now on, and several decades passed.

Then someone came and said Geom Mugeuk was the mastermind behind evil? Have these bastards lost their minds?

That must have been exactly how his grandfather felt now.

And the reason Cheonae Geosa had made this choice was clear.

“I asked where the Eldest Young Master is.”

Since such a thing had happened now, when the Eldest Young Master was in Wuhan, there was no choice but to believe it had naturally been plotted by the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

Knowing that defending Geom Muyang in this situation would only further inflame his grandfather’s anger, Jin Hagun answered obediently.

“He is at a manor not far from here.”

The Martial Alliance Leader could not go personally, nor could he summon him to the Alliance Leader’s Hall.

“Bring him to Cheongunrim.”

Jin Hagun obediently followed his grandfather’s words.

“Yes, understood.”

Jin Hagun left the Alliance Leader’s Hall.

Shaaaaaaa.

When Jin Hagun came out of the Alliance Leader’s Hall, the rain was still pouring down. After telling a warrior of the Alliance Leader’s Hall to have a carriage prepared, he stood for a moment and watched the falling rain.

He should have handled this matter with his own hands, but he had not expected the White Dragon Unit Leader to die like that. Now it would be even more difficult to reveal that Cheonae Geosa was behind it. Even if other evidence came out, his grandfather would think it had been fabricated.

Cheonae Geosa’s face came to mind. When he was young, he had followed him around, calling him Grandfather. Unlike his strict grandfather, the man had treated him and Jin Haryeong warmly. Whenever he came to see them, he would bring rare toys from all over the Central Plains.

And that man was behind the conspiracy?

Jin Hagun lifted his head and looked up at the sky. Thunder was flashing within the pitch-black storm clouds.

* * *

Geom Muyang stood in the courtyard of the manor, getting drenched by the rain as he watched the carriage arrive.

When the carriage door opened, Jin Hagun was inside. From the fact that he had come personally to find him, Geom Muyang sensed that something had gone wrong with taking away the White Dragon Unit Leader.

“The Alliance Leader wishes to see you.”

As Geom Muyang immediately walked toward the carriage, Homyeong followed after him. He said, We will escort you, but Geom Muyang declined.

“You all wait here.”

How could Homyeong not know? Now that the Martial Alliance Leader himself had stepped forward, all of them would die to a single strike of his sword.

But apart from that, sending Geom Muyang alone made him worried and uneasy. If something happened to him, all of them would have to offer up their necks. It would be better to die protecting him instead.

He sent another look saying he would accompany him, but Geom Muyang firmly shook his head.

“Please be careful.”

Leaving behind Homyeong and the warriors of the Earth Unit, and Gwak Yeong, who stood at the very back watching with concern, Geom Muyang climbed into the carriage.

Once he got in, the carriage set off.

When the carriage started moving, Jin Hagun told him what had happened in the Alliance Leader’s Hall. In the current situation, there could not be even the slightest misunderstanding, so he judged that he must not hide anything from him.

“The White Dragon Unit Leader was under Mind Control Art.”

Geom Muyang flinched. Even though he had tortured the man himself, he had not known that fact. If it was a Mind Control Art that could evade even his eyes, then it had to be a sorcery planted by someone stronger than himself.

If so, it was not a sorcery cast by Cheonae Geosa. Having seen him for so many years, there was no way the Martial Alliance Leader would have failed to notice that he possessed such sorcery.

“Whom did he name?”

“He said it was Cheonae Geosa after all.”

Geom Muyang was not surprised in the least. He had already confirmed several times just how meticulous they were.

“Then he must be dead.”

“How do you know?”

“Because that is how I would have dealt with it.”

Jin Hagun could tell. Geom Muyang, too, had grasped their intent.

“There is no way the Alliance Leader did not see through what we can see. It’s just....”

He simply does not want to believe it.

Jin Hagun could not bring himself to continue those words with his own mouth.

Geom Muyang merely nodded without saying anything.

Contrary to Jin Hagun’s thoughts, he considered it possible that the Alliance Leader had not seen through it. Or perhaps he was thinking something entirely different.

The one who truly did not want to believe it might be Jin Hagun. The fact that his grandfather could make a wrong judgment.

But Geom Muyang did not reveal such thoughts. It was only because his interests were distant that he could view it objectively, like offering advice from the sidelines. If it became one’s own affair, who could guarantee anything?

Thus the carriage carrying the two of them arrived at the bamboo grove.

The guards of the Alliance Leader’s Hall were all standing at the entrance. It seemed the Alliance Leader had forbidden anyone from entering.

Geom Muyang and Jin Hagun got down from the carriage and moved toward the grove.

Once they entered the grove, the rain became a fine drizzle.

The two walked through the rain. It had been a very long time since either Geom Muyang or Jin Hagun had been rained on like this.

There was a small pavilion in the middle of the bamboo grove.

Jin Paecheon stood in the pavilion with his hands clasped behind his back. He did not conceal his presence.

“On rainy days, I sometimes come here.”

Geom Muyang slowly moved his steps and ascended into the pavilion.

Jin Hagun did not go up with him, but waited below.

Geom Muyang, having stepped into the pavilion, greeted him with respectful courtesy.

“I greet the Alliance Leader.”

Jin Paecheon, who had been looking far into the distance, slowly turned around.

“Eldest Young Master, it has been a long time.”

His gaze and attitude toward Geom Muyang were calm and courteous. When he had ordered him brought here, he had seemed ready to roar at him at once, but his manner now was entirely different.

That was why Jin Hagun, standing below the pavilion, felt relieved on one hand and more tense on the other. He did not know when or how his grandfather’s rage might erupt.

“What do you think of the scenery?”

“It is beautiful.”

Indeed, the view from the pavilion was beautiful. The mist flowing through the thinned rain was lovely and dreamlike.

There, only the sound of rain could be heard, as if every sound in the world had vanished.

“Is the Cult Leader well?”

“He has entered closed-door cultivation.”

Since the Martial Alliance would already know that fact, Geom Muyang answered honestly.

“To tell the truth, I was surprised when I first heard the news.”

At their level, closed-door cultivation was not something that happened often. Because of that, the Alliance had even convened an emergency meeting. Was he truly in closed-door cultivation, or was he trying to pull some other trick?

But amid the meeting, as various reports and opinions were exchanged, Jin Paecheon was picturing Geom Ujin in closed-door cultivation.

He knew instinctively. The Geom Ujin he knew was not someone who would use deceit over something like this. He could be even more certain because he had met him at the last tripartite conference.

A Geom Ujin who did not play tricks was more frightening than a Geom Ujin who did.

That was why, though he did not show it outwardly, he was inwardly on edge. After all, the martial realm of none other than the Heavenly Demon was changing.

It was amid such circumstances that this incident had occurred.

“My younger brother is serving as acting Cult Leader.”

When Geom Mugeuk was mentioned, a smile formed at the corners of Jin Paecheon’s mouth. The only demon cultist who made him smile no matter the situation. Yes, if not for Geom Mugeuk, this meeting would not have existed in the first place.

“The Young Cult Leader is someone who would do well no matter what task he is entrusted with.”

Geom Muyang nodded as if in agreement.

Was it because they had thought of Geom Mugeuk?

Like the mist flowing among the bamboo, silence flowed between the two of them as well.

How much time passed like that?

Jin Paecheon abruptly said,

“It was unexpected.”

Geom Muyang looked at him. Now Jin Paecheon also gazed straight at him and said,

“That you came all the way to Wuhan.”

It was a natural question. The forgery of the Golden Dragon Hidden Weapons was certainly a serious matter. But even so, it was not enough for the Eldest Young Master to come all the way to Wuhan.

“There were circumstances that required it.”

“Can you tell me what they were?”

When Geom Muyang gave no answer, Jin Paecheon’s gaze turned slightly cold. Even though he sensed that change in his eyes, Geom Muyang gave no answer.

“You resemble your father more than the Young Cult Leader does.”

Looking at him called Geom Ujin to mind.

Well, if it had been Geom Mugeuk, he would already have been chattering away, telling him, There were this and that circumstances, and explaining everything.

Geom Muyang understood what those words meant. And he also felt that what was needed at this moment was not his own bluntness, but his younger brother’s kindness.

If it had truly been another situation, or another opponent, he would never have said it.

“That hidden weapon nearly killed my younger brother.”

At Geom Muyang’s answer, Jin Paecheon’s eyes widened.

Jin Hagun, who had been below the pavilion, raised his head and looked up.

Only now could he know for certain. Why Geom Muyang had said he would see this through to the end.

If he himself had nearly killed Jin Haryeong? Yes, even he would have to see it through. He, too, would have chased it all the way to the main altar of the Demon Cult.

“I merely tracked down the one who made the forged hidden weapons, and yet here I am meeting the Alliance Leader like this.”

It meant that whoever they were, they had paved the road well. Jin Paecheon sensed the barb in those words.

“Have you heard whom the White Dragon Unit Leader mentioned?”

“Yes.”

“What do you think?”

“I do not know that man.”

Geom Muyang looked straight at Jin Paecheon and said,

“The answer is surely known to the Alliance Leader.”

For an instant, Jin Paecheon’s expression twitched. A tremendous aura flowed out from his body.

“What does that mean?”

“Do you not already know?”

An overwhelming pressure began to whirl around them.

Dark clouds gathered, and the world grew dim. Jin Paecheon’s aura was a typhoon encountered on a dark sea.

In the rough waves that swept in an instant, Geom Muyang was lifted and thrown down again and again. A fear that burrowed into the abyss stirred even the depths of a person’s heart.

Originally, Jin Paecheon was a man suited to such an aura. Tyrannical and powerful. It was only that, having lived for so long as the Martial Alliance Leader, he had not often had opportunities to reveal that force.

Even within the storm, Geom Muyang stood firm against it. Then Jin Paecheon’s aura grew even stronger.

Below the pavilion, Jin Hagun’s heart grew urgent. It was his enraged grandfather, and Geom Muyang trying not to break.

If it were Geom Mugeuk, how would he handle this?

Thinking of him, he considered this and that, but he was not Geom Mugeuk. If he could not do as Geom Mugeuk would, then it would be better to remain silent.

Yes, let’s trust Grandfather. Though he is angry now, he is not someone who handles matters according to emotion.

In this matter, he had to play the most important role. He was the one who knew his grandfather best, and he was also the one who had to serve as the bridge with the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

‘Yes, from now on, I must not hold preconceived notions. I must look properly at each and every person.’

Those words Geom Mugeuk had repeated again and again had become the most necessary thing in his life at this moment.

Jin Paecheon’s surging aura gradually subsided, and the surroundings became peaceful once more.

He had pushed him right to the limit before internal injuries would occur, but Geom Muyang endured it there without moving a single step.

After quietly looking at him, Jin Paecheon said calmly,

“Leave Wuhan.”

If it were some other region, perhaps not, but in Wuhan it was a demand he could make well enough.

But Geom Muyang did not accept it.

“I cannot.”

Jin Paecheon spoke amicably.

“This matter is ours. Once we find the answer, I will send word to you.”

“I am sorry.”

You will never find that answer alone.

Geom Muyang did not back down. This was a promise to none other than Jin Paecheon. If he said he would leave and then did not, that would be an even greater insult to him.

“You people really are!”

Who in this murim would dare defy his words?

Over Geom Muyang’s face, the image of Geom Ujin overlapped. Since he was that man’s son, how formidable must his stubbornness be?

At that moment, Jin Paecheon’s gaze slowly turned toward the distant mist. The look in his eyes changed.

Someone was there, far off in the mist.

The fact that he could only sense their qi after they had approached this close was enough to show just how outstanding the other party’s martial arts were.

“Stop eavesdropping and come out.”

Then someone began walking slowly toward them.

“Have you not experienced it yourself over the past several decades, Alliance Leader?”

Beyond the white mist, a golden radiance began to spread.

“Those Demon Cult bastards truly do not listen.”

Chapter 633: The Seat You Must Sit In From Now On
-------------------------------------------------
Demon Buddha emerged through the golden mist.

How could such a small figure exude such an overwhelming presence?

From beneath the pavilion, he looked up and respectfully pressed his palms together.

“Have you been well, Alliance Leader?”

Jin Paecheon also received Demon Buddha with due courtesy.

“It has been a long time.”

Demon Buddha’s appearance was unexpected. And yet, where the First Young Master was, it was not so strange to find Demon Buddha there as well. Jin Paecheon knew very well what kind of relationship the two shared.

After exchanging greetings with Jin Paecheon, Demon Buddha looked at Geom Muyang beside him.

Their gazes met in midair.

“You have come?”

“Yes, I have come.”

Within that brief greeting lay deep feelings for one another.

In truth, Geom Muyang had not been able to decide what to do after the Alliance Leader told him to leave Wuhan. Had it been anyone else, he would have simply ignored the words, but he could not flatly reject the words of the Martial Alliance Leader.

In that neither-here-nor-there situation, the sight of Demon Buddha made him feel as though his radiance had brightly illuminated the predicament.

Demon Buddha slowly moved his feet and ascended into the pavilion. Before coming up, he also exchanged greetings with Jin Hagun.

“I greet the Demon Venerable.”

“Even from afar, I have heard much of Squad Leader Jin’s chivalrous deeds. You are truly admirable at such a young age.”

Jin Hagun, too, was inwardly glad at his appearance. The conflict that had been on the verge of exploding just moments ago had been defused by his arrival.

“This is the liquor the Alliance Leader enjoys.”

Entrusting the liquor he had brought to Jin Hagun, Demon Buddha asked Jin Paecheon atop the pavilion.

“Your tastes have not changed in the meantime, have they?”

Demon Buddha never came empty-handed when meeting him. Since long ago, whenever the Martial Alliance held an event, he had also been the only Demon Venerable who never failed to send a gift.

Of course, gifts sent from the Demon Cult were, in principle, to be discarded, and Demon Buddha could not have been unaware of that. Yet he had always sent them without fail.

But after the First Young Master was pushed out of the succession, Jin Paecheon had thought he would no longer receive his gifts. Today, however, he had received one again.

Having come up onto the pavilion, Demon Buddha looked around and marveled.

“The scenery seen from above is different from when viewed below.”

The rain had already stopped, and the mist had begun to thin. Beyond it, tall, straight bamboo revealed themselves.

He tried to create a pleasant atmosphere with repeated admiration, but Jin Paecheon’s reaction was cold.

“I did not know even you were in Wuhan.”

Because it was Demon Buddha, his response was at least this mild. The First Young Master’s presence was problem enough, but a Demon Venerable had come to Wuhan without prior notice? This was something worthy of a formal protest to the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

“I, too, had urgent circumstances.”

“And what circumstances did the Demon Venerable have?”

Then astonishing words flowed from Demon Buddha’s mouth.

“There was information that someone had the audacity to target our cult’s First Young Master.”

Geom Muyang was not one to show his emotions easily, but even he looked surprised at those words. It was something he himself had not known.

“Who was it?”

Jin Paecheon naturally expected it to be someone from the unorthodox factions, or someone who bore a grudge against the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

“It was Byeoksan Sword.”

The moment he heard that name, Jin Paecheon felt a heavy thud in his chest.

Byeoksan Sword was one of the masters who represented the righteous martial world. For someone like him to have tried to kill the First Young Master of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult was an incident far greater than the First Young Master’s coming to Wuhan.

Demon Buddha raised his voice as he radiated golden brilliance.

“That madman dared target whom!”

Demon Buddha was truly furious. Representing the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, he was conveying that anger directly to the Alliance Leader.

“An attempt to harm the First Young Master is a challenge to and an attack on our cult!”

In that moment, Geom Muyang understood. He understood why Demon Buddha had appeared before him so quickly.

‘Demon Buddha had already come to Wuhan to save me.’

Because it was none other than Demon Buddha, Geom Muyang’s heart swelled. Even though he had behaved so poorly in the past, Demon Buddha was still protecting him without change.

“Fortunately, I arrived before he could carry it out.”

Since he could not truly vent his anger against the Martial Alliance Leader, Demon Buddha’s radiance soon faded.

Demon Buddha’s dignified attitude seemed to be asking this:

Even now, will you question me for coming to Wuhan?

The initiative in the conversation, which until just earlier had been held by Jin Paecheon as he pressed them to leave Wuhan, now passed to Demon Buddha.

“Alliance Leader, please do not worry too much. I have not made it known outside.”

If it had been any ordinary matter, Jin Paecheon would not have asked like this.

“Was it truly him?”

He could not believe that someone like Byeoksan Sword had done such a thing without thinking of the consequences. He would sooner have believed it if it had been Hanwolgaek.

“This was my third meeting with that man.”

It meant there was no possibility he had mistaken him for someone else.

Before Jin Paecheon could ask, Demon Buddha even revealed the result of the matter. Demon Buddha was a man who knew how to guide a conversation smoothly.

“At our fourth meeting, we decided to share a drink.”

He had phrased it gently, but Jin Paecheon understood. It meant he had subdued him with his skill. The two of them would not have simply traded words and ended it there.

Relief flashed across Jin Paecheon’s face at the words that Byeoksan Sword was alive. At the same time, he felt uneasy.

‘Are you saying Byeoksan Sword lost to this man?’

Jin Paecheon quietly studied Demon Buddha. He felt different from the last time he had seen him. There was a certain ease about him. Just as much as that ease, his skill must have grown as well.

In that instant, Geom Ujin, who should be in secluded cultivation, came to mind.

‘The Demon Cult is growing stronger.’

It was a moment when he once again felt that uncomfortable truth.

In any case, it was something he could confirm through Byeoksan Sword. Demon Buddha would not be lying about it.

‘Why did Byeoksan Sword try to kill the Divine Cult’s First Young Master? Could it be? At someone’s request?’

Someone could indeed make a request of Byeoksan Sword. But that request could not be, Please kill the First Young Master of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

Unless it was his own request.

“Did you hear why he did it?”

“He did not say, but it seemed he had received someone’s request.”

Jin Paecheon sounded Demon Buddha out.

“Is there anyone you suspect?”

Demon Buddha shook his head as if he did not know. He already knew through Tongcheongak that Cheonae Geosa was behind it, but he did not so much as utter Cheonae Geosa’s name.

He only pointed out the nature of the force behind it.

“Have those who try to drive a wedge between our cult and the Martial Alliance not existed since time immemorial?”

Whoever it was, he emphasized that they were trying to divide the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult and the Martial Alliance.

Demon Buddha did not mention Hanwolgaek. If he were to explain that he had sent him back, he would also have to reveal that the Poison King was in Wuhan.

“That is why I could not contact you in advance. I ask that you understand with your generous heart.”

“I can understand sufficiently.”

“Thank you.”

Demon Buddha looked up at the sky. The sun that had been hiding behind the dark clouds was showing itself.

“The rain has stopped as well, so we will be on our way.”

At Demon Buddha’s farewell, Geom Muyang also said goodbye.

“Then I will see you again, Alliance Leader.”

“Take care on your way.”

Leave Wuhan, or let us meet again—neither side exchanged such words. What came after would have to be handled with careful judgment.

As he descended from the pavilion, Geom Muyang’s eyes met Jin Hagun’s.

Geom Muyang gave him a slight nod, and Jin Hagun likewise nodded slightly in response. In that way, they exchanged their resolve to cooperate well and handle the matter somehow.

* * *

After leaving the pavilion, Geom Muyang and Demon Buddha walked side by side through the bamboo grove and conversed.

He had thought there would be no reason for them to meet outside like this again. To think a day would come when they would walk side by side like this before the main headquarters of the Martial Alliance.

The first to break the silence was Geom Muyang.

“The one behind it is definitely Cheonae Geosa.”

At Geom Muyang’s words, Demon Buddha nodded.

“I know.”

That was precisely why this opponent could be called the most difficult of all the enemies they had faced so far.

“As long as the Alliance Leader trusts him, we cannot carelessly touch Cheonae Geosa.”

His friendship with the Alliance Leader was one thing, but the respect he commanded from the righteous factions was another problem. The moment they mishandled Cheonae Geosa, those bastards would expand it into a conflict between the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult and the Martial Alliance.

When Geom Muyang stopped walking, Demon Buddha stopped as well.

The words Geom Muyang wanted to say rose all the way to his throat, then sank back down. It was the first time he had ever said them to anyone.

He had never said them to his father, nor had he said them to Geom Mugeuk.

He said those words to Demon Buddha.

“Please help me.”

How could Demon Buddha not know how difficult those words had been to say, and how precious they were?

Demon Buddha’s eyes trembled, and the radiance flowing from his body deepened. To sincerity, he answered with even greater sincerity.

“I shall devote this body entirely and serve you with loyalty.”

Wrapped in that bright radiance, Geom Muyang felt regret.

If their relationship had been like this during the succession struggle? If he had treated Demon Buddha with such sincerity? Would the outcome of that struggle have been different?

* * *

Jin Paecheon and Jin Hagun stood side by side in the pavilion, watching the backs of Geom Muyang and Demon Buddha as they moved away.

“What do you think?”

Jin Hagun sensed that depending on how this matter concluded, it would have a great impact on the selection of his own successor as well.

Since this entire process could be his grandfather’s test, he had to judge and answer each word with caution.

“It must be one of three things. The Divine Cult is lying, Cheonae Geosa is truly the mastermind, or someone is sowing discord to make us believe that.”

It was not the answer Jin Paecheon wanted.

“I asked for your thoughts.”

“I believe at least one thing is certain.”

He knew his grandfather was worried that he might suffer a setback because he trusted Geom Mugeuk too much.

“At the very least, I do not think the Divine Cult is lying.”

As expected, worry flashed across Jin Paecheon’s face. In the past, he might have read the room and given the answer his grandfather wanted to hear.

But now, he had changed. He knew that an answer meant to evade the moment was like building a bridge of sand toward the other person.

Even if it worked for the time being, it would collapse one day. It would collapse after it had been more fully completed, when he had drawn closer to the other person, so the other person’s disappointment would be even greater, and he himself would be buried in a deeper pit of sand.

“You trust the First Young Master that much?”

There was certainly a part of him that trusted Geom Muyang. He could tell instinctively that he had spoken the truth. But there was an even more certain reason than that.

“No.”

“Then why?”

It was an answer that would worry his grandfather even more.

“The one I trust is the Young Cult Leader.”

Jin Hagun conveyed his honest feelings to his grandfather.

“Since the Young Cult Leader is serving as acting cult leader, the current master of the Divine Cult is the Young Cult Leader.”

Jin Paecheon nodded. All decision-making authority would rest with him.

“He may have hidden ambitions.”

Jin Paecheon had expected him to speak in his defense and say that was not so, but his grandson readily acknowledged it.

“He is such an intelligent person that he may indeed.”

“Then why do you say you trust him?”

Jin Hagun did not know Geom Mugeuk completely, but there was at least one thing he was certain of.

“Even if he were to devise a scheme, he is not the sort of person who would proceed while putting his older brother in danger.”

Right now, the First Young Master was isolated in Wuhan and in a dangerous situation. He did not think Geom Mugeuk would have driven his own brother into such circumstances.

“If he were that kind of person, the First Young Master would already have died during the succession struggle.”

Jin Paecheon could not refute those words. The Geom Mugeuk he had seen was no different from what his grandson said.

“I believe we must fight while keeping every possibility open.”

He braced himself for his grandfather’s rebuke. Saying they should keep every possibility open also meant saying they should suspect Cheonae Geosa.

But Jin Paecheon did not become angry. Rather, he looked at his grandson with gentle eyes.

“Hagun.”

“Yes, Grandfather.”

“Are you worried about this old man?”

“No.”

Though he said that, he was clearly worried. Worried that long friendship might cloud his judgment. Worried even about the wound his grandfather would suffer if the friendship he had believed in shattered.

Then unexpected words flowed from Jin Paecheon’s mouth.

“In my heart, I have already killed him.”

“What?”

Jin Hagun’s eyes widened, unable to understand what he meant.

“I have driven a sword into that man’s heart. I have already stood before his grave.”

Jin Hagun understood. That man meant Hwayulcheong. He could tell from the deep and bitter look in his grandfather’s eyes.

‘Ah! Grandfather has already opened every possibility!’

He had forgotten. His grandfather had lived his whole life upholding chivalry, something difficult to practice even once, and had long observed every manner of human being from the position of Martial Alliance Leader.

“I desperately hope it is not so… but I have already accepted the possibility that a friend I have known for decades may betray me.”

Because he had staked his entire life on this position.

“Because I am the Martial Alliance Leader.”

He was telling his grandson words he had never once told him before.

“The seat you must sit in from now on is such a seat.”

Jin Hagun lowered his head with an ashamed expression.

“I am sorry, Grandfather.”

He had told himself to see properly, to look straight, but he still had not been seeing his grandfather properly. To see a person truly was this difficult.

On the contrary, Jin Paecheon spoke in an even gentler tone.

“What I have felt while occupying this seat for a long time is that our righteous faction cannot chase the Demon Cult or the unorthodox factions at the same pace. Even when we tried to run ahead first and lead them, we were always several steps behind. We were busy cleaning up after them and chasing in their wake. And yet, do you know why we have not collapsed before their cruel and tenacious desires?”

“Why?”

Jin Paecheon looked up at the blue sky from which the dark clouds had receded. Filling both his eyes with that blue sky, he spoke with strength.

“Because we are right.”

Jin Hagun’s heart pounded. His grandfather had lived his entire life with this single belief. And in one sentence, he had told him the path he had to walk.

You, too, must walk the righteous path.

Jin Paecheon’s gaze, which had held the whole sky, turned toward his grandson.

“So trust the Young Cult Leader only after this belief.”

Chapter 634: A Great Evil Seems to Have Awakened
------------------------------------------------
There are moments in human relationships when one reaches a turning point.

Usually, it is only after the fact that one realizes, Ah, so that was it. But today, Jin Ha-gun could feel it with certainty.

Between his grandfather and himself, a bridge was beginning to be built—not of sand, but of sturdy wood.

“I will trust the Young Cult Leader fifth, not second.”

His grandfather had shown him the path he needed to take, so he ought to repay him in kind.

“First, I will trust in the belief that we are right. Next, I will trust you, Grandfather. After that, I will trust my subordinates in the Demon Extermination Squad, and after that, I will trust the Murim Alliance martial artists who are silently doing their duties even at this very moment. Only after trusting all of them will I trust the Young Cult Leader.”

Jin Paecheon smiled in satisfaction. This grandson of his had seemed nothing but immature, yet before he knew it, he had grown so reliable.

“Let us return.”

“Yes, Grandfather.”

The two of them descended from the pavilion and made their way toward the entrance of the bamboo grove.

Jin Ha-gun asked cautiously. Depending on the answer to this question, the fates of many people would change.

“What do you intend to do now?”

Instead of answering, Jin Paecheon asked for his grandson’s opinion.

“What do you think?”

Through this opportunity, he wanted to see how his grandson would handle such a matter. For he felt that the time had come.

“As you saw earlier, the First Young Master has no intention of backing down like this. In the end, those behind him will once again try to divide our Alliance and the Divine Cult. Before that happens…”

The reason he could not immediately continue was that it might be something his grandfather would find uncomfortable.

“I believe we must meet Cheonae Geosa.”

They would have to find out first whether he truly was the one behind it all, or whether he was being framed through someone else’s scheme.

“And when you meet him?”

“If Cheonae Geosa is indeed behind this, then he will know he is under suspicion.”

After all, he had used the Mind-Control Art to arrange for the White Dragon Corps Leader to speak his name.

“If we do not go to him and confirm the matter in this situation, he will think we suspect him.”

If they trusted him, he would expect the Alliance Leader to come to him and tell him that such a thing had happened.

“Whether the Geosa is innocent or not, in both cases, the fact remains that we must seek him out first. We must move first.”

Jin Paecheon nodded silently and continued walking, lost in thought.

Meanwhile, the two arrived at the entrance.

As Jin Paecheon climbed into the carriage waiting there, he gave an unexpected order.

“You go meet him.”

It meant he was entrusting the handling of this matter to his grandson.

Jin Ha-gun had a premonition. This test might be the final test for him to become the successor.

“Yes, I will do so.”

Now that he knew his grandfather’s true feelings, things were completely different from before. He would be able to handle the matter objectively, without worrying about the relationship between the two elders.

The carriage carrying Jin Paecheon departed at once.

Once the carriage left with the Alliance Leader’s guards, the Demon Extermination Squad martial artists gathered around Jin Ha-gun, who remained alone.

Because they had been moving together with Jin Ha-gun, they knew better than anyone that the current situation was far from ordinary.

“We’re going to Musojang.”

* * *

Jin Ha-gun stood at the entrance of Musojang and looked up at the signboard.

Musojang.

It was a name given by the martial artists of Wuhan in honor of Cheonae Geosa, who had lived his entire life in non-possession.

Jin Ha-gun suddenly recalled that the “muso” in muso-bulwi, meaning absolute power to do anything, used the same characters.

Whether the life he desired was one of non-possession, or one in which he sought the power to do as he pleased, would soon be known.

When a martial artist of the Demon Extermination Squad knocked on the door, Chief Steward Ju appeared from inside.

Recognizing Jin Ha-gun, he greeted him respectfully.

“Welcome, Squad Leader.”

Chief Steward Ju had served Cheonae Geosa for many years, and despite his outstanding martial skills, he had taken charge of the household affairs of Musojang. Out of respect for Cheonae Geosa, he had devoted his life to him.

Jin Ha-gun looked past him and asked,

“Has something happened?”

Beyond Chief Steward Ju, the courtyard was visible, and dozens of martial artists could be seen standing there. Even at a glance, the atmosphere felt unusual.

At that, Chief Steward Ju asked with a strange look in his eyes,

“Did you not come after hearing the news?”

He seemed to believe Jin Ha-gun had already known and come.

Jin Ha-gun did not bother to say otherwise. He did not know what had happened, but he would find out once he went in.

“Please, come inside.”

Chief Steward Ju guided Jin Ha-gun in. The Demon Extermination Squad martial artists followed behind him.

Those in the courtyard were all martial artists from sects in Wuhan.

They recognized Jin Ha-gun and greeted him respectfully with cupped fists. He was the leading candidate to become the next Murim Alliance Leader, and the Demon Extermination Squad he commanded was made up of the elite of the Murim Alliance.

The Demon Extermination Squad lined up between them and formed a passage.

Jin Ha-gun and Chief Steward Ju walked along the path made by the Demon Extermination Squad and went inside.

As he walked, Jin Ha-gun observed the martial artists in the courtyard. All of them were tense, their expressions stiff. Something had clearly happened.

Chief Steward Ju led him to the main hall.

There were more than ten people inside, and it was so quiet that not even the sound of breathing could be heard.

When Jin Ha-gun entered, the people there turned their heads.

They were all faces Jin Ha-gun knew.

The heads of various sects in Wuhan. Some led dozens of disciples, others hundreds.

They silently gave Jin Ha-gun a cupped-fist salute.

Jin Ha-gun likewise greeted them only with a quiet cupped-fist salute. There was a reason they were exchanging such soundless greetings.

Among them was a bed, and a physician was treating someone.

An old man lay on the bed receiving treatment.

As if to show that he had wandered outside for many long years, his skin was darkly tanned and rough. His old, shabby martial attire seemed to have accompanied him for decades.

But anyone who knew him would never judge him by his appearance. If anything, that crude and rough exterior made him shine all the more.

Cheonae Geosa Hwa Yulcheong.

He had spent his entire life wandering the Central Plains and doing good deeds. If he met someone pitiful, he emptied his purse for them; if he met someone wronged, he always avenged their grievance.

A man for whom even the words “great hero” were insufficient—Hwa Yulcheong was lying on the bed.

Until Jin Ha-gun arrived here, he had never dreamed he would witness such a scene.

And even less had he imagined that the reason would be this.

Chief Steward Ju conveyed an astonishing fact.

“Today, the elder was attacked by an unidentified assailant.”

Jin Ha-gun’s expression stiffened.

Cheonae Geosa had been ambushed, of all times, now?

Jin Ha-gun’s gaze turned to his arm.

The physician was treating his arm. It was not a wound inflicted by a sword. The roughly torn injury was quite deep to be called self-inflicted.

After carefully finishing the treatment, the physician cautiously addressed the people there.

“The tears and gouges will heal with time, but the problem is the poison.”

Cheonae Geosa had been poisoned?

“If the antidote does not work, there will be aftereffects.”

At the physician’s words, Jin Ha-gun could feel a cold chill spreading through the room. It was the anger of those present.

Jin Ha-gun and the sect heads moved to a place a little distance away from the bed.

Since Hwa Yulcheong was asleep, Jin Ha-gun intended to go outside to talk, but they showed no intention of leaving. They displayed their will to guard this place until Hwa Yulcheong awakened.

“What on earth happened?”

At Jin Ha-gun’s low question, a young martial artist standing a short distance away spoke.

“The elder was struck in my place while saving me.”

He was He Ren, the sect master of Zhengzhao Sect, a small sect in Wuhan. After the previous sect master died in an accident not long ago, he had newly become sect master.

“When I heard that the elder had returned to Wuhan after so long, I asked him for help. I wanted to ask how I should lead my sect. After receiving the elder’s precious words, I was seeing him off when a demonic practitioner attacked.”

At the words demonic practitioner, Jin Ha-gun’s heart sank. At least in this place, those were words that should not have been spoken.

“Did you just say a demonic practitioner?”

He Ren nodded, and as if to explain why, Chief Steward Ju brought over something that had been placed beneath the bed and showed it to him.

“The manor lord was struck by this.”

After confirming what it was, Jin Ha-gun’s expression hardened.

Astonishingly, it was Jingyeok, a hidden weapon of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

The very thing that had drawn Geom Muyang to Wuhan had now appeared before Jin Ha-gun as well.

“What happened to the one who carried out the ambush?”

“He died by the elder’s hand.”

A middle-aged martial artist standing beside them stepped forward.

He was Gao Xiong, the sect master of Yijian Sect. He was a man who had lost one arm while fighting demonic practitioners in the past, and when it came to the Demonic Cult, he was someone who would leap up grinding his teeth even from sleep.

“As you know, that hidden weapon is a Demonic Cult hidden weapon designated as forbidden by the Murim Alliance. If it had not been for such an ugly and vile hidden weapon, how could the elder have been struck?”

A heavy silence flowed.

The other sect heads neither agreed with those words nor denied them.

If this truly was the Demonic Cult’s doing, then this was not something that could end with an apology.

At that moment, another person stepped forward. He was Huang Gen, the sect master of Wuhan’s Bisong Sect. He deeply respected Cheonae Geosa and followed him as one would a master.

He walked back to the bed, looked down at Hwa Yulcheong, and said quietly,

“No matter whose doing it is, I intend to find the culprit even if I must devote the rest of my life to it.”

The other sect heads also nodded and stood before the bed.

All righteous faction martial artists respected Cheonae Geosa, but those gathered here in particular were people who followed him closely enough to give their lives for him.

If they moved, the Wuhan murim would move, and if Wuhan moved, the entire murim would move.

Jin Ha-gun silently looked at Hwa Yulcheong.

‘Is this a self-directed act? Or was he truly attacked?’

In the current situation, he could not know.

But one thing was certain.

Though he had lost consciousness and had not spoken a single word, he was setting the hearts of these many righteous faction experts aflame.

The Demonic Cult tried to kill Cheonae Geosa!

How enormous an incident this was becoming. For now, it was only these people, but once the matter became known, the murim would be turned upside down.

Huang Gen turned to Jin Ha-gun and said,

“Squad Leader Jin, I hope the Alliance will not sit by and watch this matter.”

At that, the others also turned around. Everyone’s gaze focused on Jin Ha-gun.

“Have the Murim Alliance step forward, cast a dragnet, and make sure the culprit cannot escape.”

“You must lodge an official protest with the Demonic Cult.”

The demands continued, but Jin Ha-gun held his tongue.

‘Is this what you wanted? Do you really intend to use these people as a shield to get through this matter? Do not touch me. If you touch me, everyone will move. Is this how you are giving that warning?’

They stood lined up before Hwa Yulcheong.

This was not simply a wall raised because a righteous faction martial artist had been ambushed. It was a wall of trust he had built over the past several decades, a wall of respect. It was a wall built through decades of effort. That was why it could not be carelessly torn down.

It was just then. From behind them, a quiet voice was heard.

“…That is enough.”

At those words, the room fell silent. The voice had come from the bed behind them.

Everyone turned around in surprise, and Hwa Yulcheong was waking up, raising himself from the bed.

“Elder!”

“Manor Lord! You’ve awakened!”

“What a relief.”

Everyone rejoiced and gathered around him.

“Why are you all making such a fuss over something so trivial?”

The moment his sleeping presence awakened, it overwhelmed everyone there.

His presence was neither sharp nor forceful. It was an exceedingly gentle presence.

And yet it was a gentleness that seemed impossible to break with any strength.

Huang Gen first checked his condition.

“How can you say it is trivial? Elder, you have been poisoned!”

But Hwa Yulcheong replied as though it were nothing serious.

“I have suppressed it with my internal energy, so I am fine.”

This time, unable to hold back, the sect master of Yijian Sect shouted,

“It was the work of the Demonic Cult bastards!”

“Nonsense! How can you conclude it was the work of a demonic practitioner simply because the attacker carried a Demonic Cult hidden weapon?”

His reaction was completely different from what Jin Ha-gun had expected.

“I am fine, so do not make a needless commotion. All of you, go back. If a fight with the Demonic Cult breaks out over something uncertain, many innocent lives will be lost. So do not act rashly, and mind your tongues.”

After sternly rebuking them, he searched for someone among the crowd. The person he sought was the young He Ren whom he had saved.

“Are you all right?”

When he smiled at him, He Ren was deeply moved and gave him a great bow.

“I will never forget the grace of your saving my life for as long as I live.”

“What good is there in remembering this old man? Take good care of your own people. You remember everything I told you, don’t you?”

“Elder!”

He Ren lowered his head and shed tears.

Lastly, Hwa Yulcheong’s gaze turned toward Jin Ha-gun. When the two of them were alone, he would call him Ha-gun by name, but now he addressed him as the leader of the Demon Extermination Squad.

“Squad Leader Jin.”

“Elder.”

Their gazes met in midair. As always, Hwa Yulcheong looked at Jin Ha-gun with gentle, benevolent eyes.

From the time he was a little boy until now, this man had always smiled brightly at him without changing.

Was this man truly the one behind the conspiracy?

That was why he was confused, and that was why he was all the more suspicious.

Hwa Yulcheong asked in a calm tone,

“What is happening in Wuhan?”

That is what I want to ask. What are you trying to do in this Wuhan?

Everyone waited for Jin Ha-gun’s answer. They expected that, as the leader of the Demon Extermination Squad, he would know facts they did not. Whether it was the Demonic Cult’s doing or not, the ambush of Cheonae Geosa was a major incident.

“Nothing has been confirmed yet. However, in this murim that had been at peace…”

Jin Ha-gun gazed at Hwa Yulcheong and calmly added,

“It seems a great evil has awakened.”

Chapter 635: They Messed with My Brother and Friend at the Same Time?
---------------------------------------------------------------------
Jin Hagun had expected Cheonae Geosa to show some reaction.

He thought that, at the words that a great evil had awakened, the man might send him some meaningful look.

Yes, that evil is me.

But Hwa Yulcheong showed no such sign at all. He looked shocked, flustered, and worried—a reaction that made Jin Hagun’s intentions seem pointless.

“Captain Jin, what on earth do you mean? A great evil?”

Jin Hagun looked at the Jingeok that Chief Steward Ju had shown earlier and said,

“That concealed weapon is an exquisitely crafted counterfeit. Recently, those fakes have been circulating through the murim in secret.”

At those words, the leaders around them murmured in agitation. In particular, the Sect Master of Ilgeom, who hated the Demonic Cult and had believed it to be their doing, frowned. If it was a fake, then it could not necessarily be said to be the Demonic Cult’s work.

“I cannot speak in more detail here.”

Jin Hagun did not say that it was not the work of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. Nor did he speak of the death of the White Dragon Corps Leader.

For now, there was nothing to be gained by provoking their feelings.

Hwa Yulcheong’s eyes shone with righteous fervor.

“If you need help, tell me anytime.”

At Hwa Yulcheong’s words, the sect leaders also sent him reassuring looks, as if to say he had only to call on them at any time.

Jin Hagun cupped his fist respectfully and said,

“You all, great and righteous heroes, are protecting the upright spirit of this murim. On behalf of the Alliance, I offer you my sincere gratitude.”

After greeting them thus, he took his leave.

“I will be going now. Please rest.”

“I’ll see you out.”

Though he had told him simply to rest, Hwa Yulcheong rose from the bed. As his entire life had shown, he was not a man who rested for even a moment.

Jin Hagun went over and supported him. His rough hand was dry, all bone beneath the skin.

“It has been a very long time since I held your hand.”

Jin Hagun tried to recall whether he had ever held hands with him, but he could not remember. It must have been something from long ago, when he was young.

Hwa Yulcheong also said to the leaders present,

“You all should return now as well.”

Jin Hagun had thought of them as the wall protecting Hwa Yulcheong, but Hwa Yulcheong was trying to send them all away. With each word he spoke, Jin Hagun’s suspicions lessened by another fraction.

The leaders followed behind the two of them.

“They may attempt another ambush. We will protect you.”

At the words of the Byeoksong Sect Master, Hwa Yulcheong shook his head.

“I am of an age where I would have no regrets even if I died now.”

“Please do not say such things.”

This time, the Sect Master of Ilgeom stepped forward.

“You must protect the murim for another thirty years yet.”

“You fool, by then even you cannot guarantee you’ll still be here.”

The leaders following behind laughed. Everyone was relieved and happy that Hwa Yulcheong was safe.

Feeling the hand that leaned on him, Jin Hagun suddenly had this thought.

What if he truly is an evil man?

Perhaps the disguised life he had lived for decades had become his real life. Perhaps, while pretending to live virtuously, he had ended up becoming a great hero.

If that were the case, how could anyone see through such a life?

He might even be deceiving himself.

Then someone naturally came to mind.

A person who, no matter how much the other party hid and hid again, seemed as though he would eventually dig it all up.

A person who would thrust his head deep into the other’s heart and brightly ask, “What is this pitch-black hole here?”

A person who seemed like he would say, “I’ll tell you if you promise to dance with me!”

Just then, Hwa Yulcheong asked from beside him,

“Why are you smiling like that?”

For a moment, Jin Hagun flinched and looked at him. It seemed he had been smiling without realizing it.

“Since you are safe, Elder, I am in a good mood as well.”

Jin Hagun shook that person from his mind. This matter was something he had to resolve himself, no matter what.

And so they came out into the main hall.

When the martial artists waiting outside saw Hwa Yulcheong, they cheered in joy. Each person called him by a different name.

Manor Lord Hwa, Great Hero Hwa, Elder Hwa, Hero Hwa.

But the respect that filled their faces was all the same. There was no one gathered here who had not received help from him at least once.

Perhaps the rumor was spreading, for there were even more martial artists gathered than before.

Hearing the cheers from inside, those outside also shouted. It seemed the martial artists who had not been able to enter the manor had formed ranks outside.

At this rate, before the day was over, all the martial artists of Wuhan would know that Hwa Yulcheong had been ambushed.

Jin Hagun silently watched the people in the courtyard chanting Hwa Yulcheong’s name.

They were excited, like men going off to war.

With a single concealed weapon, Wuhan was truly boiling over.

Just then, a martial artist came in from outside and reported something to the Sect Master of Ilgeom.

The Sect Master of Ilgeom reported to Hwa Yulcheong.

“We have found the place where the Demonic Cult bastards are hiding!”

The person most startled by those words was Jin Hagun.

The Sect Master of Ilgeom gathered his subordinates and went out first.

“I will go.”

The others followed after him.

“We will help.”

Several leaders moved with him. Half of the people there ran outside together.

Jin Hagun could not simply stand by either. If they truly had found Geom Muyang’s residence, he had to go and prevent a clash.

“I will go as well.”

As Jin Hagun followed them out, Hwa Yulcheong said to him,

“A great evil possesses conviction. A wicked and distorted conviction. That conviction makes evil even stronger.”

Their gazes intertwined in midair.

Jin Hagun wanted to ask how one was supposed to overcome that, but now there was no time for such a conversation.

“I will return.”
