Jin Hagun launched himself forward. From behind him, Hwa Yulcheong’s voice reached his ears. As he had when they were alone, he called him by name.

“Be careful, Hagun.”

* * *

The operation room of Tongcheon Pavilion was busy today as well.

Because the highest level of emergency had been declared, the situation was, in truth, wartime.

Not only Tongcheon Pavilion but even Eunwol had joined forces, pouring all their intelligence resources into Wuhan.

Because of that, information was moving between the main headquarters and Wuhan faster and more accurately than ever before.

And today, an unexpected piece of news arrived.

An adjutant brought in an urgent dispatch with a shocked expression and reported,

“The Demon-Banning Order has been broken.”

The operation room, which had been moving busily, was suddenly enveloped in silence. Everyone’s eyes turned toward the adjutant who had made the report.

Sama Myeong’s expression hardened. It was the first time this had happened since he had become Chief Strategist.

The Demon-Banning Order had been broken?

It was a report that absolutely should not exist. And at a time of such extreme emergency, of all times.

The Demon-Banning Order was a special punishment issued by the Cult Leader’s decree.

It was usually imposed when a demon who had rendered great service committed a crime: when his past achievements were too great to imprison him in the thunder prison, yet the crime could not be overlooked.

They would have their internal energy suppressed and live throughout the Central Plains with their identities hidden. They usually lived as hunters, woodcutters, herb gatherers, or in other occupations that brought them little contact with people, though in rare cases they were permitted to run businesses in marketplaces.

Each of them was absolutely forbidden from leaving their assigned region.

If they violated the Demon-Banning Order and left their designated area, the severest punishment was imposed. Since they had violated the Cult Leader’s decree, they were killed only after being subjected to terrible agony.

That was why they might rather take their own lives, but they would never break the Demon-Banning Order.

“Who broke it?”

Then an astonishing name flowed out.

“The Demon Flame Lord has violated the Demon-Banning Order and left his area.”

The Demon Flame Lord was a demon of the previous generation, a figure who had already possessed Demon Venerable-level strength in those days.

The adjutant went to the box containing the flags and brought out the flag marked with Flame. The flag placed on the strategic map was covered in dust, having gone unused for a long time.

“And it is said that he has released the restriction on his internal energy.”

It was an even more astonishing report than the news that he had escaped.

Naturally, when they were confined, their acupoints were suppressed so that they could not use internal energy. At that time, a restriction was also placed upon them, and if it was not released by headquarters but instead undone through their own internal energy, they would die within a few days.

The day of death varied depending on the martial skill they possessed—at the shortest, one day; at the longest, seven days. After seven days, death was certain.

There was no way they would not know that, yet they had released the internal energy restriction and escaped?

Either they had resolved themselves for death, or they believed they would not die.

And the person who broke the Demon-Banning Order today was not only him.

Another urgent report flew in.

“White Bone Soul Demon has broken the Demon-Banning Order and departed.”

The flag marked Bone was also placed on the strategic map. White Bone Soul Demon was a demon of two generations past, older than the Demon Flame Lord, and a figure who had reached the astonishing age of one hundred and fifty.

‘So he was still alive!’

Even Sama Myeong had forgotten his existence.

If only one person had left, it could be considered an impulsive choice, but if two had left, that meant there had been external influence.

‘Someone has drawn them out.’

Someone who could discover their locations, which were secrets of the cult, and also persuade them.

As expected, the next report followed.

“They are heading toward Wuhan.”

Sama Myeong’s expression hardened. Either one of those two experts, if released, was capable of stirring up a great bloody storm in the murim. And such great demons were going to Wuhan? It was truly the worst possible situation.

It was already like treading on thin ice with the Demon Venerables there, and now the old demons who had broken the Demon-Banning Order were going there as well?

It was impossible to know whose hand they would seize and drag down into the abyss when the ice shattered.

“Shall we send a pursuit force?”

Under normal circumstances, they would have dispatched the elites, including the Demon Army, and pursued them.

No matter what happened, no matter how great the sacrifice, they would absolutely pursue and capture them. In doing so, they had to show that the Demon-Banning Order was a principle that must never be broken.

But Sama Myeong shook his head. Not this time. No, he could not. In this situation, he could not dispatch even the Demon Army to Wuhan.

Looking at the two newly appeared flags, Sama Myeong asked,

“Do you even know who is there before you go?”

Sama Myeong’s gaze turned to one place.

“Send an urgent dispatch and inform him of this.”

It was toward the new flag that had arrived in Wuhan only today.

Extreme.

* * *

“There are people I know in Wuhan.”

With those short legs of his, the Demon Buddha had diligently woven connections, and he had them here in Wuhan as well.

“I intend to use their help to dig into Cheonae Geosa’s side.”

Would mere acquaintances help with something like this? The Demon Buddha was trying to use a card he had been saving for his sake. Guessing as much, Geom Muyang expressed his gratitude politely.

“Thank you.”

“No need for thanks. It is only natural.”

Now that the Eldest Young Master had asked for help, the Demon Buddha would use everything he could to assist Geom Muyang. If there was nothing more he could do, he intended to help even if it meant selling the underwear he was wearing.

It was then. Homyeong quickly entered and reported,

“The Demon Extermination Squad and the martial artists of Wuhan are coming here. You must leave immediately.”

Geom Muyang and the Demon Buddha left the room at once without a word.

There was no way the Demon Extermination Squad would have joined forces with them and come here. The martial artists of Wuhan were probably attacking, and the squad was coming along with them, trying to prevent a clash somehow. Leaving quickly was the way to help Jin Hagun.

Geom Muyang had not expected that a situation would come in which he had to leave this place again. Truly, the situation in Wuhan was riding a rapid current.

They quickly escaped through the back gate. Since it was not his first time, Gwak Yeong was not afraid and ran after them.

Swish!

At the Demon Buddha’s finger wind, the man keeping watch at the end of the alley collapsed. The Demon Buddha did not kill him, only knocked him unconscious.

Geom Muyang said to the Jidan martial artists,

“Even if you run into them, you must not kill them!”

Because that was likely what the forces behind this wanted.

No sooner had they escaped than the Demon Extermination Squad and the Wuhan martial artists burst into the place.

“They just left! Hurry, let us pursue them.”

At the Sect Master of Ilgeom’s shout, Jin Hagun said to him,

“From this point on, our unit will pursue them.”

But the Sect Master of Ilgeom did not withdraw meekly.

“This matter concerns Geosa-nim. Please allow us to help.”

The eyes of the martial artists standing behind him were also earnest.

If he forced them to stop, he could stop them, but in the current situation, he could not do that. Moreover, the Sect Master of Ilgeom was a man whose arm had once been severed by a demon.

‘I trust you will get away on your own.’

Jin Hagun trusted Geom Muyang. Besides, the Demon Buddha was with him as well.

“Very well. Let us pursue them together.”

And so they began their pursuit.

And far away, on top of an old tree, two people were watching the scene. Standing on a slender branch, they looked handsome and fresh.

They were none other than Geom Mugeuk and the Poison King.

“He acts so high and mighty with me. Look at that. He is being chased in a panic by martial artists of the Murim Alliance.”

“If it were you, you would have even more of them trailing behind you.”

Pretending not to hear, Geom Mugeuk said only what he wanted to say.

“Good grief, light is practically shining from the Demon Buddha’s body. How does he intend to run away like that?”

The mere sight of the two of them standing side by side on the branch formed a scene like a painting.

As he watched Geom Muyang and Jin Hagun far away, chasing and being chased, Geom Mugeuk’s eyes deepened.

“In any case, these bastards are touching both my brother and my friend at the same time.”

Geom Mugeuk turned to look at the Poison King and added,

“And even our Poison King’s faithful gatherer.”

The Poison King nodded with an expression that said that was what angered him most.

“What will you do now?”

The Poison King was eager to quickly finish the matter in Wuhan and go dig up poisonous herbs with the Demon Buddha.

But Geom Mugeuk knew this matter was not so simple. That was why he had come personally.

Besides, he had even promised Sama Myeong that he would not step forward himself. Of course, it was a promise he would not be able to keep.

“Let us first go to a tavern where many pretty female heroes come and have a drink. I really came here without resting for even a moment. I hear the women of Wuhan are very beautiful.”

The Poison King looked toward the distant scene of pursuit. His expression seemed to say, And yet that mess is happening over there? Geom Mugeuk smiled and said,

“The two of them will handle it just fine. Let us take this opportunity to let those two boring people become a little closer. We will have a drink with the female heroes and make our plans.”

Looking down at the dazzling streets of Wuhan, Geom Mugeuk added calmly,

“Who says they are the only ones allowed to weave conspiracies?”

Absolute Regression, Chapter 636

Chapter 636: You Don't Even Like Women
--------------------------------------
The Demon Extermination Squad raced ahead, and behind them followed the martial artists of Wuhan led by the One Sword Sect Master.

The One Sword Sect Master believed the demonic practitioners he was pursuing were the ones who had attacked Cheonae Geosa, and so he was desperate in the chase.

“We must not let them get away!”

At the shout from behind, Jin Hagun’s expression hardened. If it had only been Geom Muyang and Mabul, they would not have been able to catch up, but they had the Earth Battalion martial artists and even Gwak Yeong with them.

Because of that, they would not be able to shake off the pursuit so easily.

Just then, a fork in the road appeared ahead. One of the Demon Extermination Squad martial artists running at the front examined the ground and the surrounding grass and trees, then looked toward the left path. As the one most versed in tracking arts among the Demon Extermination Squad, he had discovered where Geom Muyang and his group had gone.

Soon after, the One Sword Sect Master and the Wuhan martial artists following behind arrived.

Jin Hagun urgently said to the One Sword Sect Master,

“From here, let us split up and pursue them! We will take the left path. Please take the right.”

“Squad Leader Jin, be careful!”

Fortunately, the One Sword Sect Master immediately led his martial artists and ran to the right. It seemed he never imagined that the leader of the Demon Extermination Squad was deliberately trying to shake him off.

Jin Hagun and the Demon Extermination Squad martial artists ran even faster than before. Until now, they had not been pursuing at full speed.

Once they ran like that, they could see Geom Muyang and his party running ahead. Just from this lightness skill alone, it was clear that there was a definite gap in ability between the Earth Battalion martial artists and the Demon Extermination Squad martial artists.

Mabul, who had been running, looked back.

After confirming that only the Demon Extermination Squad was chasing them, he slowed down, and the entire group came to a stop. The Earth Battalion martial artists, who had run without rest, panted for breath.

Geom Muyang set down Gwak Yeong, whom he had been carrying on his back.

“Are you all right?”

“Yes, I’m fine. Are you all right?”

No matter how uncomfortable it had been for her to be carried, how uncomfortable could it really have been? She was worried about Geom Muyang, who had carried her and run.

Geom Muyang nodded with an impassive face. His breathing was calm.

To think she had actually been carried on the back of the Demonic Cult’s eldest young master. Truly, no one would ever believe it.

Gwak Yeong expressed her sincere gratitude.

“Thank you so much.”

If the Demon Extermination Squad had not arrived right then, she would have added a joke.

Thank you for not abandoning me to the orthodox faction.

No, perhaps it was not a joke but her true feelings. Astonishingly, she felt more at ease being with these demonic practitioners.

The Demon Extermination Squad arrived behind them and stood guard over the surroundings, while Jin Hagun approached Geom Muyang.

Geom Muyang thanked him.

“Thank you.”

Geom Muyang knew well that Jin Hagun had controlled the speed of the pursuit, and that now he had shaken off the other martial artists to come meet him.

Jin Hagun also thanked him.

“Thank you for coming without killing anyone.”

In the process of escaping, they had gotten out without killing any of the orthodox faction people.

In truth, the one who had far more reason to be grateful in this situation was naturally Geom Muyang. Geom Muyang knew that Jin Hagun was overextending himself greatly right now.

If it became known in the murim that he was conversing with him like this, he would be embroiled in tremendous scandal. After all, the leader of the Demon Extermination Squad was holding a secret conversation with the eldest young master of the Demonic Cult. He had intentionally let the demonic practitioners he was chasing go free. He was staking his position as successor.

“By the way, how did you know to come to our residence?”

At Geom Muyang’s question, Jin Hagun shook his head.

“I do not know either. One of the One Sword Sect Master’s subordinates brought word of the location.”

Before he had even had time to ask what had happened, the One Sword Sect Master had led the martial artists and charged out.

“Do you think the One Sword Sect is in league with them?”

This, too, could not be said for certain. The One Sword Sect was an old sect in Wuhan.

And the reason the One Sword Sect Master was being so proactive was also because his arm had been cut off by a demonic practitioner. For that reason, they could not conclude from this situation alone that they were the organization behind everything.

“The martial artists of Wuhan are in an excited state right now. You must be careful.”

Jin Hagun hurriedly bid farewell. In a situation where the One Sword Sect Master might return to this place at any moment, they could not leisurely stand around and talk.

“If you circle around that ridge and descend, you can return to Wuhan again.”

Jin Hagun told him a path by which he could return to Wuhan without being seen.

Geom Muyang and Jin Hagun cupped their fists to each other.

“Thank you.”

“Let us meet again later.”

After respectfully greeting Mabul as well, Jin Hagun finally looked at Gwak Yeong. He did not say anything else to her. He merely nodded once, then led the Demon Extermination Squad away from that place.

Gwak Yeong could tell. It meant that he had not forgotten about saving her younger sibling, so she need not worry.

After he left, Geom Muyang looked at Mabul. At the look in his eyes asking what they should do now, Mabul calmly said,

“First, you should send that woman and your subordinates to a safe house.”

In a situation where the martial artists of Wuhan were searching for demonic practitioners with their eyes wide open, moving around with his subordinates and Gwak Yeong would pose too great a risk of drawing attention.

Until now, they had been staying in an ordinary hideout in Wuhan, but now he intended to send them to a secure safe house.

Geom Muyang said to Ho Myeong,

“Go to the safe house with Artisan Gwak.”

If Mabul had not come, Ho Myeong would have said he would remain behind even if he had to do so alone, but with a Demon Venerable protecting him, there was no need to worry.

Gwak Yeong was grateful to Geom Muyang for looking after her until the end. She was even more grateful that he had called her Artisan Gwak instead of that woman.

Yes, she believed this connection would work out well until the end. After all, it was a connection that had begun on a day when good iron had come in.

She did not tell him not to leave everything to the leader of the Demon Extermination Squad and to please take good care of her younger sibling.

After watching this man, she could tell that such words were unnecessary with him. If he saved someone, he would not boast of it, and if he failed, he would not outwardly apologize.

No other words were needed for someone like this. She bowed respectfully and said,

“Please be careful.”

And so Ho Myeong and the Earth Battalion martial artists took her and left for the safe house.

When Geom Muyang and Mabul circled around the ridge and stepped onto the road leading to Wuhan, a man appeared before them. He was a martial artist of Tongcheon Pavilion who had continued to deliver information to Geom Muyang.

“There is a message asking the two of you to meet immediately.”

“From whom?”

“It is an order from the Acting Cult Leader.”

Geom Muyang said to the martial artist,

“Tell him I cannot return right now.”

He had expected to be told to come back. But he had no intention of returning now. He was determined to resolve this matter no matter what.

“No, he did not say to meet at the main cult.”

“Then?”

From the mouth of the Tongcheon Pavilion martial artist came a place he truly could never have imagined.

“He said to meet at the tavern with the best atmosphere in Wuhan.”

Ah! It was definitely a message sent by his younger brother.

* * *

“Those female heroes over there are looking at us. Don’t look. Ah, do you see that woman in the pale pink martial attire on the other side? She has been looking at us—no, to be exact, at me—for a while now. As expected! This face works regardless of region! Regardless of age! It works everywhere.”

At that, the Poison King, who had been looking outside the tavern, replied indifferently.

“She must have been looking at me.”

“If it were anyone else, I might deny it, but I cannot. I will acknowledge half.”

The Poison King was still looking outside. The noisy tavern packed with customers was chaotic. Truly, if he had not come with Geom Mugeuk, there would never have been a moment in his life when he would sit in a tavern like this.

“You do not even like women.”

At the Poison King’s words, Geom Mugeuk said,

“What kind of misunderstanding is that! I do like women.”

The Poison King snorted.

“I really do like them, you know?”

Of course, Geom Mugeuk knew those words would not work. As long as he left the beautiful Ian alone, saying he liked women could never work.

Geom Mugeuk naturally changed the subject.

“You have never been to Wuhan, have you?”

The Poison King hated going outside. Naturally, Geom Mugeuk had assumed that such a man had never come before the headquarters of the Murim Alliance.

“I have.”

“When?”

“In the past.”

“With whom?”

At that, a truly unexpected person came up.

“With the Cult Leader.”

“Father?”

The Poison King nodded. His gaze was still directed outside.

“When?”

“When I had just ascended to the position of Demon Venerable.”

He had come to Wuhan with Father back then? It was truly something he had never imagined.

“Though it may not seem like it, our father is surprisingly diligent. What did you come for? Please tell me about that time.”

But the Poison King did not tell him.

Geom Mugeuk imagined his father and the Poison King walking down the streets of Wuhan.

Because he had thought his father looked upon the Poison King unfavorably, he had considered it a truly ill-fitting sight.

But when he actually imagined the two of them walking down that road, he thought that, unexpectedly, they might have suited each other. Then again, they were two people who had once dug up poisonous herbs together.

Just then, a group of martial artists ran past the front of the inn.

“Move aside!”

The people walking along the road stepped to either side.

“The Demonic Cult bastards have appeared!”

They probably looked like people joining in the chase after his elder brother. Martial artists who had been walking on the road blindly ran after them. Wuhan was gradually boiling over.

Rumors spread extremely quickly, and the enemies were using those rumors to pressure them. They were rumors stronger than blades.

At that moment, two people entered the tavern. They came in with bamboo hats pulled low over their faces; they were Geom Muyang and Mabul. Since their faces were not revealed, at a glance it looked as if he had come with his son.

As he sat down, Geom Muyang respectfully greeted the Poison King.

“You have come.”

“It has been a while.”

The Poison King nodded and accepted his greeting.

“On the way here, I also heard about Han Wolgaek from Mabul. Thank you for your concern.”

“Do not mind it.”

After exchanging greetings like that, Geom Muyang poured wine for Mabul seated beside him.

He did not greet Geom Mugeuk at all.

“Brother, I’m here too!”

With an expression that asked whether he was invisible, Geom Mugeuk waved his hand in front of Geom Muyang.

“I told you, I’m right here. Is your bamboo hat covering you so you can’t see this side?”

But Geom Muyang pretended not to see or hear him and emptied his cup.

Geom Mugeuk lowered his voice and said to his brother,

“How insolent! The Acting Cult Leader has graced you with his presence!”

At that, Geom Muyang looked around with an incredulous expression. He was worried someone might have heard.

“Even if I shouted that I was the Cult Leader, no one would care. It’s too noisy for them to hear anyway. And who would even imagine that we’d be here drinking?”

“Is that why you have your face so openly exposed?”

“There are four of us gathered here. If all four of us covered our faces, that would stand out more, wouldn’t it? If we are going to do this, the two handsome ones should show their faces.”

“Then you should have told us to meet somewhere discreet!”

He did not need to ask why his younger brother had come.

Since Cheonae Geosa had appeared, the cult must have been on edge as well.

What he was curious about was this.

“Did you run away from home or something?”

Advisor Sima would never have let him come.

“Run away? As if. I came with the advisor’s permission.”

There was no way he had granted permission easily. Geom Mugeuk must have persuaded him somehow.

In any case, the fact that he had ultimately sent Geom Mugeuk meant that Sima Myeong also regarded this matter as that serious.

“What in the world is going on?”

At his younger brother’s question, Geom Muyang emptied his wine cup and revealed his thoughts.

“I think they deliberately lured us into Wuhan.”

It was something he had also said to the Murim Alliance Leader. He had pursued the one who made the forged hidden weapon and ended up standing before the Alliance Leader. He was certain there was some intention behind it.

Seeing that his brother was properly reading the board, a faint smile formed at the corners of Geom Mugeuk’s mouth.

“Then this Wuhan has become one enormous trap.”

The eyes of the other two Demon Venerables sharpened. There was a great difference between coming here and getting dragged into a matter that had grown larger, and being deliberately lured in.

“Is that not why you came too?”

At Geom Muyang’s question, Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned toward the Poison King.

“No. I came to see the Poison King. I wanted to give the women of Wuhan a feast for their eyes together with him.”

At that, the Poison King struck at the heart of the matter.

“You came to keep watch in case I caused trouble.”

Mabul, who was listening from the side, could not refute those words. There was certainly such a purpose.

Geom Mugeuk did not deny it either.

“You are the cult’s final bastion, so I should be beside you to stop you.”

Rather, he emphasized it even more.

“As you know, I am someone who even intends to stop Father’s unification of the murim. Do you think I would stand by and watch the murim be destroyed?”

At the words destruction of the murim, the Poison King’s expression changed subtly. Depending on how one heard it, those words could have been unpleasant, but the Poison King did not take them offensively.

As Geom Muyang listened, he could tell.

That, even if the meaning was the same, those words made the Poison King feel far better than saying, “The Poison King will handle it well on his own, so why stop him?”

They were words that naturally dissuaded the Poison King while putting him in a better mood. They sounded as if they had been tossed out lightly, but they had not been spoken carelessly at all.

“What will we do now?”

“I’m someone who’s only just arrived. You’re the one who should tell me what we’ll do from here, hyung.”

The situation was now frustratingly deadlocked.

In the past, relying on someone else in a situation like this would have wounded his pride terribly.

But Geom Muyang had changed enough to make such a joke now.

“I await the command of our august Acting Cult Leader.”

Geom Mugeuk smiled. He was so pleased by this change in his older brother. Hyung, you can change even more. You can keep changing.

“Then it seems the time has come for this Acting Cult Leader to step forward.”

Geom Mugeuk spoke calmly to his brother.

“Cheonae Geosa is a man who has lived frugally all his life. He hasn’t kept any subordinates of his own, either. But is he truly alone? When he’s someone playing such an important role?”

At that, Geom Muyang shook his head.

“There’s no way.”

Someone was surely guarding the area around him.

The Demon Buddha spoke cautiously to Geom Muyang.

“If so, it must be one of Wuhan’s sects.”

Then Geom Muyang pointed to one place.

“They said the One Sword Sect knew of our hideout.”

“They even pursued us.”

Just as the Demon Buddha was about to become certain it was them—

Geom Mugeuk shook his head.

“It won’t be. They’re in a position to freely use Wuhan’s orthodox sects. There’s no reason they would deliberately expose their own blade to us. They’ll use that blade at the very end.”

Hearing it, Geom Muyang and the Demon Buddha nodded, for it was true.

“Then it must be a sect among those following Cheonae Geosa, one that hasn’t stood out much.”

The Demon Buddha added to Geom Muyang’s conjecture.

“It would be a sect that established itself here in Wuhan around the same time as him.”

There would not be many sects that fit that description.

Geom Mugeuk admired the two of them.

“As expected, my brother and the Demon Buddha work perfectly in sync. Poison King, let us head back now. I think it will be more than enough to leave this place to these two.”

He had given the credit to them, but Geom Muyang and the Demon Buddha knew. They had reached this conclusion because Geom Mugeuk had been there with them.

“There is somewhere I must briefly visit.”

Geom Mugeuk looked toward the distant headquarters building of the Murim Alliance and added,

“When you come to a friend’s house to visit, you should greet the elders first.”

Absolute Regression, Chapter 637

Chapter 637: I Want to Hear That Reckless Thought
-------------------------------------------------
All the way back to the Alliance Leader’s Hall, Jin Hagun thought and thought again.

What should he report to his grandfather about Cheonae Geosa?

Since it was a task entrusted to him like a test for the successor, he would have liked to give a clear answer, but in the end, the conclusion Jin Hagun reached was this.

“To be honest, I’m not sure.”

His grandfather, whom he had expected to be disappointed, silently nodded.

Jin Paecheon considered it only natural.

If Cheonae Geosa truly was a villain?

How could his grandson uncover a scheme so deep and terrifying that it had deceived even him?

And if he was not?

If one suspected an innocent man, confusion and uncertainty were inevitable.

Either way, it was not an easy situation to see through.

Jin Paecheon rose from the grand chair and slowly walked to the window.

The panoramic view of the Murim Alliance as seen from the Alliance Leader’s Hall.

He had lived his whole life to protect this unchanging scenery. And he felt that he was now facing a battle more difficult than any he had ever experienced.

Jin Hagun’s question came from behind him.

“Will you meet with Geosa-nim?”

Jin Paecheon nodded.

“My friend was ambushed. Of course I must meet him.”

The time had come to meet him in person.

How should he treat him?

His grandson trusted Geom Mugeuk so deeply that he believed this incident was not a scheme of the Demon Cult, but he himself could not simply think the same.

He had to consider many possibilities. With one wrong misunderstanding, he might lose his closest friend.

Just then, an unexpected report came from midair.

“The acting cult leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult requests an audience.”

Jin Paecheon, who had been looking out the window, turned around. Jin Hagun, too, could not hide his surprise. It seemed he had come without even notifying his grandson in advance.

Even Geom Mugeuk had come in person?

It was one of two things. Either he truly believed Cheonae Geosa was behind this, or they themselves were the ones plotting.

Jin Paecheon said calmly,

“Show him in.”

“Yes.”

Jin Hagun felt his grandfather’s aura change. The gentleness with which he treated his grandson vanished, and in its place settled the dignity and authority of the Murim Alliance Leader.

His grandfather was now standing on a battlefield.

Though Geom Mugeuk was his friend, he was making his grandfather tense.

As if to prove it, Jin Paecheon reminded him of one fact.

“Do not forget where you said you placed your trust in that man.”

Jin Hagun had said he trusted Geom Mugeuk fifth. After his faith that they were right, his grandfather, the subordinates of the Demon-Slaying Squad and the martial artists of the Murim Alliance—only then would he trust him.

His grandfather was telling him now. Do not be swayed by some idle friendship and lean into emotion.

“I will keep it in mind.”

A moment later, Geom Mugeuk entered the Alliance Leader’s Hall with a bamboo hat pulled low over his head.

At the entrance, he removed the bamboo hat, then walked slowly to the foot of the grand chair and bowed with proper courtesy.

“I greet the Alliance Leader. Have you been well?”

Jin Paecheon descended from the grand chair.

He, too, received Geom Mugeuk with due courtesy.

“Welcome, Cult Leader Geom.”

He treated him not as Young Cult Leader Geom Mugeuk, but as Acting Cult Leader Geom Mugeuk. Personal ties were personal ties, and the courtesy that had to be observed between the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult and the Murim Alliance was another matter.

“Please treat me comfortably. I did not come today as the acting cult leader, but as Hagun’s friend.”

As he said that, Geom Mugeuk looked at Jin Hagun. Their gazes met in midair. In their eyes was a sentiment that could be understood without words.

Good to see you, my friend.

You always have to surprise people like this, don’t you?

Though his grandfather was watching, Jin Hagun did not hide his gladness. Once he actually saw Geom Mugeuk, he could not suppress it. Even though this was the man who teased him like that every time, seeing him made him this glad.

Geom Mugeuk turned back to Jin Paecheon and spoke respectfully.

“I arrived in Wuhan today. Since this concerns my elder brother, I moved discreetly. I apologize for not sending word in advance.”

Since he was greeting them on the day he arrived, it was not as though he had secretly entered Wuhan and conducted activities there. In other words, the only issue was that he had come without prior notice; there was nothing wrong with Geom Mugeuk coming to Wuhan itself.

After silently nodding, Jin Paecheon asked in a gentle tone,

“How is it, now that you have become cult leader?”

Geom Mugeuk looked up at the grand chair atop the steps. The Alliance Leader’s grand chair would surely be no more comfortable a seat than his father’s. If it had been a comfortable place to sit, that seat would already have been taken by his father.

“I have come to understand how difficult it is to protect that seat.”

Then he turned to Jin Hagun and said,

“Hey, friend. This time right now is much better.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, Jin Hagun only smiled faintly.

“Why? You don’t believe me?”

Geom Mugeuk said to Jin Paecheon,

“Please make him take it on, even briefly, so he can learn just how hard it is. It feels unfair that I’m the only one suffering.”

Jin Hagun could tell. Geom Mugeuk joking like this was also his way of saying to his grandfather, Please entrust the position of successor to your grandson soon.

‘Grandfather, how could I not trust someone like this?’

He had already saved his life, and even his younger sibling’s life.

And such a man had stood before him in many forms.

There were times when he was not the Young Cult Leader, times when he was, and now he stood as the acting cult leader.

Though each position was different, he had always been the same.

That was why he could say with certainty. Even if Geom Mugeuk ascended to the position of cult leader, he would be exactly as he was now.

“To be honest, when I first came, I intended to move secretly. I thought this matter was not ours, but something for the Murim Alliance to resolve.”

“Then why did you change your mind?”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned to Jin Hagun.

“I remembered a promise I made with that friend long ago. That if something happened, we would be sure to contact each other. If there was danger, we would be sure to help each other.”

At that, Jin Paecheon asked in passing,

“Are you not the most dangerous one?”

Geom Mugeuk lowered his voice ominously as he answered.

“I am certainly the most dangerous.”

Just as it seemed he was admitting it readily,

“But only to the wicked. I do not possess the generosity and mercy that the Alliance Leader bestows upon evildoers. I do not believe in repentance and reform either.”

Since this was an opportunity to learn Geom Mugeuk’s thoughts, Jin Paecheon asked calmly,

“Why do you not believe in it?”

“Because the repentance that only one out of a hundred sinners is capable of becomes a shield for the other ninety-nine villains.”

He meant that he would prevent the greater tragedy that clumsy forgiveness could create.

Jin Paecheon did not ask whether that one person should not still be saved. Jin Paecheon, with his domineering nature, was of the same mind at least in this regard.

“I, too, have no mercy for evildoers.”

“That is why I respect you, Alliance Leader.”

Geom Mugeuk met Jin Paecheon’s intense gaze head-on.

“I thought I had to receive the Alliance Leader’s permission before I began handling this matter.”

This degree of courtesy was enough. No, compared to the past, it was overflowing with courtesy. When had the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult ever notified them before taking action? Since Geom Mugeuk became Young Cult Leader, things had at least begun to change.

Jin Paecheon asked cautiously. From the moment he heard that Geom Mugeuk had come, this was what he had wanted to ask.

“What are your thoughts on this matter?”

Not only Jin Paecheon, but Jin Hagun as well was curious about what Geom Mugeuk thought.

“Do you mean the rash opinion of someone who has only just arrived and has not even seen Cheonae Geosa’s face yet?”

Jin Paecheon nodded.

“Yes. I want to hear that rash opinion of yours.”

Geom Mugeuk held his tongue for a moment. After showing through this brief silence that what he was about to say was by no means a rash answer, Geom Mugeuk spoke firmly.

“I believe Cheonae Geosa is behind this matter, and that he is a villain.”

A heavy silence flowed through the Alliance Leader’s Hall.

Jin Paecheon knew well what kind of person Geom Mugeuk was. He was the man who had brought him and the leader of the Sado Alliance to a shabby tavern in front of the main headquarters of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

He was the man who had made the successor of the Sado Alliance and his own granddaughter dance in front of countless masters. He was the man who had danced together with them there. He was the man who had ended the succession struggle without bloodshed.

Such a man had said this.

The Alliance Leader’s friend is a villain.

Even before he heard the reason, his chest already felt tight. Because there was certain to be a plausible reason.

Instead of his grandfather, Jin Hagun asked,

“May I ask the reason?”

Jin Hagun was exactly half in doubt and half in belief.

His grandfather might not want to face the truth, but he had to hear it. In order to protect his grandfather and the Alliance.

Though Jin Hagun had asked, Geom Mugeuk answered Jin Paecheon.

“Because I would have chosen that method as well.”

“!”

It was an emotional answer, but stronger than any other. Because it was Geom Mugeuk’s words, the answer had even greater force. It was the method a person as clever as he was would have chosen.

“Gaining the Alliance Leader’s trust means gaining the trust of the entire orthodox murim. If not for the fact that Cheonae Geosa is the Alliance Leader’s close friend, would he truly have been able to gain the reputation he has now?”

He would not have. There were countless people in murim who practiced righteousness and bestowed chivalry. And yet the reason Hwa Yulcheong had come to be known as if he represented them all was because he was the friend of the Murim Alliance Leader.

Geom Mugeuk asked something unexpected.

“Do you remember when you first became acquainted with Cheonae Geosa?”

Jin Paecheon nodded.

“How did you meet?”

Geom Mugeuk dug in from their very first meeting.

Jin Paecheon told him of that day long ago.

There had been a time in his youth when he wandered as a wandering hero. In those days, when he went about punishing evil, he heard the sounds of fighting in a village he was passing through.

“When I ran over, that friend was fighting against Green Forest bandits who had attacked the village.”

That was his first meeting with him. He helped him and cut down all the bandits.

Jin Paecheon realized Geom Mugeuk’s intention.

“You seem to want to say that meeting was planned.”

Indeed, Geom Mugeuk nodded and asked,

“Why do you think it was not?”

“At that time, not only bandits, but many villagers died as well. There were more dead than living.”

After letting out a sigh, Jin Paecheon added,

“Even several children died. That man is… not the kind of person who would do that.”

Jin Paecheon answered firmly. Even if he conceded a hundred times over, he could accept that the man might have approached him intentionally. But to sacrifice innocent lives who had not even learned martial arts in order to approach him?

He recalled the sight of him that day, holding a dead child in his arms and weeping bitterly. Those tears had not been sincere? Those tears that he still remembered vividly, as if branded into him?

“There is no way.”

Geom Mugeuk did not insist.

“That is why I told you, did I not? That this is the rash opinion of someone who has not even seen Cheonae Geosa’s face yet. My thoughts went too far.”

But in his heart, he did not think they had gone too far.

Because he had once experienced something similar. When he had returned to the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult to find Bimahon, the final material for the Great Rite.

At that time, he had deceived everything and everyone solely for the purpose of taking Bimahon.

If he, too, was a man with such will?

Geom Mugeuk did not reveal those inner thoughts.

“For now, I will investigate this matter with Squad Leader Jin. Whoever the bastard is, he tried to use the Alliance Leader’s friendship, so we should use friendship to deal with him as well.”

Jin Hagun nodded. He felt a conviction that, together with Geom Mugeuk, they would surely be able to uncover the truth.

This relief no longer wounded Jin Hagun’s pride. Rather, he was grateful that he had such a reliable friend.

Jin Paecheon, on the other hand, said nothing.

Though Geom Mugeuk had taken a step back, saying he had spoken rashly, Jin Paecheon could tell that he still considered Cheonae Geosa to be behind this. If he had not believed that, he would never have said in the first place that he was a villain.

Then Geom Mugeuk gave unexpected advice.

“When you meet your friend, tell him everything honestly.”

The scope of that honesty was beyond what he expected.

“Tell him that I have come, and tell him that my elder brother and I strongly suspect him. Tell him honestly how you feel as well, Alliance Leader—that the Demon Cult keeps acting like this, and it troubles you.”

It was truly unexpected advice, but there was a reason for it.

“You are someone who has protected the position of Alliance Leader for a long time, so you must have seen countless sorts who scheme. If you set your mind to it, you could likely deceive Cheonae Geosa without showing any sign.”

No, he thought he would not be able to deceive him.

The opponent was someone who had been at the Alliance Leader’s side for decades. He knew the Alliance Leader better than anyone.

“Even so, why should the Alliance Leader engage in a battle of deception? You possess a weapon far stronger than that.”

The weapon Geom Mugeuk spoke of was this.

“Fight with truth. Are you not someone who has led the orthodox murim all your life with a life free of falsehood? A half-baked truth would only be exploited by cunning evil, but the Alliance Leader’s truth will be as strong as ten-thousand-year cold iron.”

Was it because he could not bear to lie in front of his grandson?

With an awkward expression, Jin Paecheon spoke honestly.

“I am good at lying, too. I have dealt with people through lies before.”

Geom Mugeuk laughed pleasantly.

“Yes. Fight with that truth right now.”

Of course, he did not think the Alliance Leader’s truth would defeat him. If sincerity or truth could get through, how could it be called evil?

Even so, there was a reason he had said this to the Alliance Leader.

Because if the Alliance Leader met him while in such a complicated state of mind, it was obvious he would lose a hundred battles out of a hundred. He knew he would be swayed exactly as the other man intended.

No matter how clever a person was, no matter how strong-willed, the moment they were swayed by their opponent, it was over. Rather, because they were clever and strong, they would be drawn in more deeply and more powerfully.

That was why he had given him a standard.

It might be rigid and somewhat frustrating, but precisely because it was rigid, it was something he had steadily built up and could cling to tightly. He was telling him to take his reverent and steadfast life as his weapon.

And if Murim Alliance Leader Jin Paecheon could at least face him without being swayed—

“Then you may see something that only you, Alliance Leader, can recognize.”

Absolute Regression Chapter 638

Chapter 638: Do You Know Where That Place Is?
---------------------------------------------
Geom Mugeuk and Jin Hagun left the Murim Alliance.

Geom Mugeuk was still wearing his bamboo hat to avoid drawing attention, but—

“They’ll know over there that I met the Alliance Leader.”

Geom Mugeuk did not underestimate the enemy’s intelligence network. This place, Wuhan, was a stage they had built. The protagonists of this stage were them.

A grave look flickered in Jin Hagun’s eyes. If Geom Mugeuk’s words were true, it meant those bastards were watching them from close by.

“Are you saying they have eyes inside the Alliance as well?”

“Think of it as something they’ve been preparing for decades.”

Geom Mugeuk tapped the bamboo hat he was wearing and said,

“This cannot block the passage of that much time.”

Hearing those words, Jin Hagun felt something similar to what Jin Paecheon had felt. This fight would be harder than any fight he had ever faced.

“Is it them?”

By “them,” Jin Hagun meant the hidden force behind the tripartite meeting between the righteous, unorthodox, and demonic factions.

Geom Mugeuk understood at once and nodded.

“Yes, I believe it is them.”

Because only they would be capable of plotting such a conspiracy against both the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult and the Murim Alliance.

Jin Hagun asked carefully,

“You still think it’s Cheonae Geosa, don’t you?”

Jin Hagun wanted to confirm it one last time. He believed Geom Mugeuk was grasping the situation with sharper insight than he himself could.

Geom Mugeuk persuaded him of his belief by speaking of the possibility that he might be wrong.

“Of course, it may not be him. But if it isn’t, there’s no need to keep that possibility in mind.”

“Why not?”

“If he’s innocent, then I simply apologize for misunderstanding him.”

Jin Hagun looked at Geom Mugeuk as if asking how he could think it was that easy. Geom Mugeuk truly did think it was easy.

“A friendship of decades should be used at times like that. Not as something so precious that it confuses your judgment.”

“!”

Jin Hagun felt that those words contained the core of how he ought to view this matter.

“Compared to the consequences of failing to recognize Cheonae Geosa’s black heart, briefly misunderstanding a friend is nothing at all.”

Yes, he was right. In any case, without solid evidence, his grandfather would not kill Cheonae Geosa based on a mere feeling. Which meant he himself could suspect the man to his heart’s content.

At Geom Mugeuk’s refreshingly clear conclusion, Jin Hagun felt the tightness in his chest suddenly loosen.

“Thank you for coming.”

Jin Hagun expressed his gratitude sincerely. Geom Mugeuk would never know just how grateful he was that he had come.

“Of course I had to come.”

That one sentence was enough. Yes, of course he had to come. It would have been the same the other way around. Of course he would have gone.

“By the way, where are we going?”

At Geom Mugeuk’s question, Jin Hagun smiled and said,

“A friend came from far away. I should at least give him a room to sleep in.”

* * *

The manor Jin Hagun had prepared for them was a place used by the Demon-Exterminating Unit.

It stood beneath a mountain on the outskirts, far from the marketplace, and since everyone knew it belonged to the Murim Alliance, they would not have to worry about being searched by the warriors of Wuhan for the time being.

“See? The treatment changes the moment I arrive, doesn’t it? This is the kind of person I am.”

Geom Mugeuk boasted loudly from the entrance, but of course, no one indulged his bragging.

Geom Muyang examined the inside of the manor as though looking for anything suspicious, and Mabul went out to inspect the exterior. The Poison King slipped after Mabul.

Seeing that, Geom Mugeuk shouted,

“Even if the Poison King tempts you to go dig up poisonous herbs, don’t fall for it! He thinks of you as an herb gatherer, Mabul!”

The Poison King’s back visibly flinched.

Once the two of them had gone outside, Geom Muyang scolded his younger brother.

“Don’t be rude to Mabul.”

At that, Geom Mugeuk smiled meaningfully.

“You have to scold me when he’s beside you, so Mabul will understand how you feel, hyung.”

“I’m not saying it so he’ll understand.”

“I know. I do, but if he understands anyway, isn’t that better?”

Because he was Hyung’s very own Demon Venerable. Because he was the only Demon Venerable Hyung liked.

Geom Mugeuk sincerely hoped that this relationship would remain unchanged until the very end of their lives.

After examining the courtyard, Geom Muyang entered the rooms. Even while walking down the corridor, he carefully checked whether any mechanisms had been installed.

“Are you worried Unit Leader Jin might have pulled some trick in this manor? You don’t need to worry.”

Without looking back, Geom Muyang replied,

“What makes you trust him?”

Geom Muyang was worried about the same thing Jin Paecheon had worried over with his grandson. Not to trust people carelessly.

“Not everyone is like you.”

Geom Mugeuk followed closely behind him.

“Was that a compliment?”

Geom Muyang did not answer and opened the doors to inspect the rooms.

“You use this small room. This room will go to Mabul.”

Geom Muyang chose the largest and best room as Mabul’s. Of course, he gave the smallest one to Geom Mugeuk.

“Mabul, of all people, would be fine even if we gave him the smallest room…”

Geom Muyang frowned and whipped around, and Geom Mugeuk fled into the small room as if escaping.

“I’m telling you, act like that when Mabul is around.”

Geom Mugeuk flopped down on the bed.

“Ah, this is comfortable and nice!”

Lying there, Geom Mugeuk looked out the window. After watching the drifting clouds for a while, he asked loudly,

“I heard you called Unit Leader Jin first, Hyung?”

After a short pause, Geom Muyang’s voice came from his room.

“There’s someone I have to save.”

Geom Mugeuk also knew that an artisan’s younger sibling named Gwak Yeong had been taken hostage.

“It feels so awkward hearing someone who only ever thought about killing people say he’s going to save someone.”

Even when accused of being a murderer, Geom Muyang showed no reaction.

Of course, Geom Mugeuk was not about to miss this chance to tease his older brother.

“Is that woman pretty?”

It would not have been strange if he had snapped back, but no answer came.

Geom Mugeuk looked like he was just joking around and taking it easy, but inwardly, he was more tense than anyone.

Hwa Yulcheong.

How could he let his guard down when this was the first member of the Hwa clan he would face after his regression?

Then came a rebuke from the doorway.

“You should not have come. You should have stayed behind to protect the cult.”

When he turned his head, his brother was standing at the door.

Geom Muyang knew very well that it must never happen that two people of the Heavenly Demon’s bloodline were in the same dangerous place.

“The cult will be protected better without me there, won’t it?”

Since the temporary cult leader was busy robbing the Heavenly Demon Library.

“Stop joking!”

“What are you so worried about? You’ll protect me, Hyung.”

“Me? Why?”

“I heard you came all the way here because of me.”

Caught off guard by words he had not expected, Geom Muyang was flustered.

“Who said that?”

“Who else? Someone with a loose tongue.”

There were three people who had heard those words. When he had said them to Jin Paecheon, Jin Hagun had been below the pavilion, and Mabul had been inside the mist.

“Was it Unit Leader Jin?”

He naturally thought his friend Jin Hagun must have told him. But the culprit was someone unexpected.

“Mabul told me.”

On the way here, Mabul had discreetly told him through sound transmission.

He had said it in the hope that the relationship between the brothers would improve.

“He didn’t tell me for my sake. He told me for yours, Hyung. So you’d treat me better.”

At times like this, even that blunt brother’s face seemed to ripple with emotion.

“So express yourself a little when you live. How many years has Mabul spent living with his eyes only on you? It’s all right to show it now.”

Then Geom Muyang abruptly said,

“Thank you.”

“Not to me. I’m telling you to say it directly to Mabul.”

But Geom Muyang had not been speaking to Mabul.

“I’m saying thank you to you.”

“All of a sudden?”

Since the gratitude had come out of nowhere, Geom Mugeuk sat up on the bed.

“I’ve done so many things to earn thanks from you, Hyung, that I don’t know what you’re referring to, but anyway, it’s fine. Between brothers, that much is nothing.”

At that, Geom Muyang slightly opened the front of his robe. Inside, the Supreme Heavenly Silkworm Thread was wrapped around him.

He had asked Mabul to wrap it around his brother’s heart, and it seemed he had succeeded. As expected, when all was said and done, the person who handled his brother best was Mabul.

“Ah, this isn’t something I can call ‘that much is nothing.’ I tore off part of my life for you.”

If it had been his brother of the past, he would have said, “Who do you think you’re looking down on?” and immediately unwound it and told him to take it back. No, he would never have wrapped it around his heart in the first place. Even if it had been forced on him, he would not have shown it like this.

There were sides of him that had changed, but there were also sides that remained unmistakably like his brother.

“I’ll return this to you once we go back.”

Saying he would return it once they went back meant that he intended to stand at the front and fight.

He had been furious because his younger brother had nearly been killed, and now that his younger brother had come to this dangerous place as well, he was truly enraged at those bastards.

“Our hyung is angry. I don’t know who you people are, but you’re in big trouble now.”

* * *

Geom Mugeuk and Geom Muyang began their investigation in earnest.

Through Tongtian Pavilion, they received all records on the sects that had been founded in the years before and after Cheonae Geosa settled in Wuhan.

There were eight sects that fit the criteria.

Geom Mugeuk and Geom Muyang divided the sects to be investigated in half and looked into them personally.

Since they could not determine the truth from the written information alone, they went to the sects and observed them in person. Were these not two people who could sense something suspicious just by looking at a gatekeeper’s face, or by the air flowing through a courtyard?

After the two of them finished their investigation, they returned.

“The Ningchuan Sect is excluded because it has too many branches across the Central Plains. The Bajian Gate is excluded because its sect master died in an accident not long ago and a blood relative succeeded him. The Shijian Gate is excluded because they follow Cheonae Geosa far too passionately. And the Shengdao Gang has too many disciples.”

The two shared what they had investigated with each other, and Geom Muyang’s findings were no different from Geom Mugeuk’s. They could not find the guardian sect that had been secretly protecting Cheonae Geosa.

“It seems our prediction was wrong.”

At Geom Muyang’s conclusion, the two Demon Venerables nodded. Since the two of them had personally gone out to investigate based on Tongtian Pavilion’s records, the results were likely correct.

Which meant there was no sect here in Wuhan protecting him. Had he truly taken on this work alone and come this far by himself?

Geom Mugeuk firmly shook his head.

“That can’t be.”

He was certain they had missed something.

“This isn’t something that ends in a year or two. It has been carried out over decades, and they entrusted everything to the effort and luck of one person? Impossible.”

What if he had done well for twenty years, only to die in an accident the following year? Then twenty years would have been wasted for nothing.

Someone must have supported and helped him from his side. They must have overcome countless crises no one knew about. There had to be someone who had crossed the storms of time with him.

“Could it not be that, rather than a sect founded at the time, they won over another sect after he gained fame?”

At Mabul’s thought, Geom Mugeuk nodded.

“Of course, that’s possible, but I don’t think that’s the case this time. Somehow, I feel they would have placed the person they trusted most by his side from the beginning.”

This time, Geom Muyang offered his opinion.

“Then it may not have been a sect, but an individual who helped him.”

“He was friends with the Alliance Leader. Naturally, the Murim Alliance would have monitored and investigated him according to Alliance regulations. They must have done it thoroughly. Imagine if Father had a closest friend. Think how thoroughly we would investigate him.”

And according to Tongtian Pavilion’s investigation results, Cheonae Geosa had no friends other than the Alliance Leader. He was, in every sense, completely alone.

“Did you not say those bastards handle poison?”

The one who answered Mabul’s question was Geom Muyang. He had experienced it personally while pursuing the counterfeit hidden weapons.

“That’s right. A powerful poison had been applied to the counterfeit hidden weapons.”

And that was not all.

“On top of that, whether they used drugs or some kind of great art, they even controlled people’s minds.”

They had made them commit suicide at decisive moments and forced them to make specific confessions. Just that alone proved they were no ordinary experts.

The four people present knew better than anyone.

They knew how tense the Murim Alliance became when poison was involved, and how much they shuddered at soul-bewitching arts.

Murim warriors feared the Poison King and the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable the most.

And yet the Murim Alliance had failed to notice that the Alliance Leader’s one and only friend was associating with someone who used poison or soul-bewitching arts?

Geom Mugeuk thought that could not be. The ones who had missed something were them.

Geom Mugeuk examined the records again. He read them with a different mindset than before.

There is definitely something we overlooked. The answer is here.

Reading through the records with that thought, Geom Mugeuk’s gaze stopped sharply at one point.

A smile slowly formed at the corners of Geom Mugeuk’s mouth.

“Not an individual, and not a sect. Do you know of a place that can legally use poison and even stick needles into people’s heads?”

* * *

Geom Mugeuk and the other three sat in a teahouse, looking at the building across the street.

In the middle of the bustling marketplace, where many people came and went, stood a large building occupying expensive land.

Mingxin Medical Hall.

“This is the place Cheonae Geosa, who often carried out acts of chivalry, visited most frequently. He would bring people without money here and have them treated every time. Which is why no one would have suspected it.”

It was a place Geom Muyang and the two Demon Venerables had truly never imagined.

A medical hall? The place supporting the force behind this conspiracy was a medical hall? Who could possibly have imagined that?

There was another reason to suspect this place was the organization behind it.

“The physician here, Shao Qingluo, has excellent medical skill and a good reputation. In Wuhan, he is the most popular after the divine physician of the Murim Alliance.”

The Poison King and Mabul had also heard his name before.

“In particular, Shao Qingluo is famous for being skilled at detoxification. They say that when it comes to detoxifying poison, he surpasses even the divine physician of the Murim Alliance. So when warriors of the Central Plains are poisoned, they say this is the first place they seek out. He has saved more than a few warriors by now.”

Then the Poison King, who had not said a word until now, calmly struck at the heart of the matter.

“The flower of poison arts is not poisoning, but detoxification.”

Absolute Regression, Chapter 639

Chapter 639: Does Everyone Only Like Big Brother?
-------------------------------------------------
The flower of poison arts was not poisoning, but detoxification.

The Poison King’s words meant only one thing.

“You’re saying So Jeongrak is not a physician, but someone who uses poison arts.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, the Poison King toyed with the teacup before him and said,

“You’re the one who uncovered it.”

Until Geom Mugeuk found the answer, So Jeongrak had been a physician skilled at detoxification.

But the moment Geom Mugeuk found the answer—that Myeongsim Clinic might be the place supporting Cheonae Geosa—Physician So Jeongrak became a master of poison arts.

But uncovering that truth would not be easy.

So Jeongrak, too, was a man who had lived for many long years as a respected physician.

In some ways, he was even harder to deal with than Cheonae Geosa. He had placed countless martial artists under the most difficult debt of all to repay: a debt of life.

After all, the martial artists whose lives had been saved thanks to So Jeongrak must fill not only Wuhan, but all of the Central Plains.

Geom Mugeuk asked the Poison King,

“Among poisons, is there one that can control people?”

The Poison King nodded as if it were only natural.

“It is possible if one uses gu poison.”

The Poison King asked Geom Muyang,

“Great Young Master, was there only one person being controlled? Or were there many?”

“I have seen several so far.”

“Were they important figures?”

“No.”

After confirming the number and the details in that way, the Poison King gave Geom Mugeuk his answer.

“Then it likely wasn’t gu poison. The insects that control people are difficult to obtain and not easy to handle, so they can’t be used indiscriminately.”

Geom Mugeuk drew his conclusion.

“Then besides So Jeongrak, there must be someone else with the ability to control people. So Jeongrak, who uses poison arts as well as medicine, couldn’t possibly have mastered sorcery or some grand method for controlling people on top of that.”

The Poison King nodded as if in agreement. If So Jeongrak’s ability to detoxify poison surpassed even the Divine Physician’s, then his poison arts must also have reached the extreme. And since he was well versed in medicine as well, there was no possibility he had learned something else besides.

“The Alliance Leader will investigate Cheonae Geosa’s side, so we will have to uncover this side ourselves.”

It would not be difficult for the four of them to sneak in and kill So Jeongrak. No, there was no need for all four to go in.

To Geom Mugeuk, who was immune to all poisons, So Jeongrak posed no threat at all.

But what they had to do was prove that he was the force behind it all.

They had to reveal the truth that he was not a physician practicing medicine, but a poisoner practicing poison arts.

Then they had to show that evidence to the Murim Alliance Leader. They had to make the Orthodox Murim learn of it.

After all, they could not kill him without evidence and then ask people to trust them. That was precisely what made this matter difficult.

“I’m thinking of approaching him.”

He thought it could never be uncovered from a distance. The period during which So Jeongrak had hidden his identity was far too long.

“How?”

At Mabul’s question, Geom Mugeuk smiled and answered,

“What method is there to approach a physician? I have to go as a patient.”

But there was a problem. According to the information sent by Tongcheongak:

“The problem is that if I go as an ordinary patient, I won’t be able to attract So Jeongrak’s attention. He has so many patients, and they say there are more than twenty physicians working under him. Ordinary patients can hardly even see So Jeongrak’s face. However, those afflicted by deadly poison are an exception. The more unusual the poison, the more it will draw his interest.”

In the end, it meant that only by going there poisoned by a deadly poison could they properly meet So Jeongrak.

Naturally, everyone’s gaze turned toward the Poison King.

“Fortunately, we have someone here who can administer a poison for So Jeongrak to detoxify.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, one corner of the Poison King’s mouth lifted slightly. Geom Mugeuk knew what meaning was contained in the Poison King’s smile.

That bastard dares detoxify my poison? Does he truly think he can?

Geom Mugeuk knew better than anyone that when it came to poison, the Poison King was utterly sincere.

“I know, I know. If the Poison King sets his mind to poisoning someone, no one can ever detoxify it.”

At those words, the Poison King’s expression stiffened. Just once, someone he had sworn would die had returned alive. Of course, he had said the man had not returned with a normal body, but it was still something that had left a deep wound in the Poison King’s pride.

“Please make it a poison that he can barely detoxify over several days. If possible, one that will draw his attention.”

The Poison King surely possessed such a poison.

The problem was not the poison, but the patient.

“Whom should I poison?”

At the Poison King’s question, the answer had, in truth, already been decided.

Geom Mugeuk excluded the Poison King first.

“You can’t step forward yourself, Poison King.”

Because there was a possibility the other party would recognize the Poison King. No matter how much he hid himself, there would surely be something that only those two could recognize.

“And it’s not as if Lord Mabul can step forward either.”

As he said that, Geom Mugeuk quietly glanced at his elder brother.

“As you know, my brother is tough, so he endures pain well.”

Geom Muyang looked utterly dumbfounded.

In truth, even if Geom Mugeuk wanted to do it, he could not. He was immune to all poisons, so poison would not work on him. The only person present who knew that fact was the Poison King.

“I’m the temporary cult leader, Brother.”

Then he asked Mabul for support.

“Even if it’s the Poison King, if he poisons the temporary cult leader, Father will scold him, won’t he?”

Mabul did not stand by and let him drive the matter toward Geom Muyang.

“He’d be scolded even more for poisoning the Great Young Master.”

“Why?”

When Geom Mugeuk asked as if protesting, Mabul answered as if it were obvious.

“He’s the eldest son, isn’t he? The Cult Leader has always cherished the Great Young Master greatly.”

“What about me?”

“Well, I suppose he cherished you too.”

“That’s too much! Brother scolds me for being rude to Lord Mabul, and Lord Mabul only takes Brother’s side. Did you two conspire together?”

Geom Muyang could tell.

Geom Mugeuk had asked Mabul for support in order to bring up those words. In order to let Mabul know that he had scolded him for acting rudely.

‘Why go that far? I hated you so much.’

Mabul, for his part, was also making an effort.

“You’re better at acting sick, aren’t you?”

“How would you know when I’ve never even tried? And don’t you think acting as the patient’s guardian is even more important?”

As a faint smile formed at their exchange, Geom Muyang said to the Poison King,

“I will do it.”

To Geom Muyang, this matter felt like fate. He had nearly killed Geom Mugeuk with a poison-coated hidden weapon. That was why they had come this far.

And now, a situation had arisen in which he had to be poisoned. Could this not be fate telling him to pay the price for that mistake?

“Instead, bring one martial artist from the safe house.”

At Mabul’s concern, which showed just how much he cared for him, Geom Muyang smiled faintly and said,

“It is all right, Lord Mabul.”

“How is it all right? It isn’t just any poison, but one strong enough to catch that man’s eye. Your body will be damaged greatly. I would rather do it myself.”

He truly wanted to do that for him. At times like this, how nice it would have been if his appearance had been ordinary.

As if thanking him for that sentiment, Geom Muyang bowed his head politely.

Then he bowed politely to the Poison King as well.

“I entrust this to you.”

Watching him, the Poison King gave him an unexpected gift. In such a situation, would he be the sort of man to simply administer poison and be done with it?

“In the process of treating the poison I administer this time, your body will gain greater resistance to poison. Even if you are struck by the same poison, you will be able to endure far longer than you can now.”

From those words, one could tell that the Poison King intended to use an extremely precious poison.

Geom Mugeuk quickly stepped forward.

“I’ll do it! Poisoning my brother? This unloved second son will do it. What kind of law is this? Do all of you truly only like my brother?”

Only then did Mabul’s expression ease as he spoke to the Poison King. As for safety, he trusted him to handle it well.

“Still, I ask that you use a poison that hurts less.”

Geom Mugeuk stuck out his lips and made one hateful remark.

“Then it won’t be effective. It should be a poison that makes him scream on its own…”

At that moment, a scream burst from Geom Muyang’s mouth.

“Urgh!”

The Poison King had merely glanced at Geom Muyang once.

In an instant, his face turned pale and he clutched his chest. The poison had been administered before they knew it.

“Uuuugh.”

The groan that flowed from Geom Muyang was no ordinary sound.

“Good grief, you really are impatient!”

Geom Mugeuk swiftly hoisted his brother onto his back and rushed outside.

Mabul was inwardly startled. He knew well how skilled the Poison King was at administering poison, but to think he had done it right before his eyes and he had not noticed at all.

Then the Poison King said calmly,

“The Great Young Master will be fine.”

That single word from the Poison King was more reassuring than anything else.

Mabul watched the two men rush into Myeongsim Clinic for a moment, then said,

“It will take time to treat the poison.”

Mabul knew the best way to repay the Poison King’s kindness.

“Let us go dig up some poisonous herbs.”

* * *

Myeongsim Clinic was far larger than it had appeared from the outside.

In the courtyard, as large as a plaza, there were countless people. Amid the pungent scent of medicine reaching all the way to the entrance, the scene of the place unfolded.

There were patients limping along wrapped in bandages, physicians in white robes hurrying to and fro, workers unloading piles of medicinal herbs from carts, women trimming herbs beneath trees, and people lined up side by side pounding medicinal herbs in mortars.

There were also ordinary people, not martial artists, standing in line. At a glance, they were clearly destitute people, the penniless and poor. They said the clinic treated such people for free, and it seemed that was truly the case.

With his brother on his back, Geom Mugeuk ran into the place and shouted,

“My brother has been poisoned! Poisoned!”

Then a young man nearby ran over. Judging by his appearance, he was not yet a formal physician, but seemed to be someone who assisted the physicians while learning medicine.

“This way! Hurry, this way!”

The place the young man guided them to was where patients in urgent condition were gathered.

“If you wait just a moment, a physician will come soon.”

After having Geom Muyang laid on a bed, the young man hurried off to call someone.

Geom Mugeuk looked around the place. The twenty or so beds were full of patients, from people stabbed by blades to people with cracked skulls. The place was filled with critical patients.

He had known it was large in scale, but Myeongsim Clinic in reality was truly bustling.

“Looks like there’s a war going on somewhere besides ours.”

Geom Muyang heard him, but could not answer at all. It truly hurt as if his whole body were burning away. Because his mind was perfectly clear, he felt the pain in its entirety.

“Brother, are you all right?”

At Geom Mugeuk’s question, Geom Muyang cursed at him with his eyes. Do I look all right? You try going through this!

“I really wish I could hurt in your place.”

Contrary to his words, his eyes were smiling. If he did not tease him now, when would he? That intention was plainly visible in his smiling eyes.

Thinking that focusing on something else might lessen the pain a little, Geom Mugeuk kept talking to him.

“Your internal energy?”

Geom Muyang frowned and nodded. It meant he could use it. The intention was to make sure he could use martial arts if an emergency arose. In that brief instant while administering the poison, the Poison King had considered everything that needed to be considered.

Just then, a young physician came to the area.

“What poison was he struck with?”

“I don’t know. Please, save my brother!”

The physician examined Geom Muyang’s condition. When he touched his body, Geom Muyang let out a scream.

“Uaaaaaagh!”

If his brother was screaming like this, then it really had to hurt.

The young physician was clearly flustered. He tried to determine what poison it was and to reduce the pain, but the screams only grew louder.

Without even saying to wait a moment, the physician ran out.

Looking down at his suffering brother, Geom Mugeuk suddenly wondered what he would do if this situation were real. If this were not a situation where he could trust that the Poison King had made all the proper arrangements?

What if his brother were truly dying before his eyes?

What would he feel then? Seeing how his chest tightened on its own and a chill ran down his spine, he could feel that his feelings for his brother had grown much deeper.

“You’ll laugh later as much as you endure now. So hold on just a little longer.”

His brother’s mouth shaped the words.

Shut up!

Soon after, another physician rushed over. This time, it was a middle-aged physician, older than the one before.

He examined Geom Muyang’s condition more skillfully than the young man earlier.

But he, too, could not stabilize his body. In truth, it was only natural. How could ordinary physicians handle the Poison King’s poison?

Fortunately, he gave up more quickly than the young physician had. Just from that, one could tell his skill was much higher.

After the middle-aged physician withdrew, a short while later, at last, he arrived.

The moment he saw the physician with a kind impression and dignified build, Geom Mugeuk knew. This man was So Jeongrak.

Instead of wearing a nameplate that said So Jeongrak, he wore wings. Three or four physicians followed respectfully on either side of him.

He was someone Geom Mugeuk had never seen before. Perhaps the reason there had been no Poison King among the Twelve Zodiac Kings was that this man existed separately.

As So Jeongrak passed the patients, he issued prescriptions. With a single sweeping glance at a patient’s condition, the prescription came out.

“Boil equal parts green wood lotus and seven-star grass and have him drink it!”

He unwrapped the next patient’s bandages, examined the wound, and personally applied golden wound medicine.

“Fortunately, the sword merely grazed past the organs.”

Just by looking at the outside, he could tell whether the internal organs had been injured or not.

It was the same with the next patient.

He personally set the bones of a patient said to have fallen from a high place and applied splints. He showed no concern even when blood stained his body.

When his hands touched them, the screams of pain died down.

As if amazed, Geom Mugeuk looked down at Geom Muyang. Even amid that pain, his brother was watching So Jeongrak.

In that way, treating patients without hesitation like an immortal descended from heaven, So Jeongrak reached Geom Muyang’s bed.

Seen up close, perhaps because of his dignified build, his skin was taut and his eyes held vitality.

The previously unhesitating prescriptions paused for a moment.

After examining Geom Muyang’s condition, he asked Geom Mugeuk beside him,

“Who afflicted him with this poison?”

Geom Mugeuk answered him,

“I don’t know. My brother has such a filthy temper that he has many enemies.”

Even while enduring the pain, Geom Muyang visibly looked dumbfounded.

“I heard that when it comes to detoxifying poison, you are the best physician in the Central Plains. Please, save my brother.”

So Jeongrak’s gaze deepened as he looked down at Geom Muyang. Surprisingly, So Jeongrak knew of this poison.

“This poison is one that only three people in the world can detoxify.”

Absolute Regression, Chapter 640

Chapter 640: Feels Like We’ve Become the Villains?
--------------------------------------------------
“Who are those three people?”

Instead of answering Geom Mugeuk’s question, So Jeongnak gave him a suspicious look.

“This poison isn’t the sort one can easily fall victim to even if one wishes to.”

He stared intently at Geom Mugeuk and asked,

“Who are you people?”

So Jeongnak truly seemed not to know Geom Mugeuk’s identity.

Did he really not know? Or was he pretending not to?

From what showed on the surface alone, there was no way to tell what sort of man he was.

Geom Mugeuk had told Jin Hagun that those bastards would know even that he had met the Alliance Leader.

He thought this So Jeongnak might know him as well. And if he did not, then even that would be part of the plan intended by those behind the scenes.

That was how little Geom Mugeuk underestimated this opponent.

“I am Geomyeon of the Seodopa Sect in Gansu. This is my elder brother.”

For now, Geom Mugeuk revealed his identity using the name and sect he always used when disguising himself.

“How did he come to be poisoned?”

Geom Mugeuk answered honestly.

“We were drinking tea at the teahouse across the street when my brother suddenly complained of pain and collapsed.”

There would be people who had seen him run out of that place carrying his brother on his back, so it would be confirmed soon enough.

“But is it all right to waste time like this? My brother is going to die!”

Even at this moment, Geom Muyang was gritting his teeth and enduring the pain.

Yet even while looking at him, So Jeongnak did not seem particularly urgent. He seemed to know that it was not a poison that would take his life immediately. And then came the decisive remark.

“Endure it. The more it hurts, the more beneficial it will be for you.”

With those words, it became clear. He knew exactly what this poison was.

“Ah! To think I would see this poison here. I had never heard of it being used in this era, so I thought it was a lost poison.”

Geom Mugeuk saw an irrepressible surge of emotion pass over So Jeongnak’s face.

So Jeongnak explained to the physicians who had followed him in.

“The poison this man has suffered is called Omang Sindok. It is made by mixing powdered Pal Saek Ogong with the inner core of a Taeum Hyeolmang.”

The Pal Saek Ogong was a venomous centipede said to possess eight colors and be as long as a grown man was tall, while the Taeum Hyeolmang was a serpent spirit beast that lived only in regions where extreme yin energy flowed.

In other words, this Omang Sindok was a poison made by combining two spirit beasts, either of which one might go a lifetime without ever seeing.

Hearing his explanation, Geom Muyang was inwardly moved.

‘The Poison King truly gave me a precious poison.’

The pain had been so intense that he had forgotten the Poison King’s words—that once the treatment was finished, his resistance to poison would become stronger. Since it was such a precious poison, it must have such an effect.

Of course, Geom Muyang knew. He had not administered this poison because he liked him. It was probably because of his relationship with his younger brother.

So Jeongnak’s explanation continued.

“Once poisoned by Omang Sindok, one suffers excruciating pain as if being burned alive for seven days, and on the final, seventh day, one dies while feeling the blood vessels throughout the body freeze over.”
