Of course, he had also considered the possibility that their analysis might be correct.

He envisioned his father sitting alone upon the throne of Cheonma Hall, engulfed in profound sorrow. A father grieving for his child.

Perhaps on the day he designates his brother as the new successor, he might even say this to the Eight Demonic Venerables lined up before him:

—Let us go. To the Central Plains!

A fishing rod might protrude from the carriage window of his father’s war-bound carriage. Perhaps he would march to war carrying Geom Mugeuk thus.

Truly, there was no telling how his father would react.

"So this is truly the prediction of a clever man?"

To Geom Mugeuk’s question, So Jeongrak answered.

"This outcome is of your own making."

Honestly, Geom Mugeuk did not feel bad. Because it meant his father cared for him that much.

"But if a war breaks out because I die, will you remain unharmed?"

So Jeongrak smiled meaningfully.

"We are people accustomed to living differently."

It meant they would hide completely, never revealing themselves. If experts of their caliber intentionally concealed themselves, finding them amidst a war would be impossible.

"Rather, the war will bring us enormous wealth."

With the power cultivated from that wealth, they would seek to bite the throat of the wounded victor.

Geom Mugeuk openly scoffed.

"Indeed. That is your way, is it not? A life spent waiting with bated breath and a kind face for the moment to strike from behind—is that not the life you have led?"

Though those were words that could provoke fury, So Jeongrak remained composed. The thrashing of one on the brink of death was always merely laughable.

It was a situation devoid of variables. He was afflicted by poison that would cause his meridians to burst if he used internal energy, and two supreme experts stood before him besides.

So Jeongrak gazed at the two demonic elders with satisfaction.

Ma Yeomgun flickered with a bloody demonic aura. Never before had he seen anyone reveal demonic energy so red yet so vivid.

His known unique martial art was the Flame Palm.

A demonic art infused with extreme yang energy as scorching as magma; the mere image of a palm flying like a brand to embed itself in one’s chest, burning through the skin and boring into the flesh, was a terrifying technique.

Despite having reached the age of one hundred, his eyes radiated a fearsome light rivaling that of the young.

This time, he turned his gaze to Baekgol Honma.

His face and limbs were white as if dusted with powder. At a glance, he truly looked like a skeleton. Indeed, considering his age, it was about time he became white bones.

All manner of bone ornaments hung from his gaunt frame, crafted from real human bones.

His unique martial art was the Soul-Returning Ghost Spirit Art, a forbidden demonic art that manipulated the souls of the dead.

An art that stole souls, revived corpses, and sent hundreds of skeletons rising to charge—just imagining it sent chills down the spine.

The dark energy emanating from his eyes truly felt connected to the depths of hell.

‘How about it? Even someone like you—would you raise your voice before them?’

With a triumphant air, So Jeongrak looked at Geom Mugeuk.

Just then, Geom Mugeuk abruptly addressed the two demonic elders.

"Aren't you going to greet me?"

So Jeongrak thought he had misheard.

But he had not.

Geom Mugeuk said to them in a louder voice:

"I am the Acting Cult Leader. Offer your greetings first!"

Ma Yeomgun and Baekgol Honma exchanged glances. How could they have ever imagined Geom Mugeuk would act this way?

"Acting Cult Leader? Is the Cult Leader dead?"

Ma Yeomgun asked in a voice like scraping iron. He seemed to have swallowed his age with his voice; it was grating to the ears.

At that, So Jeongrak answered politely.

"The Demonic Cult Leader is currently in secluded cultivation."

Rather, Baekgol Honma, who had been listening nearby, opened his mouth wide. The inside of his mouth was clearly visible as he smiled brightly like a child, nearly toothless, with only a few teeth remaining.

"Alive, is he? Splendid. I shall tear the Demonic Cult Leader apart and kill him with my own hands."

Despite having reached the age of one hundred fifty, he spoke in a clear, high child’s voice.

As the saying goes—growing old means becoming a child again—his voice, his expression, his entire demeanor was innocent.

Yet his eyes, brimming with dark energy, contrasted so starkly with that appearance that the overall impression was utterly eerie.

The energy flickering from Ma Yeomgun’s body flared up, growing scorching hot.

"I feel the same. Today, let us tear that brat apart."

Bound by the Demon Restriction, they had longed for only this day. The years spent consumed by malice. Once their demands were met and the restriction lifted, all those years would be compensated with blood.

Geom Mugeuk scoffed. Did they wish to bluster before him? Or had they lost all sense of reality after being bound by the Demon Restriction for so long?

A righteous faction expert might utter such words. But for one who cultivated demonic arts, such words could only be empty bluster.

Because the Guhwa Demonic Art stood at the apex of all demonic arts; facing the Guhwa Demonic Art using demonic arts was many times more difficult.

Furthermore, his father was currently challenging a new realm beyond Ten Star Great Perfection.

When Geom Mugeuk laughed, Baekgol Honma laughed broadly as well.

Geom Mugeuk looked into his mouth and said,

"You haven't any teeth. Do you still manage to eat?"

After staring at Geom Mugeuk, Baekgol Honma turned to Ma Yeomgun.

"What has become of our cult in my absence?"

"I have heard that the youth of today have lost their manners."

"We were the same in our day, yet nothing has changed."

And so the child’s voice and the scraping-iron voice went back and forth.

Despite hearing their exchange, Geom Mugeuk addressed them even more sternly.

"Greetings first! You will observe propriety yourselves before demanding it of others!"

Ma Yeomgun and Baekgol Honma looked at Geom Mugeuk. At first, they had regarded him with irritation, thinking, what kind of brash, shameless wretch is this?

But the gaze with which Geom Mugeuk looked at them was clear and profound.

He did not burn alongside their heat.

Nor did he sink into their profound darkness.

In those eyes, there was none of the reckless impetuosity or frivolity of a successor unable to distinguish heaven from earth.

Geom Mugeuk was truly chastising them.

Without a trace of fear, he berated them with dignity and authority.

Moreover, Geom Mugeuk was not exerting any internal energy whatsoever. Truly, he overwhelmed them with nothing but the raw presence of a human being.

"Remarkable."

Ma Yeomgun clapped. It was sincere applause. Never before had he been so overwhelmed by one so young that he felt admiration.

Baekgol Honma likewise revealed his honest feelings.

"Killing him would be a waste, a true waste."

He could now understand where the analysis came from—that the Demonic Cult Leader would start the Demonic Great War. How could one endure losing such a son?

Geom Mugeuk continued to chastise them calmly.

"Age is devoured with the mouth. Know when to speak and when to hold your tongue. Know when to curse and when to offer greetings. Prove with those very mouths that you have not lived your long years in vain."

A brief silence fell.

And then something astonishing happened. Ma Yeomgun, who had been quietly staring at Geom Mugeuk, truly offered a greeting.

"This Ma Yeomgun pays respects to the Acting Cult Leader."

It was a scraping-iron voice, yet a greeting with its own courtesy. Of course, this greeting did not mean they would not kill him. It was admiration for this remarkable bearing. With such momentum at so young an age, a greeting was only fitting.

Baekgol Honma, however, did not comply so readily.

"I have indeed lived my years in vain. All because of your damned Cult Leader's decree."

Even so, no easy reaction would come from Geom Mugeuk.

"It is because of the sins you committed. Greet me!"

Fwoosh!

In an instant, dark energy erupted from Baekgol Honma's eyes.

"Let us see if you possess the qualifications to receive my greeting."

The Dark Heart Eye, which peers into the souls of men, was unleashed. Through this demonic art, he could discern what manner of person his opponent was.

Before the Dark Heart Eye, no bluff and no pretense could avail. For it laid bare one's true form. He would see a fox in a tiger's hide, a coward clad in armor.

The moment he peered into Geom Mugeuk's heart—

Baekgol Honma flinched in surprise.

A moment later, the dark energy vanished from his eyes.

His gaze, having peered into the heart of the one called Geom Mugeuk, had changed.

He took a step back in their contest of spirits, quietly gazing at Geom Mugeuk.

"This Baekgol Honma pays respects to the Acting Cult Leader."

Only then did Geom Mugeuk accept their greetings.

"I am Acting Cult Leader Geom Mugeuk."

In that moment, they felt as though they stood within Cheonma Hall. It felt as though Geom Mugeuk sat upon the throne, while the two looked up at him from below.

As if he had never wielded any authority at all, Geom Mugeuk smiled broadly and greeted them anew. He was as friendly as if they beheld a completely different person.

"It is a pleasure to meet you, my seniors. Even if you mean to kill me, there's no harm in a greeting, is there?"

So Jeongrak, watching this, felt that changing the target to Geom Mugeuk had truly been a wise decision.

Receiving a greeting from those two in this situation? Who in the martial world could possibly accomplish such a thing?

He became convinced that once this Young Cult Lord grew further, no one would be able to stand in his way.

So Jeongrak sought to suppress Geom Mugeuk's momentum. He belittled it as if it were nothing, precisely because he felt its weight.

"Meeting you two while unable to use my internal energy, I was utterly terrified. He probably has no idea what he's even saying right now."

At the mention that he could not use internal energy, Ma Yeomgun voiced his displeasure in an even more grating voice.

"Why has his internal energy been suppressed? We said we would kill him ourselves!"

A greeting was merely a greeting; their desire to tear Geom Mugeuk apart remained undiminished.

So Jeongrak explained regarding Geom Mugeuk. These ones had spent decades bound by the Demon Restriction; they would be unaware of the recent state of the martial world.

"That Young Cult Lord possesses truly unparalleled martial prowess."

Those who had already died by his hand had proven as much.

"Even with his internal energy suppressed, he is absolutely not one to be taken lightly."

"Even so, we cannot kill one who cannot use internal energy. Release him."

So Jeongrak said with a composed expression.

"I cannot do that."

Though the two demonic elders were fearsome, So Jeongrak did not fear them. Those who wielded poison feared no one. The only one he acknowledged was the Poison King.

Baekgol Honma stepped forward, breaking the taut tension between them. Rather than asking to release Geom Mugeuk's internal energy, he asked something else.

"Are you fully prepared to release the restriction?"

"If you kill those two as promised, I will immediately perform the great technique."

Startled by those words, Geom Mugeuk asked,

"You said the target changed to me? Then why are there two?"

Then a name that should never have been spoken emerged.

"We will kill you, and Jinha-gun as well. If one side loses a son, the other must lose a grandson for the sake of fairness, must they not?"

So Jeongrak spoke to the two demonic elders.

"Now, please finish him."

Baekgol Honma looked at Ma Yeomgun. At the glance suggesting they act together, Ma Yeomgun nodded. He could sense that Baekgol Honma, having peered into Geom Mugeuk's heart earlier, had grown more cautious. This was unlike him. What in the world had he seen?

Just as the two were about to step forward toward Geom Mugeuk,

"But have you two met the person who will release the restriction?"

At Geom Mugeuk's question, Ma Yeomgun and Baekgol Honma stopped.

Their gazes turned toward So Jeongrak.

"Is he not right there?"

Geom Mugeuk shook his head at Ma Yeomgun's question.

"That is not something he can accomplish alone."

Ma Yeomgun's expression hardened slightly.

"It is not?"

"How could he undo the Demon Restriction's seal alone? If it could be undone so easily, would the Demon Restriction have lasted all these years?"

Ma Yeomgun asked So Jeongrak.

"Is this true?"

So Jeongrak did not answer.

"That is a matter we will handle on our own."

So Jeongrak realized. Geom Mugeuk was trying to drag out yet another of his backers. Truly, this bastard... was insane!

Immediately, the expressions of Ma Yeomgun and Baekgol Honma hardened. Of their auras, one grew hotter, and the other, colder.

So Jeongrak was at a loss. He should not have taken it easy; he should have simply killed him from the start.

Every time Geom Mugeuk opened his mouth, an unexpected situation unfolded.

That mouth opened again.

“I don’t know for certain, but that Great Art must cost an enormous amount of money, too. Probably so much that the sum alone would make one consider other thoughts.”

How could the two old demons not know what those “other thoughts” were? It meant that killing them would be cheaper than performing the Great Art.

Demon Flame Lord flatly denied it.

“That cannot be.”

White Bone Soul Demon also nodded. At the very least, he seemed to trust the one who had made this promise.

“I don’t know who promised you, but that man there is the one who handles the practical work. Isn’t the heart of the person putting a blade to your bodies what matters?”

At that, So Jeongrak spoke with composure.

“It seems you’re trying to sow discord with petty words, but it won’t work.”

Then Geom Mugeuk said something unexpected.

“From now on, I’ll show you why that man cannot be trusted.”

No sooner had he finished speaking.

Shiiik.

Geom Mugeuk’s sword was drawn.

Chwaaaak.

“Ugh!”

The one who let out the short scream was So Jeongrak. The sword energy that flew from Geom Mugeuk’s blade split in all directions, severing the vine-like strands that had connected So Jeongrak to the sleeping patients.

It looked like a light, simple move, but it contained martial learning of the very highest level. That was precisely why it looked so easy and simple.

“Didn’t he lie just now and say I couldn’t use my inner force?”

Before So Jeongrak could make an excuse, Geom Mugeuk swiftly added,

“His intention was for you two seniors to charge at me while off guard and fall into danger.”

In that instant, Demon Flame Lord and White Bone Soul Demon’s expressions hardened. They had clearly heard him say that Geom Mugeuk could not use inner force.

“Wasn’t he planning to have us injure each other, then use poison at the end to kill us all?”

So Jeongrak merely listened to Geom Mugeuk in a daze. More than being misunderstood right now, he was shocked by the fact that the poison he had administered earlier had not worked.

‘Why hasn’t his bloodline burst and killed him?’

But to the two old demons, his appearance seemed like an admission of Geom Mugeuk’s words.

So Jeongrak asked Geom Mugeuk,

“What is going on?”

It was a question asking why he had not been poisoned.

“What else? Your plan to use me to make us injure each other failed.”

Geom Mugeuk began to win over the two old demons.

“As the saying goes, the arm bends inward. Let us join forces—my life will be saved, and your restrictions will be lifted. What do you say?”

So Jeongrak was flustered. He had never imagined Geom Mugeuk would try to side with the two demons.

‘What on earth is his intention?’

But in this urgent, rapidly shifting situation, he could not grasp Geom Mugeuk’s true intent.

“Do not fall for his wicked schemes.”

As he said that, So Jeongrak secretly poisoned Geom Mugeuk again.

White Snake Powder.

It was a poison that blinded its victim and made them unable to speak. First, he had to shut that hateful mouth.

But Geom Mugeuk opened his eyes even wider and raised his voice.

“Esteemed seniors, are you going to trust that liar and entrust your lives to him?”

Absolute Regression, Chapter 646

Chapter 646: Kill Him Without Listening!
----------------------------------------
So Jeongnak was dumbfounded.

He had truly never imagined that he would be driven into a corner as a liar at today's gathering.

But that wasn't what mattered right now.

'The poison isn't working?'

So Jeongnak was flustered. If it had been any other matter, he would never have panicked in this situation. How could he not know that showing agitation would only invite more suspicion?

But the fact that the poison wasn't working was a tremendous shock to him.

At the sight of So Jeongnak's flustered state, Mayeomgun and Baekgolhonma exchanged glances. The way So Jeongnak looked now seemed like the reaction of someone hiding something.

Suppressing his startled heart, So Jeongnak administered another poison.

This time, it was the Frenzy Poison.

This poison didn't directly harm the body.

It was a poison that made one lose control of their emotions, causing them to explode with rage and madness.

It was a poison that only masters of the highest level of poison arts could handle, completely different in nature from the White Snake Powder he had administered earlier.

'Lose it! Explode with all the rage you've built up toward them!'

The moment he did, it would be over. Those two prideful old demons would never let such an affront slide.

It didn't matter if they vented their anger on him. As always, the one who lost their cool first was the loser.

He had expected Geom Mugeuk to scream and explode with rage, but.

Geom Mugeuk instead smiled even more broadly and spoke to the two old demons.

"How fortunate that you two are such cautious and wise individuals."

He laughed? He laughed? So Jeongnak was even more shocked by Geom Mugeuk's reaction.

'The poison didn't work again!'

Geom Mugeuk was perfectly fine. He even pressed the attack again with that irksome face.

"Just look at how he can't say a word. It's clear he's aiming for mutual destruction. If I were you two, I would have already lost my temper, hurled every curse I know, and beaten that man senseless."

So Jeongnak forcibly held back the urge to scream. He was so furious that he wondered if the poison he had administered had actually poisoned himself.

But no. If the poison had affected him instead, he definitely would have been the one shouting.

'Why on earth isn't the poison working?'

Then a person flashed through So Jeongnak's mind.

'Could it be? Did the Poison King give him an antidote to take beforehand?'

It was possible to take an antidote in advance against a specific poison. Of course, one had to know exactly what poison the opponent would administer.

Could the Poison King have predicted that he would administer White Snake Powder and Frenzy Poison?

Even if he generously assumed that the Poison King had predicted his poisons and given an antidote in advance, there was no way to block two poisons of such completely different natures perfectly. Moreover, the antidote had to be taken in the exact correct sequence.

Ah, come to think of it, there weren't just two poisons. Hadn't there been the first poison he administered? Even the poison that made one's qi and blood expand and burst the moment they used internal energy hadn't worked.

'Is the Poison King on that level?'

So Jeongnak fell into deep thought. Should he administer his hidden trump card poison and kill him? Or should he let him die by these two's hands as originally planned?

'I have to kill him while I have the chance!'

If Geom Mugeuk tried to kill him, he would die helplessly. His life was on the line, so he had to kill him first and worry about the rest later.

In the end, So Jeongnak administered the hidden trump card poison he possessed. The ingredients were so precious that it was a poison he wouldn't use except in truly dire situations.

Lifeless True Poison.

It was a lethal poison that killed the target instantly—a poison he had crafted himself. Because death was instantaneous upon administration, there was no antidote.

'He can't block this.'

He should have rolled his eyes back and collapsed on the spot.

But this time too, Geom Mugeuk was perfectly fine. He even sowed discord again.

"Be honest. You never intended to perform the great ritual to break the taboo in the first place, did you?"

So Jeongnak's eyes went wide. He was shocked that even his own unique poison hadn't worked.

But his appearance made it look as though Geom Mugeuk's words had struck a nerve.

He needed to say something to escape suspicion, but he couldn't overcome the shock that the Lifeless True Poison hadn't worked.

'No matter how capable the Poison King is, he couldn't create an antidote to prepare for the Lifeless True Poison in advance. If such an antidote existed, it would make a person virtually invulnerable to all poisons. Such an antidote couldn't possibly exist.'

At that very moment, So Jeongnak flinched, his eyes growing wider and wider.

'Could it be?'

So Jeongnak's body trembled. It couldn't be.

'Yes, there's no way.'

So Jeongnak looked at Geom Mugeuk with trembling eyes.

Despite being hit with poison continuously, he was completely unharmed. He should have felt at least some discomfort or dizziness, but instead he was smiling at him.

In that relaxed demeanor, four characters came to So Jeongnak's mind.

Four characters that those who had mastered the poison arts hated to see even in their dreams.

Invulnerable to all poisons!

Even as this happened, Geom Mugeuk's sowing of discord continued.

"Look at that anxious expression. Do you really think he's not deceiving you two?"

Mayeomgun and Baekgolhonma silently stared at So Jeongnak. Indeed, ever since his claim of having no internal energy was exposed as a lie, he had been showing suspicious reactions.

So Jeongnak, who had been looking at Geom Mugeuk with a face full of disbelief, finally reached a conclusion.

"The Young Cult Lord is invulnerable to all poisons!"

In the heavy silence that flowed for a moment, Geom Mugeuk responded nonchalantly.

"Is that all the excuse you could come up with?"

Then Mayeomgun shouted irritably.

"Both of you, enough!"

Emotion seeped into Mayeomgun's grating voice, making it even more unpleasant to hear.

"What nonsense are you two spouting?"

His anger was directed at both of them.

So Jeongnak calmly explained the situation.

"I just administered poison to the Young Cult Lord continuously. Yet none of it worked. The reason I couldn't properly respond to the Young Cult Lord's instigation is because of that."

Then this time, Geom Mugeuk asked the two old demons.

"Have you ever seen someone invulnerable to all poisons in your life?"

Mayeomgun had never seen such a thing.

Baekgolhonma was one hundred fifty years old, so he might have seen it once.

But Baekgolhonma was the same. He had never even heard of anyone being called invulnerable to all poisons.

Geom Mugeuk mocked So Jeongnak.

"Then you shall see one today. Here, behold this person invulnerable to all poisons to your heart's content. Why don't you call a painter and have your portrait painted together?"

The two old demons didn't look at Geom Mugeuk; they looked at So Jeongnak with suspicious eyes. The meaning in those eyes was clear.

What was this absurd talk of invulnerability to all poisons?

So Jeongnak felt suffocated.

'Damn it! What proper judgment could these old specters who should have died several times over already possibly have?'

But he couldn't afford to let them slip away like this. Especially not in a situation where poison didn't work on Geom Mugeuk.

"Didn't I tell you? The Young Cult Lord is no ordinary person. Why else would I have suppressed his internal energy and called you two here together to deal with him?"

The two old demons were half-convinced, half-doubtful.

They had come to kill Geom Mugeuk in the first place. The very fact that they were wavering meant that Geom Mugeuk's scheme of sowing discord was working properly.

"Then we can just check."

Geom Mugeuk made an unexpected proposal.

"View the site of the great ritual and confirm the person who will assist with it. After confirming whether that man truly intends to perform the great ritual for you two, then kill me."

Then Mayeomgun asked Geom Mugeuk with suspicious eyes.

"Why would you go so far for us? We are the people trying to kill you."

Baekgolhonma also regarded Geom Mugeuk with similar suspicion.

"The reason is simple. Because that man is utterly contemptible. He looked down on our cult and schemed to cause mutual destruction between you two seniors and me. Though we were active in different eras, are we not all demonic path martial artists?"

So Jeongnak's face hardened. *We?* He could tell Geom Mugeuk was deliberately using such expressions.

Geom Mugeuk's words continued.

"Don't we demonic path martial artists have our own temperament? If we die fighting, so be it, but being used by others and dying is what I hate most! Especially by rats who hide in the shadows and hatch conspiracies!"

As he spoke, Geom Mugeuk looked at So Jeongnak.

Because they fully related to those words, the two old demons glanced at So Jeongnak.

So Jeongnak could tell. Every time Geom Mugeuk spoke, the old demons' trust in the Young Cult Lord grew.

'I can't fight with words. I have to come up with a scheme.'

Geom Mugeuk kept pressing him.

"What's so difficult about showing us the site of the great ritual in advance?"

Then So Jeongnak spoke to the old demons.

"Kill the Young Cult Lord. Then I will immediately escort you there. I will also call the person who will perform the great ritual together."

So Jeongnak glared at Geom Mugeuk.

'No matter what tricks you pull, you won't be able to move them.'

The bloody aura flowing from Mayeomgun's body and the deathly aura emanating from Baekgolhonma's eyes grew even more intense.

No matter what, these two were people filled with hatred toward the Heavenly Demon Cult.

Though So Jeongnak was acting suspiciously, their resolve to kill Geom Mugeuk remained unchanged.

Then Geom Mugeuk calmly raised a question.

"Isn't it strange?"

Thinking he was up to another trick, So Jeongnak shouted.

"Don't listen to him, just kill him!"

But what Geom Mugeuk said was something they couldn't help but hear next.

"What is strange?"

In response to Mayeomgun's question, Geom Mugeuk raised his doubt.

"If his words are true, he called you two here while suppressing even my internal energy. He could have just killed me himself. Why did he insist on having you two kill me?"

As So Jeongnak tried to interject, Baekgolhonma raised his hand to stop him.

At the gaze telling him to continue, Geom Mugeuk answered his own question.

"He intends to reveal to the world that you two are the ones who killed me. If that happens, you two will be hunted by our cult's experts for the rest of your lives. Because my father would never let it go."

Then Baekgolhonma, who had been listening, opened his mouth with a child's voice.

"Do you think we haven't prepared ourselves for that?"

Of course, they had been prepared to be hunted.

What Geom Mugeuk wanted to say started from here.

"But you won't be hunted. Because you won't be able to break the taboo."

A dark aura began to surge from Baekgolhonma's eyes and engulf the surroundings, but Geom Mugeuk's words continued.

"I'm sure you know their goal is for our cult and the Martial Alliance to fight until they bleed, resulting in mutual destruction. But if it is revealed that you two killed me, do you think my father would still wage war?"

Geom Mugeuk shook his head firmly.

"He would postpone the war for the sake of revenge. He is someone who would start a war for his son; there's no way he wouldn't seek revenge. Is that truly the situation they want?"

The eyes of Baekgolhonma and Mayeomgun wavered slightly. They could feel them wavering.

"And do you think you two would be caught easily?"

They had no intention of being caught easily. They had said they would kill even the Cult Leader, but they hadn't orchestrated all of this just to die.

Geom Mugeuk pressed them even more forcefully.

"Would they prefer that the war be postponed? Or would they prefer that you two, who killed me and the Demon Annihilation Vice Lord, die failing to break the taboo—all while saving the enormous cost of the great ritual?"

It was truly plausible, so the hearts of Mayeomgun and Baekgolhonma wavered.

Baekgolhonma spoke to So Jeongnak as if giving an order.

"Guide us to the site of the great ritual."

Baekgolhonma's gaze toward him was firm. So Jeongnak could not refuse those words. If even he found himself nodding at Geom Mugeuk's words, how could those two old demons not fall for it?

He had known Geom Mugeuk was no ordinary person, but experiencing it firsthand left him unable to keep his composure.

If he resisted clumsily for no reason, the situation would obviously only become more difficult.

But even if they went, he had to suppress Geom Mugeuk's internal energy.

Saying so outright would clearly offend the prideful old men and cause a backlash, so he spoke circuitously.

"The Young Cult Lord might launch a surprise attack."

Then Mayeomgun answered confidently.

"Don't worry about that. We'll handle it ourselves."

Mayeomgun spoke to Geom Mugeuk.

"You two walk side by side in front. We will follow behind."

He could refuse no longer.

He truly hated such variables, but there was no choice.

"Very well, let us go."

In the end, So Jeongnak took the lead and walked.

'Still, today's ending won't change.'

Geom Mugeuk followed right behind him, and the two old demons followed after that.

The patients, their guardians, and the physicians were still asleep. Geom Mugeuk didn't spare them a single glance, as if they were beneath his notice.

And so So Jeongnak led the three people out of the medical hall.

People they met inside the infirmary politely bowed their heads in greeting to So Jeongnak. He normally accepted such greetings graciously, but now he had no leisure of mind for that.

The place So Jeongnak guided them to was his own residence.

After manipulating hidden mechanisms concealed in the desk, bookshelf, and display cabinet in succession, a secret door leading underground opened.

Going down the stairs, there was a spacious area. Night-luminescent pearls were embedded everywhere, making the surroundings clearly visible, and thanks to air holes, it wasn't stifling.

This was precisely the place where So Jeongnak secretly cultivated his poison arts. The walls on all sides were fully stocked with all manner of poisonous herbs and poisons. Decades of his secrets were all there.

"The great ritual will be performed here."

Now that they had arrived here, the stiff expressions of Mayeomgun and Baekgolhonma eased.

Had he not expected this situation? Inwardly, So Jeongnak was curious about Geom Mugeuk's true intentions.

Why on earth had he insisted on seeing this place? Just to buy time? Was he stalling while waiting for someone to come rescue him?

'If so, you made a misjudgment.'

This underground space, sealed on all four sides by walls, was an impossible place to escape from. Moreover, once the secret entrance was closed, no one could come looking for them here.

So Jeongnak looked at Geom Mugeuk. He was looking around here and there like a person visiting a home.

"You seem to have a very meticulous personality, but your poisonous herb organization is quite lacking. Shall I organize them for you instead? I happen to have been rigorously trained in poisonous herb organization."

The moment he saw his relaxed demeanor, So Jeongnak felt a chill in his chest and realized one fact.

'Come to think of it, it's the same for us—there's nowhere to escape either.'

Absolute Regression, Chapter 647

Chapter 647: This Is for Our Poison King
----------------------------------------
Geom Mugik examined the interior and discovered one fact.

“It seems the secret chamber was moved here from another place not too long ago.”

How had he figured that out?

Just as Geom Mugik said, this was a newly constructed underground secret chamber. Originally, it had been beneath a secluded warehouse inside the medical hall, but a few years ago, So Jeongnak had built a new one beneath his own residence. Because using the underground space there had been too inconvenient.

At So Jeongnak’s questioning gaze, Geom Mugik gave a meaningful smile.

“Did I not say so earlier? At first, you must have been perfectly careful, but gradually, you grew complacent.”

So Jeongnak could not refute those words. Because he had indeed grown complacent.

Now, he was someone everyone respected. Even if this place were discovered, he could simply say it was where he researched and produced antidotes. That he had built an underground secret chamber because handling poison was dangerous.

But as Geom Mugik dug deeper, the situation changed.

If he pushed the narrative with his eloquent tongue that this place was where he refined his poison arts, he might truly become such a person in the eyes of others.

Geom Mugik was the one who had brought them here without drawing his sword once, without frowning once. The key that opened the door to this secret chamber was that three-inch tongue of his.

Soon, So Jeongnak composed his heart.

*Stay calm. He must be trembling with anxiety too.*

His gaze, which had been fixed on Geom Mugik, turned toward the two old demons. Now, the ones who had to make the gamble were the old demons.

No matter how dazzlingly Geom Mugik tried to deceive them with his eloquence, he couldn’t possibly be stronger than his poisons.

It was unlikely, but if the two old demons ultimately did not heed his words, he intended to have them killed by using a slow-acting poison under the pretext of an antidote.

“The Young Cult Leader is mistaken. I will release your seals right here.”

So Jeongnak walked over and spoke while looking at the large stone bed in the center.

“Look at this. Have I not even prepared a bed for the Grand Ritual?”

To anyone who saw it, it looked like a place where the Grand Ritual would be performed.

Ma Yeomgun and Baekgol Honma’s expressions had also relaxed considerably compared to before.

But Geom Mugik’s eyes were sharp.

“It seems this is a bed used for experimenting with poison arts.”

Ma Yeomgun furrowed his brow and asked Geom Mugik,

“What do you mean?”

“Look at the side over there.”

Red stains remained on the spot Geom Mugik pointed to. The two old demons immediately recognized them as bloodstains and hardened their expressions.

Geom Mugik ran his hand over the stone bed as he spoke.

“I wonder how many people died here?”

The two old demons stared at So Jeongnak with cold eyes.

Hadn’t So Jeongnak spoken just moments ago as if he had prepared this bed especially for them?

But performing a Grand Ritual on a spot where people had died from poison—how could anyone feel good about that?

So Jeongnak was inwardly flustered and annoyed, but he calmly acknowledged the fact with effort.

“That is correct. It also served as my research laboratory. One must perform the Grand Ritual in a familiar place to avoid mistakes.”

Though he didn’t deny it, Ma Yeomgun and Baekgol Honma’s expressions did not relax.

So Jeongnak quickly changed the subject.

“The Grand Ritual will proceed without any hindrance. The basic ingredients are already prepared, and the remaining ones will be brought on the day the ritual is performed.”

To his explanation, Geom Mugik looked at the medicinal herbs and said,

“Is that so?”

The two old demons’ gazes turned to Geom Mugik.

“There are only poisonous herbs here. Not a single ingredient for the Grand Ritual. You are deceiving them again, claiming they don’t know about herbs. You can tell just by looking. If the ingredients for the Grand Ritual were here, would you simply stand by when a fight is about to break out?”

So Jeongnak spoke calmly to the two old demons.

“They are basic ingredients that can be reacquired quickly. Please do not be swayed by that cunning tongue of his. He is doing everything he can to drive a wedge between us simply to survive.”

He had to kill Geom Mugik as soon as possible.

“Now that you have seen the site for the Grand Ritual, please kill the Young Cult Leader as promised. Whom to believe is for you two to judge.”

He was certain they would have no choice but to believe him in the end. No matter how much Geom Mugik tried to drive a wedge, the one who could eventually release their seals was on this side.

But what if they believed Geom Mugik, even by some slim chance?

If that happened, they would all die here together. Because they wouldn’t be able to withstand his poisons.

But those old demons wouldn’t just die quietly, so he wouldn’t be able to survive either.

In the end, only Geom Mugik would survive. So please, don’t think of turning on me, you old fools.

Geom Mugik spoke to the two old demons.

“Since you’ve come all this way, shall we do one final verification?”

“What verification?”

“You said you would show us the person who will perform the Grand Ritual together, did you not? Let’s see that person too.”

So Jeongnak couldn’t understand why Geom Mugik was so fixated on this place and that person.

“Do not fall for his tricks.”

Geom Mugik rebutted as if he had been waiting for this.

“If this were a trick, I wouldn’t have asked to see another person. That would mean one more enemy for me. Is it not?”

It wasn’t wrong, so Ma Yeomgun and Baekgol Honma nodded slightly.

So Jeongnak was bewildered.

Yes, he had a point. Why would he keep asking to see someone when it meant gaining one more powerful enemy?

At that moment, a chill ran down his spine.

So Jeongnak’s trembling gaze turned to Geom Mugik.

*You… could it be! Are you trying to gather everyone in one place and kill them all at once?*

He felt that might indeed be the case. How else could he explain the composure filling that face?

That naturally led to one question.

If he was so confident in his strength, why hadn’t he just killed them back in the medical hall? Why did he insist on coming here?

*There or here…*

At that very moment, So Jeongnak realized there was one difference between there and here.

Something that was there but not here.

The sleeping medical hall staff, patients, and their guardians.

*The reason he asked to come here was because of them?*

To avoid fighting there?

If a fight had broken out there, many of those who had been sleeping would have been injured or killed.

It would have been the same even if they fought outside the medical hall. Everywhere was packed with patients.

Only this place was somewhere they could fight without people getting hurt. Had he really thought that far ahead?

*Ah! Now that I think about it!*

Earlier, to prove he had internal energy, Geom Mugik had cut the line connected between himself and the patients.

Throughout their conversation in the medical hall, Geom Mugik had never once shown concern for the patients, which was why he hadn’t noticed. Even when leaving the place, he hadn’t given them a single glance.

*Was that all an attempt to hide his inner concern for them?*

That wasn’t what was surprising.

He already knew well that Geom Mugik was no ordinary Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult. He might normally value human life. He himself had lived such a life on the surface.

But what mattered was the current situation. When the situation was favorable, what mask couldn’t one wear?

But now was not a situation where one could put on a mask and enjoy a banquet.

In the midst of a life-or-death situation, he had worn a mask of indifference and still cared about those people’s survival?

So Jeongnak found it hard to believe. Even he, who had been treating them until this morning, hadn’t been able to pay attention to the sleeping patients.

“Did you ask to come here because of the patients?”

Instead of answering, Geom Mugik asked So Jeongnak,

“Aren’t those people the ones you are supposed to take care of?”

“What?”

“You are a doctor, and they are patients. So I thought you readily brought us here?”

So Jeongnak could tell. That Geom Mugik was rebuking his actions.

He had taken them hostage rather than protecting them. He had even threatened that if he died, they would die too, having connected himself to them.

Geom Mugik was prodding at the reverse scale of a man who had lived his whole life as a respected doctor.

“Stop your mockery there.”

Geom Mugik gazed at him quietly. Those deep eyes seemed to peer into the deepest recesses of So Jeongnak’s heart. Into that pitch-black hole.

“I believe Hwa Yulcheong is the same. Just as you satisfied the desires you suppressed by killing innocents in this underground chamber, he must have something too.”

Feeling a strange sense of shame, So Jeongnak urged the two old demons.

“Will you simply continue watching like that?”

Then the two old demons stepped forward.

They had already decided to kill Geom Mugik. That was a fact that had never changed from beginning to end.

Not because they believed So Jeongnak. But because they believed the one who had come to persuade them.

Ma Yeomgun looked at Baekgol Honma and spoke.

“I will handle this junior.”

Then Baekgol Honma shook his head.

“Let us do this together.”

“You want to join forces?”

Surprisingly, Baekgol Honma nodded. Ma Yeomgun knew that Baekgol Honma was an old man with as much pride as him—no, even more.

*But joining forces?*

Ma Yeomgun knew that ever since Baekgol Honma had peered at Geom Mugik with his Dark Heart Eye earlier, his words had grown fewer and his actions more cautious.

“What did you see in that Young Cult Leader?”

An even deeper darkness flowed from Baekgol Honma’s pupils as he looked at Geom Mugik.

The people Baekgol Honma had seen until now had shown various appearances.

Some had shown the appearance of savage beasts, and some the appearance of a crying child lost on the road. He had seen a well-honed sword, and he had seen darkness.

And what he saw in Geom Mugik was Geom Mugik.

Geom Mugik was looking down at him from above a pit. And he was standing in a pit of exactly the right size to fit a coffin, looking up at him.

Since he began using the Dark Heart Eye, this was the first time he had seen not only his opponent but himself together as well.

If there had been killing intent, hatred, or rage in Geom Mugik’s eyes, he wouldn’t have been this bothered.

But those eyes held no emotion whatsoever. They were as indifferent as looking at a stone by the roadside. That gaze felt unpleasant and concerning.

When he finished using the Dark Heart Eye, he felt as if he hadn’t been looking at Geom Mugik but at himself. The reason he had greeted him so docilely was precisely because of that intense discomfort.

Baekgol Honma didn’t tell Ma Yeomgun what he had seen, but how nervous he was came through clearly.

Because of that, Ma Yeomgun didn’t let his guard down either.

Watching that scene, So Jeongnak was inwardly pleased.

*Perfect.*

As long as those two didn’t let their guard down, no matter how excellent the Young Cult Leader’s martial arts were, they could kill him.

Just as the two old demons began to raise their ki in earnest, Geom Mugik raised his hand and spoke.

“Wait! Please wait a moment.”

So Jeongnak nearly burst out shouting.

*What now! Please stop talking and fight! Fight and die!*

This time, it wasn’t words but actions.

And actions that were utterly incomprehensible.

“One step, two steps, three steps…”

Geom Mugik measured the distance from one end of the wall to the other in paces.

“What are you doing?”

An answer came that made him wish he hadn’t asked.

“I’ve grown larger recently. It’s hard to gauge.”

So Jeongnak looked at the two old demons. His gaze was filled with the sentiment of *just get rid of him*, but they simply watched what Geom Mugik was doing. He had piqued their curiosity that much, and also because they didn’t fully trust So Jeongnak.

After measuring the width of the underground chamber, Geom Mugik made one more request.

“Ah, there is something else I must do. Something very important! Something that can settle our seniors’ retirement.”

How could anyone not be curious when he spoke like that?

Geom Mugik picked up a leather sack placed in the corner and began gathering poisonous herbs into it.

“You’ve collected quite a lot of Qihwa Grass even though it can only be obtained beyond the frontier. And there’s Yin Wind Flower root here too. This is truly precious.”

With an ominous feeling he couldn’t explain, So Jeongnak shouted. Thinking Geom Mugik was up to some trick.

“Kill him quickly!”

Then Geom Mugik scolded him instead.

“Then you are unfit to be a doctor and unfit to be a poisoner!”

“What?”

“You mean you’re fine with these precious poisonous herbs being ruined in the fight? Are poisonous herbs nothing but such trivial things to you? You abandon your patients, you abandon your herbs. What exactly are you?”

So Jeongnak hadn’t expected Geom Mugik to scold him like this, so he couldn’t retort. Though he couldn’t retort, it was absurd. Who was the reason he had gotten into this mess?

Geom Mugik gathered mainly the precious poisonous herbs stored separately inside a display cabinet.

“You just stay there. Even if I can’t make poison, I know well which herbs are precious. These are things money can’t buy.”

So the talk about settling retirement meant that after killing him, they should take these herbs and sell them.

It wasn’t just a trick. Geom Mugik was sincerely gathering the poisonous herbs.

He packed them tightly into the leather sack, trying to fit in even one more. They were truly such precious poisonous herbs that their value couldn’t even be calculated in money.

So Jeongnak watched with half resignation. As he said, since they could be damaged if caught up in the fight, having him organize them like this was actually a good thing for him.

After killing Geom Mugik, would the two old demons covet the poisonous herbs? It was unlikely, no, impossible. Would they harbor such greed when they needed their seals released?

“Seniors, please step aside for a moment.”

Geom Mugik gathered the herbs behind the two as well.

Though they tensed inwardly in case he might launch a surprise attack, Geom Mugik focused solely on gathering the herbs.

Watching that, So Jeongnak scoffed coldly.

“Stalling for time like this won’t bring anyone to save you. Though I thank you for packing my herbs.”

“Sorry, but I’m not giving them to you.”

“What?”

“I’m giving them to our Poison King.”

So Jeongnak made an absurd expression. In other words, it meant Geom Mugik would win this fight. At the same time, rage welled up. He would give these precious herbs to the Poison King of all people?

Geom Mugik tossed the leather sack filled with poisonous herbs to him.

“If you want to live, hug that and stick tight to the wall. Step forward, and you die.”

Then Geom Mugik turned toward Ma Yeomgun and Baekgol Honma and drew his Black Demon Sword.

“Now, thank you for waiting. Let us have a conversation in our cult’s proper manner.”

Absolute Regression, Episode 648

Chapter 648: You’ll Probably Live to a Hundred
----------------------------------------------
Silence settled over the place where all manner of words had been exchanged.

As the auras of Demon Flame Lord and the White-Bone Soul Demon changed, the very air around them changed as well. A congealed pressure containing a hundred years, a hundred and fifty years of time began to dominate the hall.

Now that they were revealing their true auras before a real fight, they were no longer the old specters who had been swayed by Geom Mugeuk’s words.

So Jeongrak, pressed against the wall behind Geom Mugeuk, felt fear toward them for the first time.

‘Even if they’re poisoned with the deadliest venom, they won’t simply die.’

He felt as though he were facing monsters who would go mad from the poison, turn everything around them into a sea of blood, and only then breathe their last. Yes, it was because they were such people that the organization had recruited them.

This time, So Jeongrak’s gaze turned to Geom Mugeuk’s back.

‘Can you really defeat them?’

Because he was pressed against the rear wall, he could not see Geom Mugeuk’s expression. Feeling fear, as he did, would have been the natural response, and yet the sight of Geom Mugeuk’s back, the Black Demon Sword hanging loosely at his side, still seemed utterly at ease.

The first to speak was Demon Flame Lord.

“Young Cult Leader. I know what you’re relying on.”

Blood-red killing intent shimmered from his body. The inner power spreading out from his dantian heated his entire body like lava.

“The Nine Flames Demonic Art. A truly terrifying martial art.”

He did not look down on the Nine Flames Demonic Art in the slightest.

“You know that I inherited that Nine Flames Demonic Art, don’t you?”

Of course the old demons knew. But they also held this belief.

“No matter how formidable the Nine Flames Demonic Art may be, with the accomplishments of a mere man in his twenties, you can never defeat us.”

Geom Mugeuk could tell.

That was precisely why they believed they could kill him.

Where did that certainty come from? Yes, from this, perhaps?

The power of time.

The greatest power they possessed.

Demon Flame Lord was a hundred years old, and the White-Bone Soul Demon had reached the age of a hundred and fifty.

It was only natural that someone in his twenties would look easy to them. To them, their twenties must have been a past so distant they could hardly even remember it.

Time was their greatest strength, and at the same time, their greatest weakness.

“When the two of you were young, I’m sure you went around killing aged masters just fine. Then again, you must have forgotten it all.”

At that, the White-Bone Soul Demon’s eyes turned pitch-black, and something began to flow out from his body.

“Do not worry. I have not forgotten how to kill people.”

Tssssss.

What flowed out was a black mist.

Like a swarm of snakes rushing in, the black mist began to surge toward the place where Geom Mugeuk stood.

It was the Death-Spirit Black Mist, one of his signature martial arts from the Soul-Returning Ghost Spirit Art.

Once exposed to the Death-Spirit Black Mist, one’s blood and qi would be blocked, and the mind would grow hazy. Even a person whose full power was ten would be reduced to five within that black mist.

It was a demonic art that would exert its greatest power in this underground secret chamber, where all four sides were tightly sealed.

Demon Flame Lord was certain of victory.

“I understand your arrogance, but to act so leisurely before us?”

On the other hand, the White-Bone Soul Demon was executing his martial art cautiously. Still lingering in his heart was the look in Geom Mugeuk’s eyes as he had stared at him indifferently. Like a brand that would never disappear until Geom Mugeuk became a corpse.

Tssssssss!

One might have wondered how a human could possibly avoid a mist surging toward him.

But in the next instant, the eyes of Demon Flame Lord and the White-Bone Soul Demon sharpened intensely.

The black mist could not pass over to where Geom Mugeuk stood.

Something like a transparent wall was blocking the space between them and Geom Mugeuk.

The mist wandered for a long while before Geom Mugeuk, then slowly rose upward. The mist that rose in that way tried to cross over along the ceiling, but that place, too, was blocked.

Tsssssssst.

Like a snake thrashing after its head had been cut off, the black mist writhed, but it could not find a way to pass over to Geom Mugeuk’s side.

Shwaaaaaak!

Demon Flame Lord, who had been watching, launched a palm strike.

Boom!

The black mist scattered from the force, then gathered again.

But even with his palm strike, the wall blocking that place did not vanish.

In the end, the Death-Spirit Black Mist returned to the White-Bone Soul Demon and was withdrawn into his body.

Demon Flame Lord carefully stepped forward.

He slowly approached and walked up to the point where the Death-Spirit Black Mist had failed to advance. Sure enough, there was a transparent wall there.

“The Nine Flames Demonic Art?”

“That is correct.”

The third technique of the Nine Flames Demonic Art, the Great Demon Wall.

That transparent wall was the Great Demon Wall.

It was astonishing that he had deployed the Great Demon Wall without his opponents noticing, but what was even more astonishing was that this Great Demon Wall was filling the entire front without the slightest gap.

Unless one had reached the ninth level, one could never unfold it with such precision.

Geom Mugeuk asked Demon Flame Lord, who stood beyond the Great Demon Wall.

“Have you ever seen the Nine Flames Demonic Art even once?”

“No.”

“I thought as much.”

Demon Flame Lord said coldly.

“How arrogant.”

“It’s faith. It is the martial art that has allowed our cult to endure until now while commanding rough people like you seniors.”

Even as he looked at the Great Demon Wall before his eyes, Demon Flame Lord’s momentum did not diminish at all. He raised his hand.

“Someday, I wanted to burn the Nine Flames Demonic Art away with this hand.”

Wooooong.

Demon Flame Lord’s hand burned red.

He brought that blazing hand to the Great Demon Wall.

Chiiiiik.

It was right in front of Geom Mugeuk’s face. He was showing his intent to burn through this wall and melt even your face.

Looking at that crimson palm beyond the Great Demon Wall, Geom Mugeuk said,

“Judging by your palm lines, Senior, your lifeline is very long. It seems you will live to a hundred.”

Demon Flame Lord snorted and drew up even more internal energy. His hand turned even redder.

Chiiiiik.

Smoke rose along with the searing heat.

How much time had passed?

Demon Flame Lord flinched and pulled his hand away from the Great Demon Wall.

He looked down at his own hand. His palm was torn open, and blood was flowing down.

The wall made of astral energy had not been pierced; instead, it had wounded his own palm.

At the sight of blood, excitement flashed in Demon Flame Lord’s eyes.

He immediately thrust out both palms. If he could not melt it, he would smash it apart.

Boom! Boooom!

A powerful impact was transmitted, but the Great Demon Wall did not budge. His blood smeared the transparent Great Demon Wall.

But he did not stop attacking.

Kwaaang! Kwaang!

His twin palms continued to strike the space in front of Geom Mugeuk’s face. They were attacks imbued with the will to kill him at all costs.

From the outside, it looked as though he had become excited and was attacking wildly, but he had a plan.

To endure such powerful impacts and maintain the Great Demon Wall, an enormous amount of internal energy would be required.

‘You dare challenge me in a battle of internal energy?’

Demon Flame Lord drew up still more inner power and struck the Great Demon Wall.

‘This is youthful hot blood and arrogance.’

It might look impressive in this moment, but if he consumed his internal energy like this, he would not last long before all of it was exhausted. Moreover, the wall was blocking the entire front, was it not? It was no small size.

“I shall watch in your father’s stead as the martial world is dyed in blood!”

Demon Flame Lord provoked Geom Mugeuk. His intent was to prick his pride and make him continue the contest of internal energy to the end.

When Geom Mugeuk made a show of yawning beyond the wall, Demon Flame Lord inwardly cried out in delight.

‘You’ve taken the bait!’

A faint smile also formed at the lips of the White-Bone Soul Demon, who was watching.

He believed that Geom Mugeuk’s attempt to block the attacks with the Great Demon Wall had clearly been the wrong choice. He should have chosen a chaotic melee from the very beginning. In the midst of that, an opportunity might have appeared.

‘It seems I won’t need to step in.’

Kwaang! Boom! Boom!

Clutching the leather pouch of medicinal herbs to his chest, So Jeongrak cheered Demon Flame Lord on.

‘Break it! Please, smash it to pieces!’

Boom! Kwaaang!

Now, the only sound in that place was the sound of twin palms striking the Great Demon Wall.

‘He’s still holding out?’

As time passed, Demon Flame Lord’s heart filled with disbelief and astonishment. Beyond the wall, Geom Mugeuk looked far too calm.

‘How can this be?’

At that moment, the White-Bone Soul Demon’s voice came from behind him.

“Come back now.”

Boom! Kwaang! Kwaaaang!

Only after several more irritable attacks did Demon Flame Lord stop.

Demon Flame Lord’s hands, which had been burning red, returned to their original color.

Geom Mugeuk was looking at him with indifferent eyes. If he had mocked him or looked down on him, it would have felt better than this.

But those indifferent eyes made him feel one thing.

He had been pounding on the wall the entire time.

In this moment, Demon Flame Lord felt a true wall.

The White-Bone Soul Demon said in a low voice,

“The Young Cult Leader is stronger than we anticipated.”

He immediately unleashed the strongest move he possessed.

Krrrk. Krrk.

Following the mist that flowed across the floor, the ground trembled, and something began to rise from it.

Astonishingly, they were white bones. They were not mere bones. They were the essence of the Soul-Returning Ghost Spirit Art, the Death-Spirit Return, with black energy flowing from both eyes.

It was an illusion, but the skeletons truly existed before their eyes.

The demonic energy emanating from the skeletons lined up before him was so dreadful that even Demon Flame Lord, who stood beside him, took another step back.

Even summoning a single one was enough to slaughter most experts, yet now he had summoned every one he could call forth.

“They are White-Bone Ghosts. Impervious to blades and swords, and if you inhale so much as a handful of the demonic energy they exhale, the blood vessels throughout your body will twist.”

What was even more terrifying was that even if these things were somehow smashed apart, their bones would rejoin and they would revive. Unless the White-Bone Soul Demon died or his internal energy was exhausted, this hell would not end.

The White-Bone Soul Demon had truly unleashed his most powerful means at once.

But Geom Mugeuk was not frightened at all. Instead, he said to them calmly,

“I understand you. Blaming only the Forbidden Demon Order without thinking of the sins you committed yourselves—I understand that. That’s the kind of people you are.”

So Jeongrak, who was watching from behind, inwardly cheered.

‘You’re dead now!’

He thought that Geom Mugeuk, having judged himself at a disadvantage, had begun wagging his tongue again to survive.

But it would not work now. Those terrifying White-Bone Ghosts were not things that could be killed with one’s tongue.

Geom Mugeuk continued speaking.

“If only you can break the restrictions, you don’t care if a Great Demon-Orthodox War breaks out—I understand that too. That’s the kind of people you are. You might get angry and try to kill me. I understand the desire to defeat the Nine Flames Demonic Art as well. Seniors, I understand everything.”

After speaking as though he understood everything, as though he were the most broad-minded person in the world, Geom Mugeuk’s aura began to change.

“So please understand me for doing this.”

As soon as he finished speaking, the Great Demon Wall that had stood before him vanished.

At the same time, something else appeared there.

Ssssssssssst!

The things lined up in a row before Geom Mugeuk.

Larger and more terrifying than before, they were none other than the evil spirits of the Nine Flames Demonic Art.

From wall to wall, evil spirits filled the space without the slightest gap.

The second technique of the Nine Flames Demonic Art, the Great Annihilation Form, had been unleashed.

Everyone there was shocked by the sight of the evil spirits. This was not the Nine Flames Demonic Art they had imagined. They had not known such terrifying evil spirits would manifest.

So Jeongrak, who was watching from behind, also widened his eyes. At first, it had suddenly grown dark, and a massive wall had appeared.

But it was not a wall. One of the things lined up there slowly turned to look back at him.

The moment he saw that evil spirit, So Jeongrak’s entire body froze. It was truly the most terrifying face he had ever seen in his life.

It was incomparably more terrifying than the skeletons that had risen earlier. Those frightening White-Bone Ghosts looked shabby by comparison.

Geom Mugeuk’s stern voice rang thunderously through the place.

“Demon Flame Lord and White-Bone Soul Demon. Not only did you violate the Forbidden Demon Order, the solemn rule of this cult, but you also plotted a Great Demon-Orthodox War. For those crimes, I hereby execute you on the spot.”

The moment the sentence of death was passed down!

The evil spirits showed no mercy.

Kwakwakwakwakwakwakwakwakwa.

The evil spirits that filled the underground secret chamber swept everything away as they advanced.

The White-Bone Ghosts blocking their path thrust out their swords.

Kwa-deuk! Kwa-deudeuk! Kwa-deudeudeudeuk!

The White-Bone Ghosts, which could withstand not only sword qi but even sword aura, shattered into pieces and scattered in every direction.

Before they could even join back together, the evil spirits were already rushing toward the White-Bone Soul Demon.

The White-Bone Soul Demon raised his protective astral energy to its limit.

‘I’ll withstand it!’

But when he saw the terrifying evil spirits surging toward him, his body froze. He was an old wolf trembling before the roar of a tiger.

Papapapapak!

The instant his protective qi barrier shattered, the impact surged through his entire body. In that final moment, he recalled the scene he had seen within the Dark Mind’s Eye.

The sight of himself looking up at Geom Mugeuk from inside a pit. As expected, what he had seen then had not been Geom Mugeuk, but his own fate.

Puh-puh-puh-puk!

His body broke apart and scattered in pieces. The white-bone ghosts that had been trying to rise again collapsed as they were, turning to smoke and vanishing.

Mayeomgun, too, was putting forth everything he had.

The Flame Palm into which he had poured all his internal energy contained a heat as scorching as lava.

Kwaaaaaang!

The very instant the charging evil spirit collided with his twin palms!

The blazing palm seal, which until now had pierced through and burned away the bodies of every opponent, could not burrow into the evil spirit’s body. As if heated iron had been plunged into cold water, his body cooled in an instant.

The last moment he recalled was a time long ago, when he had heard someone say that no demonic art could ever defeat the Nine Flames Demonic Art.

In the hot blood of youth, he had inwardly denied it then.

What is so special about the Nine Flames Demonic Art? One day, I’ll tear it apart with these hands!

Puh-puh-puh-puk!

Mayeomgun’s entire body shattered and burst apart.

Kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa!

Only after the evil spirits had pushed all the way to the end of the secret chamber did they disappear.

When the dust settled, nothing remained there. Not a corpse, not even a single poisonous herb root.

Only the blood pooled thickly across the floor testified to the fact that Baekgol Honma and Mayeomgun had once been there.

In that empty space, only Geom Mugeuk stood with his back turned.

‘It’s grown even stronger!’

The Nine Flames Demonic Art had become incomparably stronger than when it was at the eighth stage. As the ninth-stage Nine Flames Demonic Art was unleashed in actual combat, Geom Mugeuk was swept by an intense desire to bring it to full mastery.

Watching his back, So Jeongrak’s legs gave out, and he sank to the floor.

Thud.

He had truly never imagined that Mayeomgun and Baekgol Honma would be swept away in a single move, vanishing without a trace.

‘They were no match for even one strike?’

His body trembled violently.

‘…So this is the martial art of the Heavenly Demon!’

Only now did he understand why, when Geom Mugeuk had first entered, he had measured the width of this space with his steps. He had been measuring how many of those evil spirits he would need to summon.

‘He had already made up his mind to kill them like this from that moment!’

Geom Mugeuk walked back and sat down beside the terrified So Jeongrak.

So Jeongrak looked at Geom Mugeuk with trembling eyes. Even after smashing the two old demons to pieces, his gaze had not wavered in the slightest. Those cold, merciless eyes felt terribly unfamiliar.

He could not tell whether this man was truly the same Young Cult Leader who had been so talkative earlier. It felt as though he were dreaming.

Now, So Jeongrak’s final hope was this.

That Geom Mugeuk was not immune to all poisons, but had simply taken an antidote the Poison King had given him in advance. That, by sheer luck, it had worked several times in a row.

So Jeongrak tried to administer a deadly poison one last time. This was the only method left. Please, let the Poison King not have anticipated even this!

At that very moment, Geom Mugeuk pulled out a poisonous herb that was sticking out of the leather pouch So Jeongrak held and began chewing it as if he were eating bellflower root.

So Jeongrak abandoned the thought of poisoning him and squeezed his eyes shut.

Because the poisonous herb Geom Mugeuk was now eating so deliciously was the Heavenly Yin Ten-Thousand Poisons Herb, said to kill instantly with just a touch of the tongue.

He wished all of this were a dream, but Geom Mugeuk’s vivid voice reached his ears.

“So Jeongrak, let’s have a little talk.”

Absolute Regression, Chapter 649

Chapter 649: Not Easy to Let Live
---------------------------------
Talk?

Compared to *"How should I kill you?"* it was certainly a better thing to say, but Sojeongrak was not the least bit pleased.

He could talk with anyone in the world, but he did not want to talk with Geom Muguk, who was beside him right now. Because the more he talked with him, the more cornered and disadvantaged he felt.

“Is there more for us to talk about?”

His attitude toward Geom Muguk became respectful. No, he had no choice but to become respectful. After witnessing that fight just now, who could dare act according to their temper?

“It is only natural that what we have to say changes when the situation changes.”

At that, Sojeongrak spoke as if testing the waters.

“Since you’re going to kill me anyway, just kill me.”

Of course, he didn’t mean it. Sojeongrak was determined to escape this situation somehow.

But Geom Muguk’s reaction was not what he had wanted.

“You are a person of honor as well. I suppose I can grant that much of a request.”

While sitting, Geom Muguk drew the Black Demon Sword. Right when he was about to pierce Sojeongrak’s throat without hesitation—

“Wait!”

At Sojeongrak’s urgent cry, Geom Muguk’s blade stopped before his throat.

Geom Muguk laid the sword sideways and tapped his head. With an expression that said, *Don’t get cocky.*

Who was he trying to fool? If he truly were someone unafraid of death, when the two old demons died earlier, he would have attacked while administering all the remaining poison.

For someone unafraid of death, he was pressed far too close to the wall.

Sojeongrak glared at Geom Muguk with resentful eyes.

“Even in this situation, you toy with me.”

Then Geom Muguk called his name in a low voice.

“Sojeongrak.”

He had merely called his name, yet Sojeongrak felt his spirit crushed and his body shrink.

“Do you want to experience what it truly means to be toyed with?”

Sojeongrak dared not say a word.

When poison didn’t work, coaxing and placating, inciting and threatening. He had to survive by any means necessary.

Sojeongrak glanced at Geom Muguk. Would such things work on this Geom Muguk?

Had he ever felt so powerless in his life?

Then, Geom Muguk’s calm voice was heard from beside him.

“I’m not interested in your circumstances or what kind of life you’ve lived. You tried to kill me, and you tried to kill my friend. That alone is reason enough to die.”

Then Sojeongrak blurted out.

“But they didn’t die, did they?”

Geom Muguk smiled at the far-fetched retort.

“Yes, they didn’t die.”

Sojeongrak pinned his hopes on this reaction.

Geom Muguk had a weakness. He was not ruthless enough. He had to exploit that weak heart. And so he would definitely walk out of this secret room alive.

He had something he absolutely had to do once he got out.

“What about the people you killed while conducting poison experiments here? What about trying to incite the Demonic War and cause countless deaths?”

He even had excuses for those crimes.

“I’ve saved countless patients my whole life.”

“To hide your true identity.”

“Nevertheless, didn’t the patients live? You didn’t die, the patients lived, and the war hasn’t happened yet. Can’t that atone for my sins?”

Before he could be pressed about those who died in the poison experiments, Sojeongrak quickly continued.

“I thought I would be calm and dignified at the final moment. I never thought I’d be saying such things.”

Sojeongrak added with a sigh.

“I don’t want to die.”

Then Geom Muguk answered honestly as well.

“I’m sorry, but it’s not easy to let you live.”

Sojeongrak saw hope even in this answer. If he were truly ruthless, he wouldn’t even say such things. He would have cut off his arms and gouged out his eyes to make him talk.

“I’m rather grateful that you’re being honest. It’s better than saying you’ll spare me and killing me at the last moment.”

Sojeongrak silently stared at the emptied space. The bed, the desk, the shelves, the poisonous herbs, and the two experts who would have protected him were all gone from that place.

His gaze was as miserable as his current situation, but his mind was still filled with thoughts of leaving this place.

“Young Cult Leader, are you truly immune to all poisons?”

“Are you that curious?”

In truth, the moment he had chewed the Cheoneum Maneokcho, there had been no need to ask further, but he still wanted to hear it from Geom Muguk’s own mouth. That was how unbelievable it was.

“Shall I eat something else?”

Sojeongrak spoke with a sigh.

“Heaven is truly heartless.”

That an immunity to all poisons, which one might not see even once in a lifetime, would become entangled with himself, a user of poison arts.

“To you, I must be less threatening than a gatekeeper martial artist of the Murim Alliance.”

And it wasn’t even a simple immunity to all poisons. It was a heaven-defying, prodigy-level invulnerability that possessed the power to kill Mayeomgun and Baekgolhonma in a single move.

“If you kill me, there will be a big problem. Many people saw you come to the clinic today. If I die, it will eventually be revealed that you killed me. Then the righteous faction martial artists of Wuhan won’t let it go.”

The physician Sojeongrak.

This was what he had considered his greatest hope for survival.

Geom Muguk’s cold-hearted reply came back.

“I don’t think anything will happen even if you die.”

It was truly a remark that pierced straight through a man’s pride.

At least in this moment, strength entered Sojeongrak’s eyes.

“You said it yourself, didn’t you? That I’ve failed to save far more people than I’ve saved. But that’s something you say without knowing how great a life debt is to martial artists. Many people will step forward for me.”

As if acknowledging that, Geom Muguk nodded. But the reason lay elsewhere.

“It’s not because I look down on you. It’s because of Cheonaegeosa.”

Because of Cheonaegeosa? Sojeongrak looked at Geom Muguk with an expression asking what that meant.

Then Geom Muguk asked an unexpected question.

“How many people have you killed for Cheonaegeosa until now?”

He was indeed the Young Cult Leader, a master of striking at the vitals. Sojeongrak hadn’t expected him to suddenly ask such a thing.

If he hadn’t killed a single person, he would have answered easily. But he had killed many. The years he had protected him were not merely one or two.

Momentarily flustered, Sojeongrak couldn’t give any answer.

He should have immediately said that no such thing had happened.

Had he been overwhelmed by Geom Muguk’s momentum? His head wasn’t turning quickly enough.

“You must have killed a lot.”

At Geom Muguk’s words, a thought flashed through Sojeongrak’s mind, who had been about to make excuses, albeit belatedly.

*No, perhaps this might be better?*

Because Geom Muguk would think he was needed to uncover Cheonaegeosa’s true identity.

“Give me a reason why it’s acceptable to kill me because of Cheonaegeosa!”

Geom Muguk revealed an unexpected reason.

“Because your death will be buried beneath the matter of Cheonaegeosa.”

“!”

“Soon, the matter of Cheonaegeosa will also be concluded. When it is revealed that he is a two-faced villain, the murim will be covered in his stories. If that side is properly uncovered, you become an afterthought. Ah, the one who helped Cheonaegeosa was Physician So? No one will be angry about your affairs. Since Cheonaegeosa committed such shocking deeds, it won’t be strange at all if you helped him.”

Rage erupted in Sojeongrak’s heart. Because his entire life was being denied.

But he knew well that getting angry now would not help in the slightest in this situation.

“Perhaps that might be the case.”

Feeling stifled, Sojeongrak sprang up from his seat and paced around the secret room. Then, leaning against the wall far away, he asked.

“But can you really uncover Cheonaegeosa’s other face? He is a different kind of person from me.”

He meant it sincerely as well. Was it because he had trained in poison arts? Or because of his nature? No matter how kindly he lived as a physician, he couldn’t hide his occasional bursts of temper.

He wondered if that was perhaps the reason everything had been exposed to this Geom Muguk.

But Cheonaegeosa was different from him. He sometimes had such thoughts.

Is this person really on the same side?

Because he had lived Cheonaegeosa’s life so thoroughly.

Geom Muguk answered as if he weren’t worried in the slightest.

“Everyone has their own black hole in their heart. If he has hidden it that well, Cheonaegeosa’s hole is probably bigger than yours.”

If he couldn’t live as the physician Sojeongrak?

This question would now be the only thing that could save him.

“What is it you want to ask me?”

For the first time, the expected question came from him.

“Where is the person who will carry out the grand scheme with you now?”

Yes, he had expected Geom Muguk to be curious about that. Even after coming here, he had tried somehow to get him to call him over.

For now, that man was the lifeline that would save his life.

Geom Muguk already knew a fact he hadn’t been told.

“The one who persuaded Mayeomgun and Baekgolhonma was him, wasn’t it?”

Sojeongrak’s eyes widened in surprise.

“You really…!”

He had wondered why Geom Muguk hadn’t asked about the person who had persuaded Mayeomgun and Baekgolhonma before killing them, but it seemed he had already known it was the man who would carry out the grand scheme with him.

“How did you know?”

“Because I don’t think those two suspicious people were persuaded by words.”

Even after coming here, Mayeomgun and Baekgolhonma had not fully trusted Sojeongrak. That was why they had wavered at Geom Muguk’s words. They were not people who trusted others by nature. If they had been made to break the Geummaryeong…

“Since they were individuals of profound inner power, he probably couldn’t perfectly control them with Soul Subjugation Art, but he must have been able to exert enough Soul Subjugation Art to make them participate in this matter.”

He must be a master of the Soul Subjugation Art. Those who had committed suicide with the forged dark plaque earlier were likely also people he had placed under the Soul Subjugation Art.

Sojeongrak looked at Geom Muguk wordlessly. The admiration contained in that expression already took the place of an answer.

“You can never find that person.”

“What do you mean?”

Sojeongrak uttered something incomprehensible.

“Because he is always someone you are meeting for the first time.”

Geom Muguk looked at Sojeongrak with an expression asking what he meant.

“Each time I met him, he was a different person.”

“You mean he is good at disguises?”

A shocking statement flowed from the lips of Sojeongrak, who shook his head.

“He was the same person, but whenever I recalled him, the memory was different each time. Sometimes he was a fat person, sometimes he was a scrawny person. There were even days when I wondered if he had been a woman.”

Geom Muguk could understand. That he distorted the opponent’s memories with the Soul Subjugation Art. He was no ordinary foe.

This was precisely the reason Sojeongrak had revealed this information so willingly.

“He can only be contacted through me.”

Since Geom Muguk would surely try to capture him, he would not kill him.

“You were going to break the two old demons’ restrictions together with him. Though you didn’t trust us.”

Geom Muguk stared at him and said.

“Did he really only break their restrictions?”

Sojeongrak flinched in surprise.

“When he broke their restrictions, he probably also planted Gu poison to make them obey.”

There was no way he would expend such vast sums to break their restrictions only to use them this much. He must have used them as subordinates until they died.

Sojeongrak shook his head. How did this Young Cult Leader not know anything? To make such a man an enemy?

Geom Muguk slung the poison herb Hyeoknang that had been lying on the floor onto his back.

Sojeongrak tensed. It was the moment of whether he would kill him or not.

“You have to keep me alive to meet him. Am I not someone who poses no threat to you?”

Geom Muguk, who had contemplated for a moment, made him a proposal.

“If you let me meet him, I’ll spare you.”

“I’ll let you meet him.”

He added one more condition.

“Can you live as a physician for the rest of your life?”

“I can.”

“You must not use poison arts at all?”

Sojeongrak nodded.

“The life of a physician wasn’t bad either.”

Geom Muguk’s gaze softened.

“Physician, your patients must be waiting, so hurry and go out.”

Sojeongrak was truly happy. Not merely because he had saved his life.

*Now the second plan will proceed.*

He had prepared a second plan for when the matter with Mayeomgun and Baekgolhonma went awry.

It was a plan with himself at the center.

A mass-murder poison would be scattered in Wuhan and thousands would die. And the blame would fall on the Poison King who had come here.

*You damned Demonic Cult bastards! You will pay the price for looking down on me!*

Then he would gladly die with a smile.

Sojeongrak smiled brightly, and Geom Muguk smiled back at him.

As they walked side by side toward the passage, Geom Muguk took something out of his bosom as if he had just remembered.

“Ah, this.”

Geom Muguk took out a small pouch from his bosom.

*Pwoof!*

When he opened the pouch, smoke billowed up at the two of them.

“It’s a gift from the Poison King.”

The moment he heard those words, Sojeongrak was horrified.

He had no time to say anything. The poison was already rapidly burrowing into his veins.
